// QWENgine source snapshot (October 2026): the fixed context of the long-context benchmark (q38_eval longctx).

// ===== src/server/generator.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file generator.h
/// Text generation on top of Engine + Tokenizer: prompt reuse (strict continuation of the cached conversation, or
/// the engine snapshot taken at the end of the previous request's conversation), MTP speculative decoding with an
/// adaptive draft length, greedy (exact) or sampled output (temperature, top-k and top-p over the engine's top-32 logits), stop
/// handling and UTF-8-safe streaming deltas. Prompts may hold images (GenImage): they are encoded by the vision model
/// when their tokens must be processed, and prompt reuse compares them by content hash.
///
/// Sampling with drafts uses speculative sampling with deterministic (argmax) drafts: draft d is accepted with
/// probability p(d) under the target distribution; on rejection the token is drawn from p with d removed. This is
/// exact for the target distribution (temperature + top-k + top-p over the engine's top-32 logits).

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <random>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "runtime/engine.h"
#include "runtime/tokenizer.h"
#include "runtime/vision.h"

namespace q38rt {

struct GenParams {
    int max_tokens = 512;
    float temperature = 0.0f;     // 0 = greedy
    float top_p = 1.0f;
    int top_k = 0;                // 0 = all of the engine's top-32 candidates
    uint64_t seed = 0;            // 0 = nondeterministic
    std::vector<std::string> stop;
    bool speculative = true;      // use MTP drafts when the engine has the MTP block
};

/// An image in a prompt: tokens [offset, offset + patches->n_tokens()) are its placeholders (<|image_pad|>).
struct GenImage {
    int offset = 0;
    uint64_t hash = 0;                              // identity for prompt reuse: equal hashes = the same image
    std::shared_ptr<const ImagePatches> patches;    // VisionModel::preprocess
    int n_tokens() const { return patches->n_tokens(); }
};

struct GenResult {
    std::string text;
    std::string finish_reason = "length";   // "stop" (EOS or stop string) | "length" | "abort" | "error"
    int prompt_tokens = 0, cached_tokens = 0, completion_tokens = 0;
    double prefill_s = 0, decode_s = 0;     // prompt processing and generation time, without KV-cache growth
    double resize_s = 0;                    // time spent growing the KV cache
    int cycles = 0, drafted = 0, accepted = 0;   // speculative verify passes, drafts, accepted drafts
    int passes = 0;                         // engine forward passes (prefill chunks, decode and verify passes)
    int n_past = 0;                         // tokens in the engine's KV cache
    int kv_capacity = 0;                    // the engine's KV capacity (grows on demand)
    bool restored = false;   // the prompt continued from the snapshot of the previous request
    int images = 0;          // images encoded for this prompt (others were cached)
    double encode_s = 0;     // ... and the time spent encoding them (part of prefill_s)
    std::string error;       // finish_reason "error": what failed
};

/// Progress of a generation, reported to an observer on the generating thread with the GenResult so far (for logs
/// and metrics; prefill_s and decode_s are kept current at each event).
enum class GenEvent {
    PromptStart,   // prompt reuse decided: prompt_tokens, cached_tokens, restored, n_past
    Prompt,        // a prefill chunk or decode batch of the prompt is done: n_past
    PromptDone,    // the whole prompt is in the KV cache
    Decode,        // a decode or verify pass is done: completion_tokens, drafted, accepted
    Resized,       // the KV cache grew: kv_capacity, resize_s
};
using GenObserver = std::function<void(GenEvent, const GenResult&)>;

class Generator {
public:
    /// ctx_limit: largest context the engine may grow to (Engine::resize doubles its capacity on demand).
    Generator(Engine& eng, const Tokenizer& tk, int ctx_limit = 0)
        : eng_(eng), tk_(tk), ctx_limit_(ctx_limit > 0 ? ctx_limit : eng.max_context()) {}

    /// Generate a continuation of `prompt`. on_delta(text) receives newly completed text (whole UTF-8 sequences,
    /// stop strings withheld until resolved); returning false aborts generation. `keep`: number of leading prompt
    /// tokens the next request is expected to start with (the conversation, without the assistant's generation
    /// prompt); the engine state there is snapshotted for reuse. 0 = the whole prompt. `observe` receives progress
    /// events. `images`: the prompt's images in order (requires set_vision).
    GenResult generate(const std::vector<int32_t>& prompt, const GenParams& p,
                       const std::function<bool(const std::string&)>& on_delta, size_t keep = 0,
                       const GenObserver& observe = {}, const std::vector<GenImage>& images = {});

    /// The vision encoder for prompts with images.
    void set_vision(const VisionModel* vm) { vision_ = vm; }

    /// Forget the cached conversation (next request re-processes its whole prompt).
    void reset() { history_.clear(); history_images_.clear(); eng_.reset(); }

private:
    Engine& eng_;
    const Tokenizer& tk_;
    std::vector<int32_t> history_;   // tokens committed in the engine (positions 0 .. position()-1)
    struct HistImage { int offset, n; uint64_t hash; };
    std::vector<HistImage> history_images_;   // images among them, in order
    const VisionModel* vision_ = nullptr;
    std::mt19937_64 rng_{std::random_device{}()};
    DraftController dc_;             // draft-length statistics, kept across requests (they decay within ~12 cycles)
    int ctx_limit_;
    bool ensure_capacity(int positions);   // grow the engine (doubling) up to ctx_limit_; false if impossible

    bool is_eos(int32_t t) const;
    // target distribution over a result's candidates (temperature, top-k, top-p); returns (ids, probs)
    void distribution(const DecodeResult& r, const GenParams& p, std::vector<int32_t>& ids, std::vector<double>& pr) const;
    int32_t sample(const DecodeResult& r, const GenParams& p, int32_t exclude = -1) { return sample(r, p, exclude, rng_); }
    int32_t sample(const DecodeResult& r, const GenParams& p, int32_t exclude, std::mt19937_64& rng) const;
    // acceptance of a verify pass over batch = [next, d_1..d_k]: number of accepted drafts and the token after them
    void accept(const DecodeResult* out, const int32_t* batch, int k, const GenParams& p, std::mt19937_64& rng,
                int& accepted, int32_t& after) const;
};

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/generator.cpp =====

/// @file generator.cpp
/// See generator.h.

#include "server/generator.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

namespace q38rt {

namespace {
double now_s() { return std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count(); }

// Length of the longest prefix of s made of complete UTF-8 sequences.
size_t utf8_complete(const std::string& s) {
    size_t i = 0, ok = 0;
    while (i < s.size()) {
        const unsigned char c = (unsigned char)s[i];
        const size_t n = c < 0x80 ? 1 : (c >> 5) == 0x6 ? 2 : (c >> 4) == 0xE ? 3 : (c >> 3) == 0x1E ? 4 : 1;
        if (i + n > s.size()) break;
        i += n;
        ok = i;
    }
    return ok;
}
} // namespace

bool Generator::ensure_capacity(int positions) {
    if (positions <= eng_.max_context()) return true;
    if (positions > ctx_limit_) return false;
    int cap = eng_.max_context();
    while (cap < positions) cap *= 2;
    return eng_.resize(std::min(cap, ctx_limit_));
}

bool Generator::is_eos(int32_t t) const {
    return t == tk_.eos() || t == 248046 /* <|im_end|> */ || t == 248044 /* <|endoftext|> */;
}

void Generator::distribution(const DecodeResult& r, const GenParams& gp, std::vector<int32_t>& ids,
                             std::vector<double>& pr) const {
    ids.clear();
    pr.clear();
    size_t n = r.topk_ids.size();
    if (gp.top_k > 0) n = std::min(n, (size_t)gp.top_k);
    if (n == 0) return;
    const double l0 = r.topk_vals[0];
    const double temp = gp.temperature, top_p = gp.top_p;
    double sum = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const double p = std::exp(((double)r.topk_vals[i] - l0) / temp);
        ids.push_back(r.topk_ids[i]);
        pr.push_back(p);
        sum += p;
    }
    // top-p over the (descending) candidates
    double cum = 0;
    size_t keep = n;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        cum += pr[i] / sum;
        if (cum >= top_p) { keep = i + 1; break; }
    }
    ids.resize(keep);
    pr.resize(keep);
    double s2 = 0;
    for (double p : pr) s2 += p;
    for (double& p : pr) p /= s2;
}

int32_t Generator::sample(const DecodeResult& r, const GenParams& p, int32_t exclude, std::mt19937_64& rng) const {
    if (p.temperature <= 0.0f) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < r.topk_ids.size(); i++) if (r.topk_ids[i] != exclude) return r.topk_ids[i];
        return r.topk_ids.empty() ? 0 : r.topk_ids[0];
    }
    std::vector<int32_t> ids;
    std::vector<double> pr;
    distribution(r, p, ids, pr);
    double tot = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ids.size(); i++) if (ids[i] != exclude) tot += pr[i];
    if (tot <= 0) return ids.empty() ? r.topk_ids[0] : ids[0];
    double u = std::uniform_real_distribution<double>(0.0, tot)(rng);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ids.size(); i++) {
        if (ids[i] == exclude) continue;
        if ((u -= pr[i]) <= 0) return ids[i];
    }
    for (size_t i = ids.size(); i-- > 0;) if (ids[i] != exclude) return ids[i];
    return ids[0];
}

// Greedy: a draft is accepted iff it equals the target's argmax. Sampled: accepted with probability p(draft); on
// rejection the token is drawn from p without the draft (speculative sampling with deterministic drafts).
void Generator::accept(const DecodeResult* out, const int32_t* batch, int k, const GenParams& p, std::mt19937_64& rng,
                       int& accepted, int32_t& after) const {
    int j = 0;
    after = -1;
    for (; j < k; j++) {
        const int32_t d = batch[1 + j];
        bool ok;
        if (p.temperature <= 0.0f) {
            ok = !out[j].topk_ids.empty() && d == out[j].topk_ids[0];
        } else {
            std::vector<int32_t> ids;
            std::vector<double> pr;
            distribution(out[j], p, ids, pr);
            double pd = 0;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < ids.size(); i++) if (ids[i] == d) pd = pr[i];
            ok = std::uniform_real_distribution<double>(0.0, 1.0)(rng) < pd;
            if (!ok) after = sample(out[j], p, d, rng);
        }
        if (!ok) break;
    }
    if (after < 0) after = sample(out[j], p, -1, rng);   // all accepted (or greedy): the target's own next token
    accepted = j;
}

GenResult Generator::generate(const std::vector<int32_t>& prompt, const GenParams& p,
                              const std::function<bool(const std::string&)>& on_delta, size_t keep,
                              const GenObserver& observe, const std::vector<GenImage>& images) {
    GenResult res;
    res.prompt_tokens = (int)prompt.size();
    res.n_past = eng_.position();
    res.kv_capacity = eng_.max_context();
    auto event = [&](GenEvent e) { if (observe) observe(e, res); };
    // grow the KV cache to hold `positions` (timed apart: a resize takes seconds and is not prompt or decode work)
    auto grow = [&](int positions) {
        const int before = eng_.max_context();
        const double t = now_s();
        const bool ok = ensure_capacity(positions);
        if (eng_.max_context() != before) {
            res.resize_s += now_s() - t;
            res.kv_capacity = eng_.max_context();
            event(GenEvent::Resized);
        }
        return ok;
    };
    if (prompt.empty()) { res.finish_reason = "stop"; return res; }
    if (p.seed) rng_.seed(p.seed);
    const int topk = p.temperature > 0.0f ? 32 : 1;

    // ---- prompt: reuse the engine state ----
    // 1. the cached conversation (prompt + generated tokens) is a strict prefix: continue from its end;
    // 2. else the new prompt still starts with the previous request's conversation (typical agent loop: the client
    //    re-renders our last answer differently, e.g. without its reasoning): restore the snapshot taken there and
    //    process the rest;
    // 3. else start over.
    const double t0 = now_s();
    size_t common = 0;
    while (common < history_.size() && common < prompt.size() && history_[common] == prompt[common]) common++;
    // images: equal placeholder tokens are not enough, the image must be the same; and reuse never ends inside one
    auto inside = [](size_t at, int offset, int n) { return at > (size_t)offset && at < (size_t)offset + n; };
    for (const HistImage& h : history_images_) {
        if ((size_t)h.offset >= common) break;
        const auto it = std::find_if(images.begin(), images.end(), [&](const GenImage& g) { return g.offset == h.offset; });
        if (it == images.end() || it->hash != h.hash || it->n_tokens() != h.n || inside(common, h.offset, h.n)) {
            common = h.offset;
            break;
        }
    }
    for (const GenImage& g : images)
        if (inside(common, g.offset, g.n_tokens())) common = g.offset;
    size_t start = 0;
    const int snap = eng_.snapshot_position();
    if (common > 0 && common == history_.size() && common < prompt.size()) {
        start = common;
    } else if (snap > 0 && (size_t)snap <= common && (size_t)snap < prompt.size() && eng_.restore_snapshot()) {
        start = (size_t)snap;
        history_.resize(start);
        while (!history_images_.empty() && (size_t)history_images_.back().offset >= start) history_images_.pop_back();
        res.restored = true;
    }
    if (start == 0) { eng_.reset(); history_.clear(); history_images_.clear(); }
    res.cached_tokens = (int)start;
    res.n_past = (int)start;
    event(GenEvent::PromptStart);
    // capacity for the prompt and the requested tokens (as far as the limit allows)
    const int want = (int)prompt.size() + std::min(p.max_tokens, 1024) + 8;
    if ((int)prompt.size() + 8 > ctx_limit_ || !grow(std::min(want, ctx_limit_))) {
        res.finish_reason = "length";
        return res;
    }
    DecodeResult last;
    auto processed = [&](int n_past) {   // a prompt chunk is in the KV cache
        res.passes++;
        res.n_past = n_past;
        res.prefill_s = now_s() - t0 - res.resize_s;
        event(GenEvent::Prompt);
    };
    // prompt[a, b): batched prefill (with the images starting in it, encoded first), or decode batches when short
    auto process = [&](size_t a, size_t b) {
        std::vector<std::vector<float>> emb;
        std::vector<Engine::PrefillImage> spans;
        for (const GenImage& g : images) {
            if ((size_t)g.offset < a || (size_t)g.offset >= b) continue;
            const double te = now_s();
            emb.emplace_back();
            std::string err = vision_ ? "" : "no vision encoder loaded";
            if (!vision_ || !eng_.encode_image(*vision_, *g.patches, emb.back(), err)) {
                res.finish_reason = "error";
                res.error = "image encoding failed: " + err;
                return false;
            }
            res.encode_s += now_s() - te;
            res.images++;
            spans.push_back({g.offset - (int)a, g.patches->nx, g.patches->ny, nullptr});
        }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < spans.size(); i++) spans[i].emb = emb[i].data();
        if (b - a > 16 || !spans.empty()) {
            eng_.prefill_batched(std::vector<int32_t>(prompt.begin() + a, prompt.begin() + b), &last, topk, {}, nullptr,
                                 0, [&](int done) { processed((int)a + done); }, spans);
            return true;
        }
        DecodeResult out[Engine::MAX_BATCH];
        for (size_t i = a; i < b; i += Engine::MAX_BATCH) {
            const int n = (int)std::min<size_t>(Engine::MAX_BATCH, b - i);
            eng_.decode_batch(prompt.data() + i, n, topk, false, out);
            if (eng_.has_mtp()) eng_.mtp_catchup();
            last = out[n - 1];
            processed((int)(i + n));
        }
        return true;
    };
    // snapshot (~25 ms) where the next request is expected to diverge from this one; the generation prompt after
    // it costs one or two decode batches
    size_t snap_at = keep > start && keep < prompt.size() ? keep : prompt.size();
    for (const GenImage& g : images)
        if (inside(snap_at, g.offset, g.n_tokens())) snap_at = prompt.size();
    // an image could not be encoded (e.g. no VRAM for its activations): the request fails, the next one starts over
    auto fail = [&]() {
        eng_.reset();
        history_.clear();
        history_images_.clear();
        return res;
    };
    if (!process(start, snap_at)) return fail();
    eng_.save_snapshot();
    if (snap_at < prompt.size() && !process(snap_at, prompt.size())) return fail();
    history_ = prompt;
    history_images_.clear();
    for (const GenImage& g : images) history_images_.push_back({g.offset, g.n_tokens(), g.hash});
    res.prefill_s = now_s() - t0 - res.resize_s;
    res.n_past = eng_.position();
    event(GenEvent::PromptDone);

    // ---- generation ----
    const double t1 = now_s(), resize0 = res.resize_s;
    auto decoded = [&]() {   // a decode or verify pass is committed
        res.passes++;
        res.n_past = eng_.position();
        res.decode_s = now_s() - t1 - (res.resize_s - resize0);
        event(GenEvent::Decode);
    };
    std::string pending;   // decoded bytes not yet delivered (incomplete UTF-8 or possible stop-string prefix)
    size_t max_stop = 0;
    for (const std::string& s : p.stop) max_stop = std::max(max_stop, s.size());
    bool finished = false;
    // deliver text; returns false when generation must end (stop string or client abort)
    auto emit = [&](int32_t tok) -> bool {
        res.completion_tokens++;
        pending += tk_.token_to_piece(tok);
        for (const std::string& s : p.stop) {
            const size_t at = s.empty() ? std::string::npos : pending.find(s);
            if (at != std::string::npos) {
                const std::string out = pending.substr(0, at);
                res.text += out;
                if (!out.empty() && !on_delta(out)) res.finish_reason = "abort";
                else res.finish_reason = "stop";
                pending.clear();
                return false;
            }
        }
        const size_t complete = utf8_complete(pending);
        const size_t hold = max_stop > 0 ? std::min(complete, max_stop - 1) : 0;   // could start a stop string
        const size_t n_out = complete - hold;
        if (n_out > 0) {
            const std::string out = pending.substr(0, n_out);
            pending.erase(0, n_out);
            res.text += out;
            if (!on_delta(out)) { res.finish_reason = "abort"; return false; }
        }
        return true;
    };

    int32_t next = sample(last, p);
    // Seeded sampling must be reproducible: its draft lengths (which set how the RNG is consumed) may depend neither on
    // timing nor on earlier requests, so it uses a fresh controller without measured costs.
    DraftController seeded;
    seeded.adapt_cost = false;
    DraftController& dc = p.seed ? seeded : dc_;
    const bool spec = p.speculative && eng_.has_mtp();
    int k_next = 0;   // draft count chosen for the next cycle during the previous verify pass (0: choose now)
    while (!finished) {
        if (is_eos(next)) { res.finish_reason = "stop"; break; }
        if (res.completion_tokens >= p.max_tokens) { res.finish_reason = "length"; break; }
        if (eng_.position() + Engine::MAX_BATCH + 1 >= eng_.max_context() &&
            !grow(eng_.position() + Engine::MAX_BATCH + 1 + 1024)) {
            res.finish_reason = "length";
            break;
        }
        if (!spec) {
            if (!emit(next)) break;
            DecodeResult r;
            eng_.decode(next, topk, false, r);
            history_.push_back(next);
            next = sample(r, p);
            decoded();
            continue;
        }
        const int k = std::min(k_next > 0 ? k_next : dc.choose(), p.max_tokens - res.completion_tokens);
        if (k < 1) {   // last token of the budget: no point drafting
            if (!emit(next)) break;
            res.finish_reason = "length";
            break;
        }
        int32_t batch[Engine::MAX_BATCH];
        batch[0] = next;
        const double c0 = now_s();
        if (!eng_.take_drafts(next, k, batch + 1)) eng_.mtp_draft(next, k, batch + 1);
        // While the CPU computes its LM-head rows, guess the outcome from the GPU's rows (same rule, a copy of the
        // RNG) so the engine can draft the next cycle; take_drafts() uses those drafts only if the guess was right.
        k_next = 0;
        const int done = res.completion_tokens;
        const Engine::SpecGuessFn guess = [&](const DecodeResult* part, int) {
            Engine::SpecGuess g;
            std::mt19937_64 rng = rng_;
            int jj;
            int32_t aft;
            accept(part, batch, k, p, rng, jj, aft);
            for (int t = 1; t <= jj; t++) if (is_eos(batch[t])) return g;
            const int left = p.max_tokens - (done + jj + 1);
            if (is_eos(aft) || left < 1) return g;
            g.accepted = jj;
            g.next = aft;
            g.k = k_next = std::min(dc.choose(), left);
            return g;
        };
        DecodeResult out[Engine::MAX_BATCH];
        eng_.decode_batch(batch, k + 1, topk, false, out, true, &guess);
        int j;
        int32_t after;
        accept(out, batch, k, p, rng_, j, after);
        eng_.commit(j + 1);
        dc.update(k, j, now_s() - c0);
        res.cycles++;
        res.drafted += k;
        res.accepted += j;
        for (int t = 0; t <= j; t++) history_.push_back(batch[t]);   // all committed in the engine
        for (int t = 0; t <= j; t++) {
            if (t > 0 && is_eos(batch[t])) { res.finish_reason = "stop"; finished = true; break; }
            if (!emit(batch[t])) { finished = true; break; }
            if (res.completion_tokens >= p.max_tokens) { res.finish_reason = "length"; finished = true; break; }
        }
        next = after;
        decoded();
    }
    if (!pending.empty() && res.finish_reason != "abort") {   // flush what is left (not a stop string)
        res.text += pending;
        on_delta(pending);
    }
    res.decode_s = now_s() - t1 - (res.resize_s - resize0);
    res.n_past = eng_.position();
    return res;
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/metrics.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file metrics.h
/// Server observability: the metrics behind GET /metrics and the request log on stderr.
///
/// Metrics use the Prometheus text format. The llama.cpp server's names (llamacpp:*) keep existing dashboards and
/// scrapers working; engine-specific series are under q38:*. Counters advance while a request runs (after every
/// prefill chunk and decode pass), so rate() stays smooth through long generations. As in llama.cpp, the gauges
/// llamacpp:prompt_tokens_seconds and llamacpp:predicted_tokens_seconds average over the interval since the
/// previous /metrics query; q38:last_*_tokens_seconds give the most recent request's speeds.
///
/// The log has one line per request event, tagged with a task number (llama.cpp style): arrival, prompt reuse,
/// prompt processing progress (share of the prompt in the KV cache), periodic decode speed, KV-cache growth, and a
/// timing summary per request (prompt eval / eval / total time, draft acceptance).

#include <cstdint>
#include <map>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
#include "server/generator.h"

namespace q38rt {
namespace metrics {

/// What the log reports about a new request.
struct RequestInfo {
    std::string id, endpoint;   // response id; chat.completions | responses | completions
    int prompt_tokens = 0, tools = 0, max_tokens = 0, images = 0;
    std::string effort;         // reasoning effort ("" for raw completions)
    float temperature = 0;
    bool stream = false;
};

class Monitor {
public:
    /// ctx_limit: largest context (KV-cache usage ratio); kv_capacity: initial KV capacity; log_interval_s: seconds
    /// between the decode progress lines of a running request (0: no progress lines, only arrival, prompt and
    /// summary).
    Monitor(int ctx_limit, int kv_capacity, double log_interval_s);

    /// One request from arrival to completion: deferred until start(), running until finish(). The destructor
    /// settles the counters when a request ends otherwise (client gone while queued, exception).
    class Request {
    public:
        Request(Monitor& m, const RequestInfo& info);
        ~Request();
        Request(const Request&) = delete;
        Request& operator=(const Request&) = delete;
        void start();                                 // the request has the engine
        void event(GenEvent e, const GenResult& g);   // Generator observer
        /// finish: stop | length | tool_calls | abort; tool_calls: names of the called tools (logged)
        void finish(const std::string& finish, const std::vector<std::string>& tool_calls, const GenResult& g);

    private:
        struct Totals {   // a request's contribution to the counters
            double prompt_tokens = 0, prompt_s = 0, predicted = 0, predicted_s = 0, passes = 0, drafted = 0,
                   accepted = 0, cycles = 0, resize_s = 0, images = 0, encode_s = 0;
        };
        static Totals totals(const GenResult& g);
        void account(const GenResult& g);   // add the counters' progress since the last event (holds m_.mu_)
        std::string tag() const;
        Monitor& m_;
        RequestInfo info_;
        double t_arrive_;
        bool queued_ = false;       // another request had the engine on arrival
        int task_;
        enum class State { Queued, Running, Done } state_ = State::Queued;
        Totals seen_;
        double t_log_ = 0;          // last progress line
        int logged_tokens_ = 0;     // completion tokens at the last decode line
        double logged_s_ = 0;       // decode time at the last decode line
    };

    /// Prometheus text exposition; starts a new interval for the two llama.cpp throughput gauges.
    std::string prometheus();

    /// Write "HH:MM:SS.mmm " + text (+ newline) to stderr in one piece.
    static void log(const std::string& text);

private:
    std::mutex mu_;
    const int ctx_limit_;
    const double log_interval_;
    const double start_time_;
    int next_task_ = 0;
    int deferred_ = 0, processing_ = 0;
    // counters
    double prompt_tokens_ = 0, prompt_s_ = 0, predicted_ = 0, predicted_s_ = 0, passes_ = 0, cached_ = 0;
    double drafted_ = 0, accepted_ = 0, cycles_ = 0, restores_ = 0, resizes_ = 0, resize_s_ = 0;
    double images_ = 0, encode_s_ = 0;
    std::map<std::pair<std::string, std::string>, uint64_t> requests_;   // (endpoint, finish) -> requests
    // throughput since the previous /metrics query
    double b_prompt_tokens_ = 0, b_prompt_s_ = 0, b_predicted_ = 0, b_predicted_s_ = 0;
    // state
    int n_past_ = 0, kv_capacity_ = 0;
    double progress_ = 0;   // running request: share of its prompt in the KV cache
    int predicted_now_ = 0; // running request: tokens generated so far
    double last_prompt_tps_ = 0, last_predicted_tps_ = 0;
};

} // namespace metrics
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/metrics.cpp =====

/// @file metrics.cpp
/// See metrics.h.

#include "server/metrics.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <ctime>

namespace q38rt {
namespace metrics {

namespace {
double now_s() { return std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count(); }

std::string fmt(const char* f, ...) __attribute__((format(printf, 1, 2)));
std::string fmt(const char* f, ...) {
    char buf[512];
    va_list ap;
    va_start(ap, f);
    const int n = vsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, f, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    if (n < (int)sizeof buf) return std::string(buf, n > 0 ? n : 0);
    std::string s(n, '\0');
    va_start(ap, f);
    vsnprintf(&s[0], n + 1, f, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    return s;
}

double per(double a, double b) { return b > 0 ? a / b : 0.0; }
} // namespace

void Monitor::log(const std::string& text) {
    timespec ts;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);
    tm t;
    localtime_r(&ts.tv_sec, &t);
    const std::string line = fmt("%02d:%02d:%02d.%03ld ", t.tm_hour, t.tm_min, t.tm_sec, ts.tv_nsec / 1000000) + text + "\n";
    fwrite(line.data(), 1, line.size(), stderr);   // one call: lines of concurrent requests do not interleave
}

Monitor::Monitor(int ctx_limit, int kv_capacity, double log_interval_s)
    : ctx_limit_(ctx_limit), log_interval_(log_interval_s), start_time_((double)time(nullptr)), kv_capacity_(kv_capacity) {}

// ---- requests ----

Monitor::Request::Request(Monitor& m, const RequestInfo& info) : m_(m), info_(info), t_arrive_(now_s()) {
    int ahead;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_.mu_);
        task_ = m_.next_task_++;
        ahead = m_.deferred_ + m_.processing_;
        m_.deferred_++;
    }
    queued_ = ahead > 0;
    std::string s = tag() + "new request " + info.id + ": " + info.endpoint + fmt(", %d prompt tokens", info.prompt_tokens);
    if (info.tools) s += fmt(", %d tool%s", info.tools, info.tools > 1 ? "s" : "");
    if (info.images) s += fmt(", %d image%s", info.images, info.images > 1 ? "s" : "");
    if (!info.effort.empty()) s += ", reasoning " + info.effort;
    s += fmt(", max %d tokens, temperature %.2f", info.max_tokens, info.temperature);
    if (info.stream) s += ", streamed";
    if (queued_) s += fmt(" (queued behind %d request%s)", ahead, ahead > 1 ? "s" : "");
    log(s);
}

Monitor::Request::~Request() {
    if (state_ == State::Done) return;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_.mu_);
        if (state_ == State::Queued) {
            m_.deferred_--;
        } else {
            m_.processing_ = 0;
            m_.progress_ = 0;
            m_.predicted_now_ = 0;
        }
        m_.requests_[{info_.endpoint, state_ == State::Queued ? "abort" : "error"}]++;
    }
    log(tag() + (state_ == State::Queued ? "client disconnected while queued" : "ended without a result"));
}

std::string Monitor::Request::tag() const { return fmt("task %d | ", task_); }

void Monitor::Request::start() {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_.mu_);
        m_.deferred_--;
        m_.processing_ = 1;
        m_.progress_ = 0;
        m_.predicted_now_ = 0;
    }
    state_ = State::Running;
    t_log_ = now_s();
    if (queued_) log(tag() + fmt("started after waiting %.1f s", t_log_ - t_arrive_));
}

Monitor::Request::Totals Monitor::Request::totals(const GenResult& g) {
    Totals t;
    t.prompt_tokens = std::max(0, std::min(g.n_past, g.prompt_tokens) - g.cached_tokens);
    t.prompt_s = g.prefill_s;
    t.predicted = g.completion_tokens;
    t.predicted_s = g.decode_s;
    t.passes = g.passes;
    t.drafted = g.drafted;
    t.accepted = g.accepted;
    t.cycles = g.cycles;
    t.resize_s = g.resize_s;
    t.images = g.images;
    t.encode_s = g.encode_s;
    return t;
}

void Monitor::Request::account(const GenResult& g) {
    const Totals t = totals(g);
    Monitor& m = m_;
    m.prompt_tokens_ += t.prompt_tokens - seen_.prompt_tokens;
    m.prompt_s_ += t.prompt_s - seen_.prompt_s;
    m.predicted_ += t.predicted - seen_.predicted;
    m.predicted_s_ += t.predicted_s - seen_.predicted_s;
    m.b_prompt_tokens_ += t.prompt_tokens - seen_.prompt_tokens;
    m.b_prompt_s_ += t.prompt_s - seen_.prompt_s;
    m.b_predicted_ += t.predicted - seen_.predicted;
    m.b_predicted_s_ += t.predicted_s - seen_.predicted_s;
    m.passes_ += t.passes - seen_.passes;
    m.drafted_ += t.drafted - seen_.drafted;
    m.accepted_ += t.accepted - seen_.accepted;
    m.cycles_ += t.cycles - seen_.cycles;
    m.resize_s_ += t.resize_s - seen_.resize_s;
    m.images_ += t.images - seen_.images;
    m.encode_s_ += t.encode_s - seen_.encode_s;
    m.n_past_ = g.n_past;
    m.kv_capacity_ = g.kv_capacity;
    seen_ = t;
}

void Monitor::Request::event(GenEvent e, const GenResult& g) {
    const double now = now_s();
    const double resize_s = g.resize_s - seen_.resize_s;   // this resize (before account() moves seen_)
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_.mu_);
        account(g);
        m_.progress_ = g.prompt_tokens > 0 ? std::min(1.0, (double)g.n_past / g.prompt_tokens) : 1.0;
        m_.predicted_now_ = g.completion_tokens;
        if (e == GenEvent::PromptStart) {
            m_.cached_ += g.cached_tokens;
            if (g.restored) m_.restores_++;
        }
        if (e == GenEvent::Resized) m_.resizes_++;
    }
    switch (e) {
    case GenEvent::PromptStart: {
        std::string s = tag() + fmt("prompt: %d tokens", g.prompt_tokens);
        if (g.cached_tokens)
            s += fmt(", %d reused from the %s", g.cached_tokens,
                     g.restored ? "snapshot of the previous request" : "cached conversation");
        log(s + fmt(", %d to process", g.prompt_tokens - g.cached_tokens));
        t_log_ = now;
        break;
    }
    case GenEvent::Prompt:   // every prefill chunk (~2 s), at most one line per second
        if (m_.log_interval_ > 0 && g.n_past < g.prompt_tokens && now - t_log_ >= std::min(1.0, m_.log_interval_)) {
            log(tag() + fmt("prompt processing progress: n_past = %d / %d (%.1f%%), %.1f tok/s", g.n_past, g.prompt_tokens,
                            100.0 * g.n_past / g.prompt_tokens, per(g.n_past - g.cached_tokens, g.prefill_s)));
            t_log_ = now;
        }
        break;
    case GenEvent::PromptDone: {
        const int n = g.prompt_tokens - g.cached_tokens;
        std::string s = tag() + fmt("prompt done: %d tokens in %.0f ms (%.1f tok/s), n_past = %d", n, g.prefill_s * 1e3,
                                    per(n, g.prefill_s), g.n_past);
        if (g.images) s += fmt(", %d image%s encoded in %.0f ms", g.images, g.images > 1 ? "s" : "", g.encode_s * 1e3);
        log(s);
        t_log_ = now;
        break;
    }
    case GenEvent::Decode:
        if (m_.log_interval_ > 0 && now - t_log_ >= m_.log_interval_) {
            std::string s = tag() + fmt("decoding: n_decoded = %d, %.2f tok/s", g.completion_tokens,
                                        per(g.completion_tokens, g.decode_s));
            if (logged_tokens_ > 0)
                s += fmt(" (last %.0f s: %.2f tok/s)", g.decode_s - logged_s_,
                         per(g.completion_tokens - logged_tokens_, g.decode_s - logged_s_));
            if (g.drafted) s += fmt(", draft acceptance %.0f%%", 100.0 * g.accepted / g.drafted);
            log(s);
            t_log_ = now;
            logged_tokens_ = g.completion_tokens;
            logged_s_ = g.decode_s;
        }
        break;
    case GenEvent::Resized:
        log(tag() + fmt("KV cache grown to %d tokens in %.1f s (n_past = %d)", g.kv_capacity, resize_s, g.n_past));
        break;
    }
}

void Monitor::Request::finish(const std::string& finish, const std::vector<std::string>& tool_calls, const GenResult& g) {
    const int n = std::max(0, g.prompt_tokens - g.cached_tokens);
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_.mu_);
        account(g);
        m_.requests_[{info_.endpoint, finish}]++;
        m_.processing_ = 0;
        m_.progress_ = 0;
        m_.predicted_now_ = 0;
        if (n > 0 && g.prefill_s > 0) m_.last_prompt_tps_ = n / g.prefill_s;
        if (g.completion_tokens > 0 && g.decode_s > 0) m_.last_predicted_tps_ = g.completion_tokens / g.decode_s;
    }
    state_ = State::Done;
    std::string what = finish;
    if (finish == "tool_calls") {
        what = "tool calls (";
        for (size_t i = 0; i < tool_calls.size(); i++) what += (i ? ", " : "") + tool_calls[i];
        what += ")";
    } else if (finish == "length") {
        what = "length limit";
    } else if (finish == "abort") {
        what = "aborted (client disconnected)";
    } else if (finish == "error") {
        what = "error: " + g.error;
    }
    const double pt = g.prefill_s * 1e3, et = g.decode_s * 1e3;
    std::string s = tag() + fmt("done: %s, n_past = %d (%.1f%% of the %d-token context)\n", what.c_str(), g.n_past,
                                100.0 * g.n_past / m_.ctx_limit_, m_.ctx_limit_);
    s += fmt("prompt eval time = %10.2f ms / %5d tokens (%8.2f ms per token, %8.2f tokens per second)\n", pt, n,
             per(pt, n), per(n, g.prefill_s));
    if (g.images) s += fmt("image encode time = %9.2f ms / %5d images (included in prompt eval)\n", g.encode_s * 1e3, g.images);
    s += fmt("       eval time = %10.2f ms / %5d tokens (%8.2f ms per token, %8.2f tokens per second)\n", et,
             g.completion_tokens, per(et, g.completion_tokens), per(g.completion_tokens, g.decode_s));
    if (g.resize_s > 0) s += fmt("     resize time = %10.2f ms (KV cache grown to %d tokens)\n", g.resize_s * 1e3, g.kv_capacity);
    s += fmt("      total time = %10.2f ms / %5d tokens", pt + et + g.resize_s * 1e3, n + g.completion_tokens);
    if (g.drafted)
        s += fmt("\ndraft acceptance rate = %0.5f (%5d accepted / %5d generated), %d verify passes, %.2f tokens per pass",
                 per(g.accepted, g.drafted), g.accepted, g.drafted, g.cycles, per(g.accepted + g.cycles, g.cycles));
    log(s);
}

// ---- exposition ----

std::string Monitor::prometheus() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(mu_);
    std::string out;
    auto head = [&](const char* name, const char* type, const char* help) {
        out += fmt("# HELP %s %s\n# TYPE %s %s\n", name, help, name, type);
    };
    auto metric = [&](const char* name, const char* type, const char* help, double v) {
        head(name, type, help);
        out += fmt("%s %.10g\n", name, v);
    };
    // llama.cpp server names
    metric("llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total", "counter", "Number of prompt tokens processed.", prompt_tokens_);
    metric("llamacpp:prompt_seconds_total", "counter", "Prompt process time", prompt_s_);
    metric("llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total", "counter", "Number of generation tokens processed.", predicted_);
    metric("llamacpp:tokens_predicted_seconds_total", "counter", "Predict process time", predicted_s_);
    metric("llamacpp:n_decode_total", "counter",
           "Total number of engine forward passes (prefill chunks, decode and verify passes).", passes_);
    metric("llamacpp:n_busy_slots_per_decode", "counter", "Average number of busy slots per forward pass (one slot).",
           passes_ > 0 ? 1.0 : 0.0);
    metric("llamacpp:prompt_tokens_seconds", "gauge", "Average prompt throughput in tokens/s (since the previous query).",
           per(b_prompt_tokens_, b_prompt_s_));
    metric("llamacpp:predicted_tokens_seconds", "gauge",
           "Average generation throughput in tokens/s (since the previous query).", per(b_predicted_, b_predicted_s_));
    metric("llamacpp:kv_cache_usage_ratio", "gauge", "KV-cache usage (of the context limit). 1 = 100 percent usage.",
           per(n_past_, ctx_limit_));
    metric("llamacpp:kv_cache_tokens", "gauge", "KV-cache tokens.", n_past_);
    metric("llamacpp:requests_processing", "gauge", "Number of requests processing.", processing_);
    metric("llamacpp:requests_deferred", "gauge", "Number of requests deferred.", deferred_);
    // engine-specific
    metric("q38:prompt_tokens_cached_total", "counter",
           "Prompt tokens reused from the KV cache (cached conversation or snapshot) instead of processed.", cached_);
    metric("q38:snapshot_restores_total", "counter",
           "Requests whose prompt continued from the engine snapshot of the previous request.", restores_);
    metric("q38:draft_tokens_total", "counter", "MTP draft tokens verified.", drafted_);
    metric("q38:draft_tokens_accepted_total", "counter", "MTP draft tokens accepted.", accepted_);
    metric("q38:verify_passes_total", "counter", "Speculative verify passes.", cycles_);
    metric("q38:draft_acceptance_ratio", "gauge", "Accepted / verified MTP draft tokens since start.",
           per(accepted_, drafted_));
    metric("q38:images_encoded_total", "counter", "Images run through the vision encoder (cached ones are not).", images_);
    metric("q38:image_encode_seconds_total", "counter", "Time spent in the vision encoder (part of prompt processing).",
           encode_s_);
    metric("q38:kv_cache_resizes_total", "counter", "KV-cache growths.", resizes_);
    metric("q38:kv_cache_resize_seconds_total", "counter", "Time spent growing the KV cache.", resize_s_);
    metric("q38:kv_cache_capacity_tokens", "gauge", "Allocated KV-cache capacity (grows on demand up to the context limit).",
           kv_capacity_);
    metric("q38:context_limit_tokens", "gauge", "Largest context (prompt + generated tokens).", ctx_limit_);
    metric("q38:request_prompt_progress", "gauge", "Running request: share of its prompt in the KV cache (0 when idle).",
           processing_ ? progress_ : 0.0);
    metric("q38:request_tokens_predicted", "gauge", "Running request: tokens generated so far (0 when idle).",
           processing_ ? predicted_now_ : 0);
    metric("q38:last_prompt_tokens_seconds", "gauge", "Prompt throughput of the last finished request.", last_prompt_tps_);
    metric("q38:last_predicted_tokens_seconds", "gauge", "Generation throughput of the last finished request.",
           last_predicted_tps_);
    head("q38:requests_total", "counter", "Finished requests by endpoint and finish reason.");
    for (const auto& [key, n] : requests_)
        out += fmt("q38:requests_total{endpoint=\"%s\",finish=\"%s\"} %llu\n", key.first.c_str(), key.second.c_str(),
                   (unsigned long long)n);
    metric("process_start_time_seconds", "gauge", "Start time of the process since unix epoch in seconds.", start_time_);
    b_prompt_tokens_ = b_prompt_s_ = b_predicted_ = b_predicted_s_ = 0;
    return out;
}

} // namespace metrics
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/api.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file api.h
/// OpenAI-compatible HTTP API on top of Generator, for agent harnesses and OpenAI SDK clients:
///
///   POST /v1/chat/completions  Chat Completions: tools, tool_choice, parallel_tool_calls, reasoning control
///                              (reasoning_effort, chat_template_kwargs), streaming with tool-call deltas
///   POST /v1/responses         Responses API subset: message / function_call / function_call_output / reasoning
///                              input items, instructions, function tools, previous_response_id (in-memory),
///                              streaming events
///   POST /v1/completions       raw text completion (no chat template)
///   GET  /v1/models, /v1/models/{id}, /health
///   GET  /metrics              Prometheus metrics (llama.cpp names + engine-specific ones; see metrics.h)
///
/// The /v1 prefix is optional. Prompts are rendered with chat::render (the GGUF's chat template); the model's
/// output is split into reasoning (inside <think>), content and tool calls (the template's XML format, returned
/// as OpenAI tool calls with JSON arguments). With a vision encoder, messages may carry images (Chat image_url parts,
/// Responses input_image parts) as data: URLs (base64 PNG, JPEG, BMP, GIF, ...); remote URLs are not fetched. One
/// generation runs at a time: concurrent requests wait their turn. Each request is logged on stderr (arrival, prompt
/// reuse and processing progress, decode speed, timings).

#include <deque>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
#include "runtime/tokenizer.h"
#include "server/generator.h"
#include "server/http.h"
#include "server/json.h"
#include "server/metrics.h"

namespace q38rt {
namespace api {

struct Config {
    std::string model_name = "qwen3.8-27b";
    int ctx_limit = 32768;                     // prompt + generated tokens
    int max_tokens = 0;                        // cap on generated tokens per request (0: up to the context limit)
    bool thinking = true;                      // default when a request does not choose
    std::string effort = "xhigh";              // default reasoning effort (template values: xhigh | medium | low)
    float temperature = 0.0f, top_p = 1.0f;    // sampling defaults when a request does not choose
    int top_k = 0;
    bool speculative = true;
    std::string api_key;                       // if set, /v1/* and /metrics require "Authorization: Bearer <key>"
    int kv_init = 0;                           // initial KV capacity (reported by /metrics)
    double log_interval = 5;                   // seconds between decode progress lines (0: none)
};

/// Responses API `input` (a string or a list of items) as Chat Completions messages: message items keep their
/// role, function_call items become assistant tool calls (consecutive ones grouped into one assistant turn),
/// function_call_output items become tool messages, reasoning items become the next assistant turn's
/// reasoning_content. False with a client-facing message on unsupported items.
bool responses_input_to_messages(const json::Value& input, json::Value& messages, std::string& err);

/// Tools of either API in the Chat Completions form the template renders ({"type": "function", "function":
/// {"name", "description", "parameters"}}). Chat-form tools are kept as sent. Tools that are not functions
/// (built-in tools of the Responses API) are left out and their types listed in `skipped`.
bool normalize_tools(const json::Value& tools, json::Value& out, std::vector<std::string>& skipped, std::string& err);

class Server {
public:
    /// vision: the mmproj encoder for image input (null: text only).
    Server(Generator& gen, const Tokenizer& tk, const Config& cfg, const VisionModel* vision = nullptr);
    ~Server();
    /// http::Handler. Thread-safe.
    void handle(const http::Request& req, http::Responder& res);

private:
    struct Impl;
    std::unique_ptr<Impl> impl_;
};

} // namespace api
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== tools/q38_server.cpp =====

/// @file q38_server.cpp
/// OpenAI-compatible HTTP server for QWENgine (see src/server/api.h for the endpoints, docs/SERVER.md for usage).
///
///   q38_server <model.gguf> [--host 127.0.0.1] [--port 8080] [--ctx 32768] [--ctx-init 4096] [--kv q8_0|q4_0|f16]
///              [--name qwen3.8-27b] [--api-key KEY] [--no-think] [--effort xhigh|medium|low]
///              [--temp T] [--top-p P] [--top-k K] [--max-tokens N] [--no-spec] [--threads N] [--log-interval S]
///              [--mmproj mmproj.gguf] [--image-min-tokens N] [--image-max-tokens N]
///
/// Sampling defaults (used when a request does not set them) come from the GGUF's general.sampling.* keys, else
/// greedy. The API key can also be given in the Q38_API_KEY environment variable.

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>
#include "runtime/engine.h"
#include "runtime/tokenizer.h"
#include "runtime/vision.h"
#include "server/api.h"
#include "server/generator.h"
#include "server/http.h"

using namespace q38rt;

static void usage(const char* argv0) {
    fprintf(stderr,
            "usage: %s <model.gguf> [options]\n"
            "  --host H           listen address (default 127.0.0.1; 0.0.0.0 for all interfaces)\n"
            "  --port P           port (default 8080)\n"
            "  --ctx N            largest context (prompt + output tokens; default 32768, at most 262144)\n"
            "  --ctx-init N       initial KV-cache size, doubled on demand up to --ctx (default 4096)\n"
            "  --kv T             KV-cache type: q8_0 (default), q4_0, f16\n"
            "  --name NAME        model id reported by /v1/models (default qwen3.8-27b; requests may use any name)\n"
            "  --api-key KEY      require 'Authorization: Bearer KEY' (or set Q38_API_KEY)\n"
            "  --no-think         answer without reasoning unless a request asks for it\n"
            "  --effort E         default reasoning effort: xhigh (default), medium, low\n"
            "  --temp T, --top-p P, --top-k K\n"
            "                     sampling defaults (default: the GGUF's recommended values)\n"
            "  --max-tokens N     cap on generated tokens per request (default: up to the context limit)\n"
            "  --no-spec          disable MTP speculative decoding\n"
            "  --threads N        CPU threads for the CPU-resident weights\n"
            "  --log-interval S   seconds between decode progress lines of a running request (default 5; 0: none)\n"
            "  --mmproj FILE      vision encoder (mmproj GGUF) for image input\n"
            "  --image-min-tokens N, --image-max-tokens N\n"
            "                     image size range in tokens of 32x32 pixels (default 8 and 1024): images are scaled\n"
            "                     to fit, keeping their aspect ratio\n",
            argv0);
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    if (argc < 2 || argv[1][0] == '-') { usage(argv[0]); return 2; }
    EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.model_path = argv[1];
    api::Config ac;
    int ctx_init = 4096;
    std::string host = "127.0.0.1";
    int port = 8080;
    float temp = -1, top_p = -1;
    int top_k = -1;
    std::string mmproj;
    int img_min = 8, img_max = 1024;
    if (const char* k = getenv("Q38_API_KEY")) ac.api_key = k;
    for (int i = 2; i < argc; i++) {
        const std::string a = argv[i];
        auto next = [&]() {
            if (i + 1 >= argc) { fprintf(stderr, "option %s needs a value\n", a.c_str()); exit(2); }
            return std::string(argv[++i]);
        };
        if (a == "--host") host = next();
        else if (a == "--port") port = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--ctx") ac.ctx_limit = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--ctx-init") ctx_init = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--threads") cfg.cpu_threads = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--name") ac.model_name = next();
        else if (a == "--api-key") ac.api_key = next();
        else if (a == "--no-think") ac.thinking = false;
        else if (a == "--effort") ac.effort = next();
        else if (a == "--temp") temp = (float)atof(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--top-p") top_p = (float)atof(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--top-k") top_k = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--max-tokens") ac.max_tokens = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--no-spec") ac.speculative = false;
        else if (a == "--log-interval") ac.log_interval = atof(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--mmproj") mmproj = next();
        else if (a == "--image-min-tokens") img_min = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--image-max-tokens") img_max = atoi(next().c_str());
        else if (a == "--kv") {
            const std::string k = next();
            if (k != "q8_0" && k != "q4_0" && k != "f16") { fprintf(stderr, "--kv: q8_0, q4_0 or f16\n"); return 2; }
            cfg.kv_type = k == "f16" ? cuda::KVType::F16 : k == "q4_0" ? cuda::KVType::Q4_0 : cuda::KVType::Q8_0;
        } else if (a == "-h" || a == "--help") { usage(argv[0]); return 0; }
        else { fprintf(stderr, "unknown option %s\n", a.c_str()); usage(argv[0]); return 2; }
    }
    if (ac.effort != "xhigh" && ac.effort != "medium" && ac.effort != "low") {
        fprintf(stderr, "--effort: xhigh, medium or low\n");
        return 2;
    }
    if (ac.ctx_limit < 256) { fprintf(stderr, "--ctx must be at least 256\n"); return 2; }
    if (img_min < 1 || img_max < img_min) { fprintf(stderr, "--image-min-tokens / --image-max-tokens: 1 <= min <= max\n"); return 2; }
    VisionModel vision;   // loaded first: a bad file fails before the long engine start
    if (!mmproj.empty() && !vision.load(mmproj, img_min, img_max)) return 1;
    // the KV cache starts small (more VRAM for weights = faster decode) and doubles on demand up to --ctx
    cfg.max_context = std::min(ctx_init, ac.ctx_limit);
    if (!ac.speculative) cfg.spec_k = 0;   // weight plan for plain decode
    ac.kv_init = cfg.max_context;
    Engine eng;
    if (!eng.init(cfg)) return 1;
    if (ac.ctx_limit > eng.max_context_limit()) {
        fprintf(stderr, "--ctx %d exceeds the supported context of %d tokens\n", ac.ctx_limit, eng.max_context_limit());
        return 2;
    }
    Tokenizer tk;
    if (!tk.load(eng.gguf())) return 1;
    eng.set_verbose(false);   // the plan was shown; later re-plans (KV-cache growth) are logged per request

    const GGUFParser& g = eng.gguf();
    ac.temperature = temp >= 0 ? temp : g.find_kv("general.sampling.temp") ? g.get_kv_float("general.sampling.temp") : 0.0f;
    ac.top_p = top_p >= 0 ? top_p : g.find_kv("general.sampling.top_p") ? g.get_kv_float("general.sampling.top_p") : 1.0f;
    ac.top_k = top_k >= 0 ? top_k : (int)g.get_kv_int("general.sampling.top_k", 0);

    Generator gen(eng, tk, ac.ctx_limit);
    api::Server server(gen, tk, ac, vision.loaded() ? &vision : nullptr);
    fprintf(stderr,
            "q38_server: %s on http://%s:%d/v1 (context up to %d tokens, KV cache growing from %d; MTP %s; thinking %s "
            "by default, effort %s; sampling temp %.2f top-p %.2f top-k %d%s%s); metrics at http://%s:%d/metrics\n",
            ac.model_name.c_str(), host.c_str(), port, ac.ctx_limit, cfg.max_context,
            eng.has_mtp() && ac.speculative ? "speculative" : "off", ac.thinking ? "on" : "off", ac.effort.c_str(),
            ac.temperature, ac.top_p, ac.top_k, vision.loaded() ? "; image input" : "",
            ac.api_key.empty() ? "" : "; API key required", host.c_str(), port);
    return http::serve(host, port, [&](const http::Request& req, http::Responder& res) { server.handle(req, res); }) ? 0 : 1;
}

// ===== src/server/chat.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file chat.h
/// The model's chat format, ported from the chat template stored in the GGUF (tokenizer.chat_template, a Jinja
/// template of the Qwen3.5 family): rendering of OpenAI-style message lists (system / user / assistant with
/// reasoning and tool calls / tool results) and tool definitions into the prompt text, and parsing of the model's
/// output (reasoning in <think>, content, tool calls in the XML format the template teaches).
///
/// render_chat() reproduces the template byte for byte (tests/unit/test_chat checks it against the Jinja template
/// rendered by jinja2), with two documented relaxations: system/developer messages after the first are rendered in
/// place as system turns (the template raises), and video parts are rejected (no video input). Image parts render as
/// the template's placeholder <|vision_start|><|image_pad|><|vision_end|>; the caller expands each <|image_pad|> to
/// the image's tokens.

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "server/json.h"

namespace q38rt {
namespace chat {

struct Options {
    bool enable_thinking = true;            // false: empty <think></think> block, no reasoning instructions
    std::string reasoning_effort = "xhigh"; // xhigh | medium | low (template values; used when thinking is on)
    bool preserve_thinking = true;          // false: drop reasoning of assistant turns before the last user query
    bool add_generation_prompt = true;
};

/// Render `messages` (+ `tools`, an array of OpenAI tool objects or null) into the prompt text. Returns false
/// with a client-facing message on invalid input. `images` receives the URL of each image part in prompt order
/// ({"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": U}} or "image_url": U, "image": U); null: images are refused.
bool render(const json::Value& messages, const json::Value& tools, const Options& opt, std::string& out, std::string& err,
            std::vector<std::string>* images = nullptr);

/// Tool call parsed from model output. `arguments` is a JSON object.
struct ToolCall {
    std::string id, name;
    json::Value arguments;
};

/// Split assistant output (the part after </think>) into content and tool calls. Parameter values are typed with
/// the tool's JSON schema (strings stay strings; other types are parsed as JSON, falling back to the raw string).
void parse_tool_calls(const std::string& text, const json::Value& tools, std::string& content,
                      std::vector<ToolCall>& calls);

/// Render one tool call the way the template renders an assistant's tool call (used to prefill forced calls).
std::string tool_call_opening(const std::string& name);

/// Streaming split of model text into reasoning (before </think>) and content (after it, leading newlines removed).
/// Text that may be the start of the marker is held back until resolved.
class ThinkSplitter {
public:
    explicit ThinkSplitter(bool thinking) : thinking_(thinking) {}
    void feed(const std::string& text, std::string& reasoning, std::string& content);
    void finish(std::string& reasoning, std::string& content);
    bool thinking() const { return thinking_; }

private:
    bool thinking_;
    bool content_started_ = false;
    std::string held_;
};

/// Streaming split of content into plain text (safe to emit) and tool-call text (from the first "<tool_call>" on).
/// Trailing whitespace and any suffix that may start "<tool_call>" are held back until resolved.
class ToolCallStreamer {
public:
    std::string feed(const std::string& text);   // returns plain text to emit now
    std::string finish();                        // remaining plain text (no tool call started)
    bool in_call() const { return in_call_; }
    const std::string& call_text() const { return call_; }   // "<tool_call>..." (complete after finish)

private:
    bool in_call_ = false;
    std::string held_, call_;
};

} // namespace chat
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/chat.cpp =====

/// @file chat.cpp
/// See chat.h. The rendering follows the Jinja template in the GGUF statement by statement; the comments quote the
/// template's conditions.

#include "server/chat.h"
#include <cstring>
#include <random>

namespace q38rt {
namespace chat {

namespace {

const char* kReasoningXhigh =
    "Reasoning effort is set to xhigh. Please think carefully through the task, validate key assumptions, consider "
    "plausible alternatives, and prioritize correctness, consistency, and clarity in the final answer.";
const char* kReasoningLow =
    "Reasoning effort is set to low. Keep your thinking brief and focused, moving directly to the conclusion without "
    "unnecessary elaboration.";
const char* kToolsHeader = "# Tools\n\nYou have access to the following functions:\n\n<tools>";
const char* kToolsFooter =
    "\n</tools>\n\nIf you choose to call a function ONLY reply in the following format with NO suffix:\n\n<tool_call>\n"
    "<function=example_function_name>\n<parameter=example_parameter_1>\nvalue_1\n</parameter>\n"
    "<parameter=example_parameter_2>\nThis is the value for the second parameter\nthat can span\nmultiple lines\n"
    "</parameter>\n</function>\n</tool_call>\n\n<IMPORTANT>\nReminder:\n- Function calls MUST follow the specified "
    "format: an inner <function=...></function> block must be nested within <tool_call></tool_call> XML tags\n"
    "- Required parameters MUST be specified\n- You may provide optional reasoning for your function call in natural "
    "language BEFORE the function call, but NOT after\n- If there is no function call available, answer the question "
    "like normal with your current knowledge and do not tell the user about function calls\n</IMPORTANT>";

// Length of the whitespace character (Python str.isspace) starting at s[i], 0 if none.
size_t space_len(const std::string& s, size_t i) {
    const unsigned char c = (unsigned char)s[i];
    if (c == ' ' || (c >= 0x09 && c <= 0x0d) || (c >= 0x1c && c <= 0x1f)) return 1;
    if (c == 0xC2 && i + 1 < s.size() && ((unsigned char)s[i + 1] == 0x85 || (unsigned char)s[i + 1] == 0xA0)) return 2;
    if (c == 0xE1 && i + 2 < s.size() && (unsigned char)s[i + 1] == 0x9A && (unsigned char)s[i + 2] == 0x80) return 3;
    if (c == 0xE2 && i + 2 < s.size()) {
        const unsigned char b = (unsigned char)s[i + 1], d = (unsigned char)s[i + 2];
        if (b == 0x80 && ((d >= 0x80 && d <= 0x8A) || d == 0xA8 || d == 0xA9 || d == 0xAF)) return 3;
        if (b == 0x81 && d == 0x9F) return 3;
    }
    if (c == 0xE3 && i + 2 < s.size() && (unsigned char)s[i + 1] == 0x80 && (unsigned char)s[i + 2] == 0x80) return 3;
    return 0;
}

// Python str.strip() (Jinja |trim)
std::string trim(const std::string& s) {
    size_t a = 0;
    while (a < s.size()) { const size_t n = space_len(s, a); if (!n) break; a += n; }
    size_t b = s.size();
    while (b > a) {
        size_t n = 0;
        for (size_t k = 1; k <= 3 && k <= b - a; k++) if (space_len(s, b - k) == k) { n = k; break; }
        if (!n) break;
        b -= n;
    }
    return s.substr(a, b - a);
}

std::string rstrip(const std::string& s) {
    size_t b = s.size();
    while (b > 0 && (s[b - 1] == ' ' || s[b - 1] == '\n' || s[b - 1] == '\t' || s[b - 1] == '\r')) b--;
    return s.substr(0, b);
}

bool starts_with(const std::string& s, const char* p) { return s.compare(0, strlen(p), p) == 0; }
bool ends_with(const std::string& s, const char* p) {
    const size_t n = strlen(p);
    return s.size() >= n && s.compare(s.size() - n, n, p) == 0;
}

// render_content(content): string, array of parts with "text" or an image (URLs appended to `images`), or none
bool render_content(const json::Value& c, std::string& out, std::string& err, std::vector<std::string>* images,
                    bool is_system) {
    out.clear();
    if (c.is_string()) { out = c.as_string(); return true; }
    if (c.is_null()) return true;
    if (c.is_array()) {
        for (const json::Value& item : c.items()) {
            const std::string type = item["type"].as_string();
            if (item.has("image") || item.has("image_url") || type == "image" || type == "image_url") {
                if (is_system) { err = "System message cannot contain images."; return false; }
                if (!images) { err = "image input needs the vision encoder (start the server with --mmproj)"; return false; }
                const json::Value& u = item.has("image_url") ? item["image_url"] : item["image"];
                const std::string url = u.is_object() ? u["url"].as_string() : u.as_string();
                if (url.empty()) { err = "image content part without a URL"; return false; }
                images->push_back(url);
                out += "<|vision_start|><|image_pad|><|vision_end|>";
                continue;
            }
            if (item.has("video") || type == "video" || type == "input_audio" || type == "file") {
                err = "video, audio and file content parts are not supported";
                return false;
            }
            if (item.has("text")) { out += item["text"].as_string(); continue; }
            err = "unexpected item type in message content";
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    err = "unexpected message content type";
    return false;
}

std::string role_of(const json::Value& m) {
    std::string r = m["role"].as_string();
    if (r == "developer") r = "system";
    if (r == "function") r = "tool";
    return r;
}

std::string random_call_id() {
    static thread_local std::mt19937_64 rng{std::random_device{}()};
    static const char* hex = "0123456789abcdef";
    std::string id = "call_";
    for (int i = 0; i < 24; i++) id += hex[rng() % 16];
    return id;
}

// JSON schema of tool parameter `param` of function `name` (null if unknown)
const json::Value& param_schema(const json::Value& tools, const std::string& name, const std::string& param) {
    static const json::Value none;
    for (const json::Value& t : tools.items()) {
        const json::Value& f = t.has("function") ? t["function"] : t;   // chat format or flat (Responses API)
        if (f["name"].as_string() == name) return f["parameters"]["properties"][param];
    }
    return none;
}

bool schema_is_string(const json::Value& schema) {
    const json::Value& t = schema["type"];
    if (t.is_string()) return t.as_string() == "string";
    if (t.is_array()) {
        bool str = false, other = false;
        for (const json::Value& x : t.items()) {
            if (x.as_string() == "string") str = true;
            else if (x.as_string() != "null") other = true;
        }
        return str && !other;
    }
    return false;
}

json::Value typed_value(const std::string& raw, const json::Value& schema) {
    if (schema_is_string(schema)) return json::Value(raw);
    json::Value v;
    const std::string t = trim(raw);
    if (!t.empty() && json::Value::parse(t, v)) return v;
    if (t == "True" || t == "TRUE") return json::Value(true);
    if (t == "False" || t == "FALSE") return json::Value(false);
    return json::Value(raw);
}

// one "<tool_call>" body (without the tags): XML <function=...> form, or a JSON {"name", "arguments"} object
bool parse_one(const std::string& body_in, const json::Value& tools, ToolCall& call) {
    const std::string body = trim(body_in);
    call.id = random_call_id();
    call.arguments = json::Value::object();
    if (starts_with(body, "{")) {
        json::Value j;
        if (!json::Value::parse(body, j) || !j["name"].is_string()) return false;
        call.name = j["name"].as_string();
        const json::Value& a = j.has("arguments") ? j["arguments"] : j["parameters"];
        if (a.is_object()) call.arguments = a;
        else if (a.is_string()) { json::Value p; if (json::Value::parse(a.as_string(), p) && p.is_object()) call.arguments = p; }
        return true;
    }
    const size_t f = body.find("<function=");
    if (f == std::string::npos) return false;
    const size_t ne = body.find('>', f);
    if (ne == std::string::npos) return false;
    call.name = trim(body.substr(f + 10, ne - f - 10));
    size_t cur = ne + 1;
    while (true) {
        const size_t ps = body.find("<parameter=", cur);
        if (ps == std::string::npos) break;
        const size_t ke = body.find('>', ps);
        if (ke == std::string::npos) break;
        const std::string key = trim(body.substr(ps + 11, ke - ps - 11));
        size_t vs = ke + 1, ve = body.find("</parameter>", vs);
        size_t next = ve == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : ve + 12;
        if (ve == std::string::npos) {   // unterminated (truncated output): up to the next tag or the end
            ve = std::min(body.find("<parameter=", vs), body.find("</function>", vs));
            if (ve == std::string::npos) ve = body.size();
            next = ve;
        }
        std::string val = body.substr(vs, ve - vs);
        if (!val.empty() && val.front() == '\n') val.erase(0, 1);   // template: "<parameter=k>\n" value "\n</parameter>"
        if (!val.empty() && val.back() == '\n') val.pop_back();
        call.arguments.set(key, typed_value(val, param_schema(tools, call.name, key)));
        if (next == std::string::npos || next >= body.size()) break;
        cur = next;
    }
    return !call.name.empty();
}

} // namespace

bool render(const json::Value& messages, const json::Value& tools, const Options& opt, std::string& out, std::string& err,
            std::vector<std::string>* images) {
    out.clear();
    if (!messages.is_array() || messages.items().empty()) { err = "'messages' must be a non-empty array"; return false; }
    const std::vector<json::Value>& msgs = messages.items();
    const size_t n = msgs.size();
    for (const json::Value& m : msgs) {
        if (!m.is_object()) { err = "every message must be an object"; return false; }
        const std::string r = role_of(m);
        if (r != "system" && r != "user" && r != "assistant" && r != "tool") {
            err = "unsupported message role '" + m["role"].as_string() + "'";
            return false;
        }
    }
    // contents in message order = the order they appear in the prompt (each is rendered once), so images are listed in
    // prompt order
    std::vector<std::string> content(n);
    if (images) images->clear();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++)
        if (!render_content(msgs[i]["content"], content[i], err, images, role_of(msgs[i]) == "system")) return false;

    // {%- if enable_thinking is undefined or enable_thinking is true %} reasoning instructions
    std::string ri;
    if (opt.enable_thinking) {
        if (opt.reasoning_effort == "xhigh") ri = kReasoningXhigh;
        else if (opt.reasoning_effort == "low") ri = kReasoningLow;
        else if (opt.reasoning_effort != "medium") {
            err = "unsupported reasoning effort '" + opt.reasoning_effort + "' (xhigh, medium, low)";
            return false;
        }
    }
    const bool first_system = role_of(msgs[0]) == "system";
    const bool have_tools = tools.is_array() && !tools.items().empty();
    if (have_tools) {
        out += "<|im_start|>system\n";
        if (!ri.empty()) out += ri + "\n\n";
        out += kToolsHeader;
        for (const json::Value& t : tools.items()) out += "\n" + t.dump_py();
        out += kToolsFooter;
        if (first_system) {
            const std::string c = trim(content[0]);
            if (!c.empty()) out += "\n\n" + c;
        }
        out += "<|im_end|>\n";
    } else if (first_system) {
        const std::string c = trim(content[0]);
        if (!c.empty()) out += "<|im_start|>system\n" + (ri.empty() ? std::string() : ri + "\n\n") + c + "<|im_end|>\n";
        else if (!ri.empty()) out += "<|im_start|>system\n" + ri + "<|im_end|>\n";
    } else if (!ri.empty()) {
        out += "<|im_start|>system\n" + ri + "<|im_end|>\n";
    }

    // last real user query (user messages that only carry tool responses do not count)
    long last_query = -1;
    for (size_t k = n; k-- > 0;) {
        if (role_of(msgs[k]) != "user") continue;
        const std::string c = trim(content[k]);
        if (!(starts_with(c, "<tool_response>") && ends_with(c, "</tool_response>"))) { last_query = (long)k; break; }
    }
    if (last_query < 0) { err = "no user query found in messages"; return false; }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const json::Value& m = msgs[i];
        const std::string role = role_of(m);
        const std::string c = trim(content[i]);
        if (role == "system") {
            if (i == 0) continue;
            out += "<|im_start|>system\n" + c + "<|im_end|>\n";   // relaxation: the template raises here
        } else if (role == "user") {
            out += "<|im_start|>user\n" + c + "<|im_end|>\n";
        } else if (role == "assistant") {
            std::string rc = m["reasoning_content"].is_string() ? m["reasoning_content"].as_string()
                                                                : m["reasoning"].as_string();
            rc = trim(rc);
            if (opt.preserve_thinking || (long)i > last_query) out += "<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n" + rc + "\n</think>\n\n" + c;
            else out += "<|im_start|>assistant\n" + c;
            const json::Value& tcs = m["tool_calls"];
            if (tcs.is_array()) {
                for (size_t j = 0; j < tcs.items().size(); j++) {
                    const json::Value& tc0 = tcs.items()[j];
                    const json::Value& tc = tc0.has("function") ? tc0["function"] : tc0;
                    const std::string name = tc["name"].as_string();
                    if (j == 0) out += (trim(c).empty() ? "" : "\n\n") + std::string("<tool_call>\n<function=") + name + ">\n";
                    else out += "\n<tool_call>\n<function=" + name + ">\n";
                    json::Value args = tc["arguments"];
                    if (args.is_string()) {
                        if (args.as_string().empty()) args = json::Value();
                        else {
                            json::Value parsed;
                            if (!json::Value::parse(args.as_string(), parsed) || !parsed.is_object()) {
                                err = "tool call arguments of '" + name + "' are not a JSON object";
                                return false;
                            }
                            args = parsed;
                        }
                    }
                    if (args.is_object())
                        for (const auto& kv : args.members())
                            out += "<parameter=" + kv.first + ">\n" +
                                   (kv.second.is_string() ? kv.second.as_string() : kv.second.dump_py()) + "\n</parameter>\n";
                    out += "</function>\n</tool_call>";
                }
            }
            out += "<|im_end|>\n";
        } else {   // tool
            if (i > 0 && role_of(msgs[i - 1]) != "tool") out += "<|im_start|>user";
            out += "\n<tool_response>\n" + c + "\n</tool_response>";
            if (i + 1 == n || role_of(msgs[i + 1]) != "tool") out += "<|im_end|>\n";
        }
    }
    if (opt.add_generation_prompt)
        out += opt.enable_thinking ? "<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n" : "<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n";
    return true;
}

std::string tool_call_opening(const std::string& name) { return "<tool_call>\n<function=" + name + ">\n"; }

void parse_tool_calls(const std::string& text, const json::Value& tools, std::string& content, std::vector<ToolCall>& calls) {
    calls.clear();
    size_t p = text.find("<tool_call>");
    if (p == std::string::npos) { content = text; return; }
    content = rstrip(text.substr(0, p));
    std::string tail;
    while (p != std::string::npos) {
        const size_t q = p + 11;
        const size_t end = text.find("</tool_call>", q);
        ToolCall call;
        if (parse_one(text.substr(q, end == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : end - q), tools, call))
            calls.push_back(std::move(call));
        if (end == std::string::npos) break;
        const size_t nx = text.find("<tool_call>", end + 12);
        if (nx == std::string::npos) tail = text.substr(end + 12);
        p = nx;
    }
    const std::string t = trim(tail);
    if (!t.empty()) content += (content.empty() ? "" : "\n\n") + t;   // text after the calls (the format forbids it)
}

void ThinkSplitter::feed(const std::string& text, std::string& reasoning, std::string& content) {
    static const std::string marker = "</think>";
    held_ += text;
    if (thinking_) {
        const size_t at = held_.find(marker);
        if (at == std::string::npos) {
            size_t cut = held_.size();   // hold back a suffix that is a prefix of the marker
            for (size_t k = std::min(held_.size(), marker.size() - 1); k > 0; k--)
                if (marker.compare(0, k, held_, held_.size() - k, k) == 0) { cut = held_.size() - k; break; }
            reasoning += held_.substr(0, cut);
            held_.erase(0, cut);
            return;
        }
        reasoning += held_.substr(0, at);
        held_.erase(0, at + marker.size());
        thinking_ = false;
    }
    if (!content_started_) {
        size_t i = 0;
        while (i < held_.size() && held_[i] == '\n') i++;
        held_.erase(0, i);
        if (held_.empty()) return;
        content_started_ = true;
    }
    content += held_;
    held_.clear();
}

void ThinkSplitter::finish(std::string& reasoning, std::string& content) {
    if (thinking_) reasoning += held_; else content += held_;
    held_.clear();
}

std::string ToolCallStreamer::feed(const std::string& text) {
    static const std::string marker = "<tool_call>";
    if (in_call_) { call_ += text; return ""; }
    held_ += text;
    const size_t at = held_.find(marker);
    if (at != std::string::npos) {
        const std::string out = rstrip(held_.substr(0, at));   // the separator before the call is not content
        call_ = held_.substr(at);
        held_.clear();
        in_call_ = true;
        return out;
    }
    size_t cut = held_.size();
    for (size_t k = std::min(held_.size(), marker.size() - 1); k > 0; k--)
        if (marker.compare(0, k, held_, held_.size() - k, k) == 0) { cut = held_.size() - k; break; }
    while (cut > 0 && (held_[cut - 1] == ' ' || held_[cut - 1] == '\n' || held_[cut - 1] == '\t' || held_[cut - 1] == '\r'))
        cut--;
    const std::string out = held_.substr(0, cut);
    held_.erase(0, cut);
    return out;
}

std::string ToolCallStreamer::finish() {
    if (in_call_) return "";
    std::string out = held_;
    held_.clear();
    return out;
}

} // namespace chat
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/server/json.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file json.h
/// Small, strict JSON value with a recursive-descent parser (RFC 8259: escapes incl. \uXXXX surrogate pairs ->
/// UTF-8, numbers, nesting depth limit) and two writers: compact (API responses) and Python-style (what the model's
/// chat template produces with `tojson`: ", " / ": " separators, non-ASCII kept, Python float repr). Objects keep
/// their members in insertion order (the chat template renders tool schemas in the client's key order).

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace q38rt {
namespace json {

class Value {
public:
    enum class Type { Null, Bool, Number, String, Array, Object };

    Value() = default;
    Value(std::nullptr_t) {}
    Value(bool b) : type_(Type::Bool), b_(b) {}
    Value(int v) : type_(Type::Number), n_(v), int_(true) {}
    Value(int64_t v) : type_(Type::Number), n_((double)v), int_(true) {}
    Value(double v) : type_(Type::Number), n_(v) {}
    Value(const char* s) : type_(Type::String), s_(s) {}
    Value(std::string s) : type_(Type::String), s_(std::move(s)) {}

    static Value array() { Value v; v.type_ = Type::Array; return v; }
    static Value object() { Value v; v.type_ = Type::Object; return v; }

    Type type() const { return type_; }
    bool is_null() const { return type_ == Type::Null; }
    bool is_bool() const { return type_ == Type::Bool; }
    bool is_number() const { return type_ == Type::Number; }
    bool is_string() const { return type_ == Type::String; }
    bool is_array() const { return type_ == Type::Array; }
    bool is_object() const { return type_ == Type::Object; }

    bool as_bool(bool def = false) const { return type_ == Type::Bool ? b_ : def; }
    double as_number(double def = 0) const { return type_ == Type::Number ? n_ : def; }
    const std::string& as_string() const { static const std::string empty; return type_ == Type::String ? s_ : empty; }
    bool is_integer() const { return type_ == Type::Number && int_; }   // integer literal / constructed from an int
    const std::vector<Value>& items() const { return a_; }
    using Members = std::vector<std::pair<std::string, Value>>;
    const Members& members() const { return o_; }   // insertion order

    /// Object member (null value if missing or not an object).
    const Value& operator[](const std::string& key) const;
    bool has(const std::string& key) const;
    /// Set (replace in place, or append) an object member.
    Value& set(const std::string& key, Value v);
    Value& push(Value v) { type_ = Type::Array; a_.push_back(std::move(v)); return a_.back(); }

    /// Compact JSON.
    std::string dump() const;
    /// Python json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False) formatting.
    std::string dump_py() const;

    /// Parse a complete JSON text. Returns false (with a message) on any syntax error or trailing garbage.
    static bool parse(const std::string& text, Value& out, std::string* error = nullptr);

private:
    Type type_ = Type::Null;
    bool b_ = false;
    double n_ = 0;
    std::string s_;
    bool int_ = false;
    std::vector<Value> a_;
    Members o_;
    void dump_to(std::string& out, bool py) const;
    friend struct Parser;
};

/// JSON string literal (with quotes) for s.
