    LayerWeights& w = L_[n_trunk_];
    const float eps = config().rms_eps;
    const size_t erb = quant_row_bytes(tok_embd_->quant, D);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++)
        dequantize_row_ref(tok_embd_->quant, gguf_.tensor_data(*tok_embd_) + (size_t)toks[i] * erb, h_x + (size_t)i * D, D);
    cuda::copy_floats(d_x, h_x, m * D, st_);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        const int q = q0 + i;
        float* dst = d_y + (size_t)i * D;
        if (!h_from_ring) CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(dst, h_first, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
        else if (q == 0) CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemsetAsync(dst, 0, D * 4, st_));
        else CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(dst, d_hring_ + (size_t)((q - 1) % HRING) * D, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
    }
    // [enorm(e) | hnorm(h)] -> d_g rows of 2*D, quantized for the GPU (act_h_ rows of 2*D) and copied for the CPU
    cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows(d_x, nullptr, nullptr, w.enorm, D, m, eps, d_xn, nullptr, st_);
    cuda::copy_rows(d_g, 2 * D, d_xn, D, D, m, st_);
    cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows(d_y, nullptr, nullptr, w.hnorm, D, m, eps, d_xn, nullptr, st_);
    cuda::copy_rows(d_g + D, 2 * D, d_xn, D, D, m, st_);
    cuda::quantize_act_q8(d_g, 2 * D, m, act_h_, st_);
    cuda::copy_floats(h_g, d_g, m * 2 * D, st_);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_, st_));
    begin_gpu_group();
    gemv_gpu(w.eh_proj, act_h_, d_x, m, 2 * D, D);
    mark_gpu_group();
    wait_event(W_MTP);
    quantize_rows_q8_K(h_g, h_xq, 2 * D, m);
    CpuJob job;
    job.add(w.eh_proj, h_xq, h_rows, true, m, 2 * D / QK_K, w.eh_proj.cpu2_n());
    RUN_CPU_JOB(job);
    if (w.eh_proj.cpu2_n() > 0)
        cuda::copy_rows(d_x + w.eh_proj.gpu_part, D, h_rows, w.eh_proj.cpu2_n(), w.eh_proj.cpu2_n(), m, st_);
    // decoder block on the MTP KV cache
    cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows2(d_x, cuda::RowSrc{}, cuda::RowSrc{}, w.attn_norm, D, m, eps, d_xn, nullptr, &act_x_, st_, h_xq);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_, st_));
    layer_attention(n_trunk_, m, q0);
    cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows2(d_x, y_src(), cuda::RowSrc{}, w.post_norm, D, m, eps, d_xn, nullptr, &act_x_, st_, h_xq);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_, st_));
    layer_ffn(n_trunk_, m);
    cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows2(d_x, cuda::RowSrc::device(d_y, D), y2_src(), w.head_norm, D, m, eps, d_xn, nullptr, &act_x_, st_);
    if (draft) {
        const int last = m - 1;
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_hmtp_, d_xn + (size_t)last * D, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
        const int nd = std::min(cfg_.draft_rows, lm_.gpu_part);
        const cuda::ActQ8 a = act_row(act_x_, D, last);
        cuda::gemv_q8n(lm_.type, lm_.gpu, lm_.gpu_rb, D, nd, a, 1, D, d_logits, VOCAB, st_);
        cuda::topk(d_logits, nd, 1, 0, d_tkv, d_tki, d_topk_work, st_);
        cuda::copy_floats((float*)h_tki, (const float*)d_tki, 1, st_);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
        *draft = h_tki[0];
    }
}

void Engine::mtp_catchup() {
    if (!has_mtp_) return;
    discard_predraft();
    if (pos_ - mtp_pos_ + 1 > HRING) { LOG_ERROR("MTP fell %d positions behind (ring %d)", pos_ - mtp_pos_, HRING); std::abort(); }
    while (mtp_pos_ < pos_) {
        const int m = std::min(MAX_BATCH, pos_ - mtp_pos_);
        int32_t toks[MAX_BATCH];
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) toks[i] = tok_ring_[(mtp_pos_ + i) % HRING];
        mtp_rows(mtp_pos_, m, toks, nullptr, true, nullptr);
        mtp_pos_ += m;
    }
}

void Engine::mtp_draft(int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts) {
    if (!has_mtp_ || k < 1) return;
    if (spec_open_) { LOG_ERROR("mtp_draft: speculative pass not committed"); std::abort(); }
    discard_predraft();
    mtp_draft_at(pos_, next, k, drafts);
}

void Engine::discard_predraft() {
    if (!pre_.valid) return;
    pre_.valid = false;
    mtp_pos_ = std::min(mtp_pos_, pre_.old_mtp_pos);   // rows from there on are recomputed by the next catch-up
}

bool Engine::take_drafts(int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts) {
    if (!pre_.valid) return false;
    if (spec_open_ || pre_.pos != pos_ || pre_.next != next || k > pre_.k) {
        // The catch-up rows below both the committed position and the guessed draft row hold committed tokens and
        // stay valid; the draft row and the chained rows are recomputed by the next drafting.
        pre_.valid = false;
        if (!spec_open_) mtp_pos_ = std::min(mtp_pos_, std::max(pre_.old_mtp_pos, std::min(pos_, pre_.pos)));
        else mtp_pos_ = std::min(mtp_pos_, pre_.old_mtp_pos);
        stats_.predraft_misses++;
        return false;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) drafts[i] = pre_.d[i];
    pre_.valid = false;   // mtp_pos_ already includes the draft row (pos_ + 1)
    return true;
}

// mtp_draft for the token `next` at position pos (>= position(): the overlapped drafting runs before the commit)
void Engine::mtp_draft_at(int pos, int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts) {
    const int pos_saved = pos_;
    pos_ = pos;
    struct Restore { int& p; int v; ~Restore() { p = v; } } restore{pos_, pos_saved};
    if (pos_ - mtp_pos_ + 1 > HRING) { LOG_ERROR("MTP fell %d positions behind (ring %d)", pos_ - mtp_pos_, HRING); std::abort(); }
    // known pairs first if they do not fit one pass with the draft row
    while (pos_ - mtp_pos_ + 1 > MAX_BATCH) {
        int32_t toks[MAX_BATCH];
        for (int i = 0; i < MAX_BATCH; i++) toks[i] = tok_ring_[(mtp_pos_ + i) % HRING];
        mtp_rows(mtp_pos_, MAX_BATCH, toks, nullptr, true, nullptr);
        mtp_pos_ += MAX_BATCH;
    }
    // catch-up rows + the draft row q = pos_ (token `next`) in one pass
    const int m = pos_ - mtp_pos_ + 1;
    int32_t toks[MAX_BATCH];
    for (int i = 0; i < m - 1; i++) toks[i] = tok_ring_[(mtp_pos_ + i) % HRING];
    toks[m - 1] = next;
    mtp_rows(mtp_pos_, m, toks, nullptr, true, &drafts[0]);
    mtp_pos_ = pos_ + 1;
    // chained steps on the MTP's own state; their KV rows are tentative (rewritten by the next catch-up)
    for (int i = 1; i < k; i++) mtp_rows(pos_ + i, 1, &drafts[i - 1], d_hmtp_, false, &drafts[i]);
}

void Engine::commit(int m) {
    if (!spec_open_ || m < 1 || m > spec_n_) { LOG_ERROR("commit(%d) without a matching speculative pass", m); std::abort(); }
    spec_open_ = false;
    dn_pending_ = m;
    pos_ += m;
}

void Engine::flush_pending() {
    if (spec_open_) { LOG_ERROR("flush_pending: speculative pass not committed"); std::abort(); }
    if (dn_pending_ == 0) return;
    const int m = dn_pending_;
    for (size_t il = 0; il < L_.size(); il++) {
        if (L_[il].full) continue;
        cuda::DNTokens in;
        const float* sv = dn_save(dn_sb_, (int)il);
        in.sqkv = sv;
        in.sbeta = sv + (size_t)MAX_BATCH * DN_CH;
        in.salpha = in.sbeta + MAX_BATCH * 64;
        cuda::deltanet_multi(L_[il].dn, conv_cur_, in, m, 0, m, config().rms_eps, nullptr, nullptr, st_);
    }
    conv_cur_ ^= 1;
    dn_pending_ = 0;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Batched prefill
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

void Engine::prefill_free() {
    if (pb_.base) cudaFree(pb_.base);
    for (float* h : pb_.h_emb) if (h) cudaFreeHost(h);
    if (pb_.borrowed)   // the resident LM-head rows held prefill buffers: restore them (stream-ordered after prefill)
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(lm_.gpu, h_lm_gpu_copy_, (size_t)lm_.gpu_part * lm_.gpu_rb, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    pb_ = PB();
    emb_pending_[0] = emb_pending_[1] = false;
}

// cudaMemGetInfo overstates what can be allocated (measured: 116 MiB reported free, largest allocation 76 MiB) and cuBLAS
// loads its kernels lazily into device memory (a prefill GEMM failed with CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR when its buffers
// left the reported 16 MiB margin but not the real one): check that `bytes` more can really be allocated.
static bool vram_headroom(size_t bytes) {
    void* p = nullptr;
    if (cudaMalloc(&p, bytes) != cudaSuccess) { (void)cudaGetLastError(); return false; }
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaFree(p));
    return true;
}

bool Engine::scratch_acquire(const std::vector<size_t>& sizes, std::vector<void*>& out) {
    scratch_release();
    out.assign(sizes.size(), nullptr);
    size_t total = 0;
    for (size_t x : sizes) total += align_up(x, 256);
    const size_t lm_bytes = (h_lm_gpu_copy_ && !lm_.col_split) ? (size_t)lm_.gpu_part * lm_.gpu_rb : 0;
    const size_t margin = 16u << 20;
    size_t free_b, total_b;
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_b, &total_b));
    // largest buffers into the LM-head rows when free VRAM alone does not hold everything (as prefill_alloc); second
    // attempt with them when the cudaMalloc or the headroom check fails
    for (int borrow = 0; borrow < 2; borrow++) {
        std::vector<bool> in_lm(sizes.size(), false);
        size_t malloc_bytes = total;
        if ((borrow || total + margin > free_b) && lm_bytes > 0) {
            std::vector<size_t> order(sizes.size());
            for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); i++) order[i] = i;
            std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [&](size_t a, size_t b) { return sizes[a] > sizes[b]; });
            size_t left = lm_bytes;
            for (size_t i : order) {
                const size_t a = align_up(sizes[i], 256);
                if (sizes[i] && a <= left) { in_lm[i] = true; left -= a; malloc_bytes -= a; }
            }
        }
        if (malloc_bytes + margin > free_b) continue;
        if (malloc_bytes && cudaMalloc(&scratch_base_, malloc_bytes) != cudaSuccess) {
            (void)cudaGetLastError();
            scratch_base_ = nullptr;
            continue;
        }
        if (!vram_headroom(margin)) {
            if (scratch_base_) CHECK_CUDA(cudaFree(scratch_base_));
            scratch_base_ = nullptr;
            continue;
        }
        uint8_t* lc = (uint8_t*)lm_.gpu;
        uint8_t* mc = scratch_base_;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < sizes.size(); i++) {
            if (!sizes[i]) continue;
            uint8_t*& c = in_lm[i] ? lc : mc;
            out[i] = c;
            c += align_up(sizes[i], 256);
        }
        scratch_borrowed_ = malloc_bytes < total;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void Engine::scratch_release() {
    if (scratch_base_) {
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
        cudaFree(scratch_base_);
    }
    scratch_base_ = nullptr;
    if (scratch_borrowed_)   // stream-ordered after the work that used the rows
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(lm_.gpu, h_lm_gpu_copy_, (size_t)lm_.gpu_part * lm_.gpu_rb, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    scratch_borrowed_ = false;
}

bool Engine::prefill_alloc(int B, size_t stage, bool borrow) {
    if (pb_.B == B && pb_.stage_bytes == stage) return true;
    prefill_free();
    const size_t Bz = (size_t)B;
    // X, Y, Xn16 live through the whole chunk. One region reused by the phases of each layer (attention or DeltaNet, then FFN) and by the MTP block at the
    // chunk end: 136 KB per token instead of 330 KB as separate buffers, so 1024-token chunks fit (dequant and
    // host-weight streaming are paid once per chunk). Offsets are within the region.
    struct Sub { size_t off = 0; size_t take(size_t bytes) { size_t o = off; off += align_up(bytes, 256); return o; } };
    Sub at, dn, ff, mt;
    // attention: q|gate projection (dead after attn_prep: its slot then holds O16), K, V, roped q, gate
    const size_t aQKV = at.take(Bz * A_Q * 4), aK = at.take(Bz * A_KV * 4), aV = at.take(Bz * A_KV * 4),
                 aQ = at.take(Bz * A_O * 4), aGate = at.take(Bz * A_O * 4);
    // DeltaNet: qkv (dead after the conv kernels: its slot then holds the core output and O16), z, beta, alpha, work
    const size_t dQKV = dn.take(Bz * A_Q * 4), dZ = dn.take(Bz * DN_V * 4), dBeta = dn.take(Bz * 64 * 4),
                 dAlpha = dn.take(Bz * 64 * 4), dWork = dn.take(cuda::dn_work_bytes(B));
    static_assert(DN_CH <= A_Q && DN_V * 4 + A_O * 2 <= A_Q * 4, "qkv slot holds DeltaNet core output + O16");
    const size_t sO16 = Bz * DN_V * 4;   // O16 after the core output inside the qkv slot
    // FFN: gate, up (SwiGLU writes the FP16 rows in place over gate)
    const size_t fG = ff.take(Bz * FF * 4), fU = ff.take(Bz * FF * 4);
    // MTP block: output_norm(x), h_{q-1} rows, [enorm(e)|hnorm(h)] FP16, embeddings, K, V, q, gate
    const size_t mHn = mt.take(Bz * D * 4), mHs = mt.take(Bz * D * 4), mH16 = mt.take(Bz * 2 * D * 2),
                 mE = mt.take(Bz * D * 4), mK = mt.take(Bz * A_KV * 4), mV = mt.take(Bz * A_KV * 4),
                 mQ = mt.take(Bz * A_O * 4), mGate = mt.take(Bz * A_O * 4);
    // attn_prep_rows reads a (stale, unused) q|gate input of A_Q floats per row from mHs
    const size_t region = std::max({at.off, dn.off, ff.off, has_mtp_ ? std::max(mt.off, mHs + Bz * A_Q * 4) : 0});
    // Placement: everything in one cudaMalloc when the free VRAM fits it (+ margin for lazily loaded cuBLAS kernels);
    // otherwise the largest buffers go into the resident LM-head rows (unused during the prefill layers; restored from
    // the pinned host copy by prefill_free, ~30 ms) and the rest into the cudaMalloc. This lets decode keep that VRAM
    // for weights instead of a reserve that only prefill used.
    const bool ring = d_slots_ != nullptr;   // FP16 dequant scratch = the decode streaming ring (64 MB, idle now)
    const size_t wf16_elems = ring ? (size_t)NSLOT * SLOT_BYTES / 2 : (16u << 20);
    const size_t ws = 4u << 20;              // cuBLAS workspace
    enum { BR, BX, BY, BXN, BS0, BS1, BW, BWS, BROPE, NBUF };
    const size_t sz[NBUF] = {region, Bz * D * 4, Bz * D * 4, Bz * D * 2, stage, stage, ring ? 0 : wf16_elems * 2, ws,
                             Bz * 64 * 4 + Bz * 3 * 4};
    const size_t margin = 16u << 20;
    size_t free_b, total_b;
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_b, &total_b));
    size_t total = 0;
    for (size_t x : sz) total += align_up(x, 256);
    const size_t lm_bytes = (h_lm_gpu_copy_ && !lm_.col_split) ? (size_t)lm_.gpu_part * lm_.gpu_rb : 0;
    bool in_lm[NBUF] = {};
    size_t malloc_bytes = total;
    static const bool force_borrow = getenv("Q38_PREFILL_BORROW") != nullptr;   // test knob (integration tests)
    if ((total + margin > free_b || borrow || force_borrow) && lm_bytes > 0) {
        size_t left = lm_bytes;
        for (int i : {BR, BX, BY, BS0, BS1, BW, BXN, BWS, BROPE}) {
            const size_t a = align_up(sz[i], 256);
            if (sz[i] && a <= left) { in_lm[i] = true; left -= a; malloc_bytes -= a; }
        }
    }
    if (cfg_.verbose)
        LOG_INFO("prefill buffers B=%d: %.0f MiB (%.0f MiB in the LM-head rows), %.0f MiB free", B, total / 1048576.0,
                 (total - malloc_bytes) / 1048576.0, free_b / 1048576.0);
    if (malloc_bytes + margin > free_b) return false;
    if (malloc_bytes) {
        const cudaError_t e = cudaMalloc(&pb_.base, malloc_bytes);
        if (e != cudaSuccess) {
            (void)cudaGetLastError();
            if (cfg_.verbose) LOG_INFO("prefill buffers B=%d: cudaMalloc of %.0f MiB failed (%s)", B, malloc_bytes / 1048576.0, cudaGetErrorString(e));
            pb_.base = nullptr;
            return false;
        }
        if (!vram_headroom(margin)) {   // the reported free VRAM was not all allocatable
            if (cfg_.verbose) LOG_INFO("prefill buffers B=%d: less than %.0f MiB left after them", B, margin / 1048576.0);
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaFree(pb_.base));
            pb_.base = nullptr;
            return false;
        }
    }
    uint8_t* ptr[NBUF] = {};
    {
        uint8_t* lc = (uint8_t*)lm_.gpu;
        uint8_t* mc = pb_.base;
        for (int i = 0; i < NBUF; i++) {
            if (!sz[i]) continue;
            uint8_t*& c = in_lm[i] ? lc : mc;
            ptr[i] = c;
            c += align_up(sz[i], 256);
        }
    }
    pb_.borrowed = malloc_bytes < total;
    uint8_t* r = ptr[BR];
    pb_.B = B;
    pb_.X = (float*)ptr[BX]; pb_.Y = (float*)ptr[BY]; pb_.Xn16 = (__half*)ptr[BXN];
    pb_.qkv = (float*)(r + aQKV); pb_.k = (float*)(r + aK); pb_.v = (float*)(r + aV); pb_.q = (float*)(r + aQ);
    pb_.gate = (float*)(r + aGate);
    pb_.z = (float*)(r + dZ); pb_.beta = (float*)(r + dBeta); pb_.alpha = (float*)(r + dAlpha); pb_.dn_work = r + dWork;
    pb_.dn_out = (float*)(r + dQKV);
    pb_.O16 = (__half*)(r + dQKV + sO16);
    pb_.G = (float*)(r + fG); pb_.U = (float*)(r + fU); pb_.H16 = (__half*)pb_.G;
    pb_.mHn = (float*)(r + mHn); pb_.mHs = (float*)(r + mHs); pb_.mH16 = (__half*)(r + mH16); pb_.mE = (float*)(r + mE);
    pb_.mk = (float*)(r + mK); pb_.mv = (float*)(r + mV); pb_.mq = (float*)(r + mQ); pb_.mgate = (float*)(r + mGate);
    pb_.Wf16 = ring ? (__half*)d_slots_ : (__half*)ptr[BW]; pb_.stage[0] = ptr[BS0]; pb_.stage[1] = ptr[BS1];
    pb_.cublas_ws = ptr[BWS];
    pb_.rope = (float*)ptr[BROPE];
    pb_.rope_pos = (int*)(pb_.rope + Bz * 64);
    pb_.wf16_elems = wf16_elems; pb_.stage_bytes = stage;
    for (float*& h : pb_.h_emb) CHECK_CUDA(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h, Bz * D * 4, cudaHostAllocDefault));
    if (!cuda::cublas_bind(cublas_, st_, pb_.cublas_ws, ws)) { LOG_ERROR("cuBLAS setup failed"); exit(1); }
    if (!ev_copied_[0]) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ev_copied_[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ev_used_[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ev_emb_[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));
        }
    }
    slot_used_[0] = slot_used_[1] = false;
    build_pieces();
    return true;
}

// Row-block size for the dequant + cuBLAS GEMM loop: multiples of 512 rows run at 30-45 TFLOPS on this GPU, odd
// sizes (e.g. 963, 3276) at 11-18 (tile quantization; measured).
static int gemm_rows(int rows, size_t fit) {
    int r = (int)std::min<size_t>((size_t)rows, fit);
    if (r >= 512) r -= r % 512;
    return std::max(r, 1);
}

// Order must match prefill_batched / gemm_split exactly.
void Engine::build_pieces() {
    pieces_.clear();
    auto add = [&](const SplitMat& m) {
        if (m.cpu_n() <= 0 || m.cpu_k() <= 0) return;
        int per = (int)std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(m.cpu_n(), pb_.stage_bytes / m.cpu_rb));
        const int R = gemm_rows(m.cpu_n(), pb_.wf16_elems / m.cpu_k());
        if (per >= R && per < m.cpu_n()) per -= per % R;   // whole GEMM blocks per staged piece
        for (int r0 = 0; r0 < m.cpu_n(); r0 += per) pieces_.push_back({&m, r0, std::min(per, m.cpu_n() - r0)});
    };
    for (int il = 0; il < n_trunk_; il++) {
        const LayerWeights& w = L_[il];
        if (w.full) { add(w.q); add(w.k); add(w.v); add(w.o); }
        else { add(w.qkv); add(w.z); add(w.ssm_out); }
        add(w.gate); add(w.up); add(w.down);
    }
    if (has_mtp_) {   // MTP prefill only fills the MTP KV cache: eh_proj, then the K and V projections
        const LayerWeights& m = L_[n_trunk_];
        add(m.eh_proj); add(m.k); add(m.v);
    }
}

// MTP block over a prefill chunk (positions p0..p0+nb-1): only what its KV cache needs — pairs (h_{q-1}, x_q) ->
// eh_proj -> attn_norm -> K/V (RoPE, norms) appended at q. Also refreshes the h/token rings for the decode-time
// MTP passes that follow.
void Engine::prefill_mtp(const std::vector<int32_t>& tokens, int c0, int nb, int p0, const float* h_emb) {
    const LayerWeights& m = L_[n_trunk_];
    const ModelConfig& c = config();
    const float eps = c.rms_eps;
    float* Hn = pb_.mHn;   // output_norm(x) of the chunk rows
    float* Hs = pb_.mHs;   // row t = h_{p0+t-1}
    float* E = pb_.mE;     // the chunk's token embeddings (host copy still holds them)
    __half* H16 = pb_.mH16;
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(E, h_emb, (size_t)nb * D * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    cuda::rmsnorm_rows(pb_.X, nullptr, nullptr, output_norm_, nb, D, eps, nullptr, Hn, st_);
    if (p0 > 0) CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(Hs, d_hring_ + (size_t)((p0 - 1) % HRING) * D, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
    else CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemsetAsync(Hs, 0, D * 4, st_));
    if (nb > 1) CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(Hs + D, Hn, (size_t)(nb - 1) * D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
    // [enorm(e) | hnorm(h)] as fp16 rows of 2*D
    cuda::rmsnorm_rows(E, nullptr, nullptr, m.enorm, nb, D, eps, pb_.Xn16, nullptr, st_);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(H16, 2 * D * 2, pb_.Xn16, D * 2, D * 2, nb, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
    cuda::rmsnorm_rows(Hs, nullptr, nullptr, m.hnorm, nb, D, eps, pb_.Xn16, nullptr, st_);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(H16 + D, 2 * D * 2, pb_.Xn16, D * 2, D * 2, nb, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
    gemm_split(m.eh_proj, H16, 2 * D, nb, pb_.Y, D);
    cuda::rmsnorm_rows(pb_.Y, nullptr, nullptr, m.attn_norm, nb, D, eps, pb_.Xn16, nullptr, st_);
    gemm_split(m.k, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.mk, A_KV);
    gemm_split(m.v, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.mv, A_KV);
    // Q is not needed for the cache: attn_prep_rows gets a stale Q buffer (its q/gate outputs are unused)
    cuda::attn_prep_rows(Hs, pb_.mk, pb_.mv, m.q_norm, m.k_norm, nb, p0, pb_.rope, eps, cfg_.kv_type,
                         const_cast<cuda::KVLayer&>(m.kv), pb_.mq, pb_.mgate, st_);
    const int keep = std::min(nb, HRING);
    for (int t = nb - keep; t < nb; t++) {
        const int q = p0 + t;
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_hring_ + (size_t)(q % HRING) * D, Hn + (size_t)t * D, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, st_));
        tok_ring_[q % HRING] = tokens[c0 + t];
    }
    mtp_pos_ = p0 + nb;
}

void Engine::issue_piece(size_t i) {
    const Piece& pc = pieces_[i % pieces_.size()];
    const int slot = (int)(i & 1);
    if (slot_used_[slot]) CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamWaitEvent(cst_, ev_used_[slot], 0));   // slot free?
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(pb_.stage[slot], pc.m->cpu + (size_t)pc.r0 * pc.m->cpu_rb, (size_t)pc.n * pc.m->cpu_rb,
                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, cst_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_copied_[slot], cst_));
    pstats_.bytes_streamed += (double)pc.n * pc.m->cpu_rb;
}

// Y[:, cols] (+)= X . W^T for a split matrix: resident share dequantized from VRAM, host share streamed
// through the staging buffer. Row split -> output columns [0,gpu) and [gpu,N); column split (FFN down)
// -> two partial products over K accumulated into all N columns.
void Engine::pmark(int phase) {
    if (!pprof_) return;
    if (pp_n_ >= pp_ev_.size()) { pp_ev_.push_back(nullptr); CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreate(&pp_ev_.back())); pp_phase_.push_back(0); }
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(pp_ev_[pp_n_], st_));
    pp_phase_[pp_n_++] = phase;
}

void Engine::pflush() {
    if (!pprof_ || pp_n_ < 2) { pp_n_ = 0; return; }
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventSynchronize(pp_ev_[pp_n_ - 1]));
    for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < pp_n_; i++) {
        float ms = 0;
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, pp_ev_[i], pp_ev_[i + 1]));
        pp_ms_[pp_phase_[i]] += ms;
    }
    pp_n_ = 0;
}

void Engine::gemm_split(const SplitMat& m, const __half* X, int ldx, int B, float* Y, int ldy) {
    const size_t scratch = pb_.wf16_elems;
    if (m.gpu_n() > 0 && m.gpu_k() > 0) {
        const int K = m.gpu_k(), rows = m.gpu_n();
        const int R = gemm_rows(rows, scratch / K);
        for (int r0 = 0; r0 < rows; r0 += R) {
            const int n = std::min(R, rows - r0);
            pmark(PP_DEQ);
            cuda::dequant_rows_f16(m.type, (const uint8_t*)m.gpu + (size_t)r0 * m.gpu_rb, m.gpu_rb, K, n, pb_.Wf16, st_);
            pmark(PP_GEMM);
            cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, pb_.Wf16, n, K, X, ldx, B, Y + r0, ldy, false, st_);
            pmark(PP_OTHER);
        }
    }
    if (m.cpu_n() > 0 && m.cpu_k() > 0) {
        const int K = m.cpu_k();
        // consume this matrix's pieces from the staging ring (issued ahead on the copy stream)
        while (piece_next_ < pieces_.size() && pieces_[piece_next_].m == &m) {
            const size_t i = piece_next_++;
            const Piece& pc = pieces_[i];
            const int slot = (int)(i & 1);
            pmark(PP_WAIT);
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamWaitEvent(st_, ev_copied_[slot], 0));
            pmark(PP_OTHER);
            const int R = gemm_rows(pc.n, scratch / K);
            for (int s0 = 0; s0 < pc.n; s0 += R) {
                const int ns = std::min(R, pc.n - s0);
                pmark(PP_DEQ);
                cuda::dequant_rows_f16(m.type, pb_.stage[slot] + (size_t)s0 * m.cpu_rb, m.cpu_rb, K, ns, pb_.Wf16, st_);
                pmark(PP_GEMM);
                const int r = pc.r0 + s0;
                if (!m.col_split)
                    cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, pb_.Wf16, ns, K, X, ldx, B, Y + m.gpu_part + r, ldy, false, st_);
                else
                    cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, pb_.Wf16, ns, K, X + m.gpu_part, ldx, B, Y + r, ldy, m.gpu_part > 0, st_);
                pmark(PP_OTHER);
            }
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_used_[slot], st_));
            slot_used_[slot] = true;
            if (i + 2 < piece_issue_limit_) issue_piece(i + 2);
        }
    }
}

void Engine::gemm_bf16(const uint16_t* W, int N, const __half* X, int B, float* Y) {
    cuda::dequant_rows_f16(QuantFormat::BF16, W, (size_t)D * 2, D, N, pb_.Wf16, st_);
    cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, pb_.Wf16, N, D, X, D, B, Y, N, false, st_);
}

int Engine::rope_delta_at(int pos) const {
    int d = 0;
    for (const ImageSpan& s : images_)
        if (s.start + s.nx * s.ny <= pos) d += std::max(s.nx, s.ny) - s.nx * s.ny;
    return d;
}

bool Engine::rope_positions(int p0, int n, std::vector<int>& pos3) const {
    bool any = false;
    for (const ImageSpan& s : images_) any |= s.start < p0 + n && s.start + s.nx * s.ny > p0;
    if (!any) return false;
    pos3.resize((size_t)3 * n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const int q = p0 + i;
        int d = 0, t = -1, h = 0, w = 0;
        for (const ImageSpan& s : images_) {
            const int len = s.nx * s.ny;
            if (q >= s.start + len) { d += std::max(s.nx, s.ny) - len; continue; }
            if (q >= s.start) {
                const int p = s.start + d, k = q - s.start;
                t = p; h = p + k / s.nx; w = p + k % s.nx;
            }
            break;
        }
        if (t < 0) t = h = w = q + d;
        pos3[3 * i] = t; pos3[3 * i + 1] = h; pos3[3 * i + 2] = w;
    }
    return true;
}

void Engine::prefill_batched(const std::vector<int32_t>& tokens, DecodeResult* last, int topk,
                             const std::vector<int>& logit_rows, std::vector<std::vector<float>>* rows_out, int chunk,
                             const std::function<void(int)>& progress, const std::vector<PrefillImage>& images) {
    const double t_start = now_s();
    discard_predraft();
    const ModelConfig& c = config();
    const float eps = c.rms_eps;
    const int T = (int)tokens.size();
    if (T == 0) return;
    flush_pending();   // the batched DeltaNet kernels start from a fully applied state
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(cst_));   // decode-ring prefetches land in d_slots_ = the prefill dequant scratch
    pprof_ = getenv("Q38_PROFILE_PREFILL") != nullptr;
    for (double& v : pp_ms_) v = 0;
    if (has_mtp_) mtp_catchup();   // MTP prefill continues from position()
    if (pos_ + T > cfg_.max_context) { LOG_ERROR("prefill exceeds context (%d + %d > %d)", pos_, T, cfg_.max_context); return; }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < images.size(); i++) {
        const PrefillImage& im = images[i];
        const int prev_end = i ? images[i - 1].offset + images[i - 1].nx * images[i - 1].ny : 0;
        if (im.nx < 1 || im.ny < 1 || !im.emb || im.offset < prev_end || im.offset + im.nx * im.ny > T) {
            LOG_ERROR("prefill: invalid image span %zu (offset %d, %d x %d, %d tokens)", i, im.offset, im.nx, im.ny, T);
            return;
        }
    }
    for (const PrefillImage& im : images) {
        images_.push_back({pos_ + im.offset, im.nx, im.ny});
        rope_delta_ += std::max(im.nx, im.ny) - im.nx * im.ny;
    }
    // 2 x 24 MB staging ring for the streamed host weights. When the buffers do not fit (free VRAM moves with the
    // desktop's use, and a cudaMalloc can fail below the reported free size), first place them in the borrowed LM-head
    // rows: that keeps the piece size, hence the GEMM shapes, so the results do not depend on free VRAM (12 MB pieces
    // changed cuBLAS's kernels and the logits). Then halve the pieces, then the chunk.
    int B = std::min(chunk > 0 ? chunk : 1024, T);
    while (!prefill_alloc(B, 24u << 20) && !prefill_alloc(B, 24u << 20, true) && !prefill_alloc(B, 12u << 20, true)) {
        B /= 2;
        if (B < 8) { LOG_ERROR("not enough VRAM for prefill buffers"); return; }
    }
    if (rows_out) rows_out->clear();
    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::vector<float>>> saved;   // (prompt index, final hidden state) for logits
    const size_t erb = quant_row_bytes(tok_embd_->quant, D);
    size_t img = 0;          // first image not entirely before the current row
    std::vector<int> pos3;   // (t, h, w) of a chunk's rows when it holds image tokens
    for (int c0 = 0; c0 < T; c0 += B) {
        const int nb = std::min(B, T - c0);
        const int p0 = pos_;
        const int par = (c0 / B) & 1;
        float* h_emb = pb_.h_emb[par];
        if (emb_pending_[par]) CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventSynchronize(ev_emb_[par]));   // chunk c-2's copies are done
        if (progress && c0 >= 2 * B) progress(c0 - B);   // ... and so is chunk c-2 (ev_emb_ is its last operation)
        for (int t = 0; t < nb; t++) {
            const int gi = c0 + t;
            while (img < images.size() && images[img].offset + images[img].nx * images[img].ny <= gi) img++;
            if (img < images.size() && gi >= images[img].offset)
                memcpy(h_emb + (size_t)t * D, images[img].emb + (size_t)(gi - images[img].offset) * D, D * 4);
            else
                dequantize_row_ref(tok_embd_->quant, gguf_.tensor_data(*tok_embd_) + (size_t)tokens[gi] * erb,
                                   h_emb + (size_t)t * D, D);
        }
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(pb_.X, h_emb, (size_t)nb * D * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
        // shared by the attention layers and the MTP block
        if (rope_positions(p0, nb, pos3)) {   // pageable copy: pos3 is staged before the call returns
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(pb_.rope_pos, pos3.data(), pos3.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
            cuda::rope_table_pos(pb_.rope_pos, nb, c.rope_theta, pb_.rope, st_);
        } else {
            cuda::rope_table(p0 + rope_delta_at(p0), nb, c.rope_theta, pb_.rope, st_);
        }
        // start streaming this chunk's host-weight pieces (2 in flight)
        piece_next_ = 0;
        piece_issue_limit_ = pieces_.size();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min<size_t>(2, pieces_.size()); i++) issue_piece(i);
        const float* pending = nullptr;
        for (int il = 0; il < c.num_layers; il++) {
            LayerWeights& w = L_[il];
            cuda::rmsnorm_rows(pb_.X, pending, nullptr, w.attn_norm, nb, D, eps, pb_.Xn16, nullptr, st_);
            if (w.full) {
                gemm_split(w.q, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.qkv, A_Q);
                gemm_split(w.k, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.k, A_KV);
                gemm_split(w.v, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.v, A_KV);
                cuda::attn_prep_rows(pb_.qkv, pb_.k, pb_.v, w.q_norm, w.k_norm, nb, p0, pb_.rope, eps, cfg_.kv_type,
                                     w.kv, pb_.q, pb_.gate, st_);
                pmark(PP_ATTN);
                cuda::attn_prefill(pb_.q, pb_.gate, w.kv, cfg_.kv_type, nb, p0, pb_.O16, st_);
                pmark(PP_OTHER);
                gemm_split(w.o, pb_.O16, A_O, nb, pb_.Y, D);
            } else {
                gemm_split(w.qkv, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.qkv, DN_CH);
                gemm_split(w.z, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.z, DN_V);
                gemm_bf16(w.beta, DN_VH, pb_.Xn16, nb, pb_.beta);
                gemm_bf16(w.alpha, DN_VH, pb_.Xn16, nb, pb_.alpha);
                pmark(PP_DN);
                cuda::deltanet_rows(w.dn, pb_.qkv, pb_.z, pb_.beta, pb_.alpha, nb, conv_cur_, eps, pb_.dn_work, pb_.dn_out,
                                    pb_.O16, st_);
                pmark(PP_OTHER);
                gemm_split(w.ssm_out, pb_.O16, DN_V, nb, pb_.Y, D);
            }
            cuda::rmsnorm_rows(pb_.X, pb_.Y, nullptr, w.post_norm, nb, D, eps, pb_.Xn16, nullptr, st_);
            gemm_split(w.gate, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.G, FF);
            gemm_split(w.up, pb_.Xn16, D, nb, pb_.U, FF);
            cuda::swiglu_rows_inplace(pb_.G, pb_.U, nb, FF, FF, st_);
            gemm_split(w.down, pb_.H16, 2 * FF, nb, pb_.Y, D);
            pending = pb_.Y;
        }
        // residual of the last FFN; keep pre-norm hidden states in X
        cuda::rmsnorm_rows(pb_.X, pb_.Y, nullptr, output_norm_, nb, D, eps, nullptr, nullptr, st_);
        conv_cur_ ^= 1;   // every DeltaNet layer wrote its conv window to the other buffer
        if (has_mtp_) prefill_mtp(tokens, c0, nb, p0, h_emb);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_emb_[par], st_));
        emb_pending_[par] = true;
        pmark(PP_OTHER);
        pflush();
        pos_ += nb;
        if (progress && c0 + nb == T && c0 >= B) {   // last chunk queued: chunk c-1 is the only one not reported yet
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventSynchronize(ev_emb_[par ^ 1]));
            progress(c0);
        }
        // final hidden states of the requested rows (their logits are computed after the prefill: the LM-head rows
        // may hold prefill buffers until then)
        for (int t = 0; t < nb; t++) {
            const int gi = c0 + t;
            const bool want = rows_out && std::find(logit_rows.begin(), logit_rows.end(), gi) != logit_rows.end();
            if (!want && !(gi == T - 1 && last)) continue;
            saved.push_back({gi, std::vector<float>(D)});
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(saved.back().second.data(), pb_.X + (size_t)t * D, D * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, st_));
            CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
        }
    }
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
    if (progress) progress(T);
    sseq_ = sissued_ = 0;   // the decode ring held prefill scratch: the next pass re-issues its first pieces
    // Release the prefill scratch (and restore borrowed LM-head rows): keeping ~300 MB of VRAM pinned down left the
    // device nearly full, and decode afterwards ran ~35% slower (CPU work 129 -> 175 ms/token, measured; cause
    // inside the driver / display stack not identified). Re-allocation per prefill costs ~10 ms.
    prefill_free();
    for (const auto& [gi, h] : saved) {
        const bool want = rows_out && std::find(logit_rows.begin(), logit_rows.end(), gi) != logit_rows.end();
        const bool is_last = gi == T - 1;
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_x, h.data(), D * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
        cuda::add_rmsnorm_rows2(d_x, cuda::RowSrc{}, cuda::RowSrc{}, output_norm_, D, 1, eps, d_xn, nullptr, &act_x_, st_, h_xq);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ev_, st_));
        DecodeResult r;
        lm_head(want ? 1 : topk, want, &r, 1, /*streamed=*/false);
        if (want) rows_out->push_back(r.logits);
        if (is_last && last) {
            if (want) {   // derive top-k from the full logits
                std::vector<int> order(r.logits.size());
                for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); i++) order[i] = (int)i;
                const int k = std::min(std::max(topk, 1), 32);
                std::partial_sort(order.begin(), order.begin() + k, order.end(),
                                  [&](int a, int b2) { return r.logits[a] > r.logits[b2]; });
                last->topk_ids.assign(order.begin(), order.begin() + k);
                last->topk_vals.clear();
                for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) last->topk_vals.push_back(r.logits[order[i]]);
                last->logits = r.logits;
            } else {
                *last = r;
            }
        }
    }
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
    pstats_.t_total += now_s() - t_start;
    pstats_.tokens += T;
    if (pprof_) {
        static const char* names[PP_N] = {"dequant", "gemm", "wait-copy", "attention", "deltanet", "other"};
        double tot = 0;
        for (double v : pp_ms_) tot += v;
        fprintf(stderr, "prefill GPU time by phase (%d tokens, %.0f ms):", T, tot);
        for (int i = 0; i < PP_N; i++) fprintf(stderr, " %s %.0f (%.0f%%)", names[i], pp_ms_[i], 100.0 * pp_ms_[i] / std::max(tot, 1e-9));
        fprintf(stderr, "\n");
    }
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/engine.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file engine.h
/// Inference engine for Qwen3.8-27B (qwen35: 48 Gated DeltaNet + 16 gated-attention layers, optional MTP block)
/// on one 8 GB Turing GPU plus the host CPU. See docs/ARCHITECTURE.md for the full description.
///
/// Decode is hybrid: the residency planner splits every large matrix into a VRAM-resident prefix (rows; for the
/// FFN, 256-wide blocks of the intermediate dimension) computed by the GPU and a host-resident remainder computed
/// by the CPU thread pool at the same time; part of the host remainder is also streamed to the GPU over PCIe each
/// pass. Recurrent state, KV cache, norms, attention and all other non-GEMV work run on the GPU. decode_batch()
/// processes up to MAX_BATCH tokens per pass over the weights (speculative verification); the MTP block drafts.
/// Prefill runs entirely on the GPU (cuBLAS FP16 GEMMs on dequantized weight blocks, host shares streamed).

#include <cstdint>
#include <map>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include "cpu/cpu_kernels.h"
#include "cpu/thread_pool.h"
#include "cuda/gemv.cuh"
#include "cuda/ops.cuh"
#include "runtime/gguf_parser.h"
#include "runtime/planner.h"

namespace q38rt {

class VisionModel;
struct ImagePatches;

struct EngineConfig {
    std::string model_path;
    int max_context = 8192;
    cuda::KVType kv_type = cuda::KVType::Q8_0;
    int cpu_threads = 8;
    double vram_reserve_mib = 60;       // left free for the desktop / driver (prefill borrows the LM-head rows)
    double gpu_weight_cap_mib = -1;     // optional cap on the weight budget (testing)
    double stream_mib = 2000;           // cap on decode-time PCIe streaming per token (0 = off); the planner picks the amount
    bool mtp = true;                    // load the MTP (nextn) block if the GGUF has one (speculative drafting)
    int draft_rows = 65536;             // LM-head rows kept in VRAM for the MTP draft head (vocabulary prefix)
    bool mtp_q4k = true;                // requantize the MTP block Q6_K -> Q4_K at load (-31% bytes per draft step)
    bool cpu_r4 = true;                 // CPU kernels on R4 re-encoded IQ3_S / IQ3_XXS host rows (exact)
    bool cpu_r2 = true;                 // CPU kernels on R2 re-encoded IQ2_XXS / IQ2_XS / IQ2_S host rows (exact)
    bool mtp_overlap = true;            // speculative passes: draft the next cycle on the GPU (MTP block forced into
                                        // VRAM) while the CPU computes its LM-head rows (decode_batch guess)
    int spec_k = 2;                     // plan for speculative cycles with k drafts (trunk passes of k+1 tokens,
                                        // MTP block k times per cycle; only with the MTP block); 0 = plan for plain
                                        // decode. 2 beat 0 and 1 on all prompt types with adaptive drafts
    bool verbose = true;
};

/// A weight matrix split between GPU (prefix) and CPU (remainder).
struct SplitMat {
    std::string name;
    QuantFormat type = QuantFormat::INVALID;
    int K = 0, N = 0;            // N rows of K elements (GGUF ne0 = K, ne1 = N)
    bool col_split = false;      // FFN down: split along K
    int gpu_part = 0;            // rows (row split) or columns (col split) on the GPU
    void* gpu = nullptr;
    size_t gpu_rb = 0;           // bytes per GPU row
    uint8_t* cpu = nullptr;
    size_t cpu_rb = 0;           // bytes per CPU row
    int gpu_n() const { return col_split ? N : gpu_part; }
    int gpu_k() const { return col_split ? gpu_part : K; }
    int cpu_n() const { return col_split ? N : N - gpu_part; }
    int cpu_k() const { return col_split ? K - gpu_part : K; }
    // Decode-time PCIe streaming: the first `stream_part` rows (row split) or columns (col split) of the
    // host share are copied to VRAM ring slots every token (in slot-sized pieces) and computed by the GPU;
    // the CPU does the rest.
    int stream_part = 0;
    // CPU-only exact re-encoding of the host share (R4 for IQ3, R2 for IQ2; cpu::re_*): same layout as `cpu` (row
    // split: cpu_n rows; col split: N rows of cpu_k columns), used by the CPU kernels; `cpu` stays for streaming and
    // prefill
    uint8_t* cpu_re = nullptr;
    size_t cpu_re_rb = 0;
    const uint8_t* cpu2_ptr_re() const {
        return col_split ? cpu_re + (size_t)(stream_part / 256) * (cpu_re_rb / (cpu_k() / 256)) : cpu_re + (size_t)stream_part * cpu_re_rb;
    }
    size_t str_rb() const { return col_split ? quant_row_bytes(type, stream_part) : cpu_rb; }
    int cpu2_n() const { return col_split ? N : cpu_n() - stream_part; }       // CPU rows after streaming
    int cpu2_k() const { return col_split ? cpu_k() - stream_part : K; }       // CPU columns after streaming
    const uint8_t* cpu2_ptr() const { return col_split ? cpu + str_rb() : cpu + (size_t)stream_part * cpu_rb; }
};

struct LayerWeights {
    bool full = false;
    bool mtp = false;            // the MTP (nextn) block: full attention + FFN, eh_proj in front
    SplitMat eh_proj;            // MTP: [enorm(embed) | hnorm(h)] (10240) -> 5120
    float *enorm = nullptr, *hnorm = nullptr, *head_norm = nullptr;
    float* attn_norm = nullptr;
    float* post_norm = nullptr;
    // DeltaNet
    SplitMat qkv, z, ssm_out;
    uint16_t* alpha = nullptr;   // bf16 [48][5120] (GPU)
    uint16_t* beta = nullptr;
    cuda::DeltaNetParams dn;
    // attention
    SplitMat q, k, v, o;
    float* q_norm = nullptr;
    float* k_norm = nullptr;
    cuda::KVLayer kv;
    // FFN: gate/up row split and down column split share gpu_part
    SplitMat gate, up, down;
};

struct DecodeResult {
    std::vector<float> topk_vals;    // descending
    std::vector<int32_t> topk_ids;
    std::vector<float> logits;       // full vocabulary, only if requested
};

/// Adaptive draft length for speculative decoding: tracks the acceptance rate of draft depth d (given that drafts
/// 1..d-1 were accepted) and the measured cycle time per k with exponential decay, and picks the k (1..max_k)
/// maximizing expected tokens per cycle / cycle time.
struct DraftController {
    static constexpr int K_MAX = 6;            // Engine::MAX_BATCH - 1
    int max_k = K_MAX;
    double decay = 0.92;                       // per cycle: memory of ~12 cycles
    // Acceptance at depth 1: prior 0.9 (weight 4). At depth d > 1 the evidence is blended with the estimate of depth
    // d-1 (weight 2): conditional acceptance is nearly constant over depths for a given text (q38_eval draftstats:
    // 0.97 ... 0.90 on code edits, 0.75 ... 0.70 on prose), so a depth not drafted yet inherits the last one's rate
    // and long drafts get tried exactly where they can pay.
    double hits[K_MAX + 1] = {0, 3.6};
    double tries[K_MAX + 1] = {0, 4};
    // relative cycle time per k: verify pass of k+1 tokens + k MTP steps (overlapped with the previous pass), replaced
    // by measurements when adapt_cost (off for reproducible seeded sampling: k must not depend on timing); priors:
    // measured k+1-token pass times relative to 2 tokens (batchtest, 4K context, 2026-10-22)
    double cost[K_MAX + 1] = {0, 1.00, 1.12, 1.27, 1.43, 1.63, 1.83};
    bool cost_measured[K_MAX + 1] = {};
    bool adapt_cost = true;
    int choose() const {
        int best = 1;
        double best_rate = 0, tpc = 1, pr = 1, p = 0;
        for (int k = 1; k <= max_k; k++) {
            p = k == 1 ? hits[1] / tries[1] : (hits[k] + 2.0 * p) / (tries[k] + 2.0);
            pr *= p;
            tpc += pr;
            const double rate = tpc / cost[k];
            if (rate > best_rate) { best_rate = rate; best = k; }
        }
        return best;
    }
    /// after a cycle with k drafts of which `accepted` were kept, taking `seconds`
    void update(int k, int accepted, double seconds) {
        for (int d = 1; d <= max_k; d++) { hits[d] *= decay; tries[d] *= decay; }
        for (int d = 1; d <= k && d <= accepted + 1; d++) {   // depth d evaluated iff drafts 1..d-1 accepted
            tries[d] += 1;
            if (d <= accepted) hits[d] += 1;
        }
        if (!adapt_cost) return;
        if (!cost_measured[k]) {   // first measurement of k: rescale the relative priors of the others
            const double scale = seconds / cost[k];
            for (int j = 1; j <= max_k; j++) if (!cost_measured[j]) cost[j] *= scale;
            cost_measured[k] = true;
        }
        cost[k] = 0.8 * cost[k] + 0.2 * seconds;
    }
};

/// Per-token timing breakdown (host wall clock, accumulated).
struct DecodeStats {
    double t_total = 0, t_cpu_work = 0, t_wait_gpu = 0, t_lm = 0;
    int tokens = 0;
    long predrafts = 0, predraft_misses = 0;   // overlapped drafting: drafts made during a verify pass / discarded
};

class Engine {
public:
    Engine();
    ~Engine();

    bool init(const EngineConfig& cfg);

    /// Process one token at position(); returns the top-k (k <= 32) of the next-token logits,
    /// plus the full logits if want_logits.
    void decode(int32_t token, int topk, bool want_logits, DecodeResult& out) { decode_batch(&token, 1, topk, want_logits, &out); }

    /// Process n <= MAX_BATCH consecutive tokens at positions position() .. position()+n-1 in one pass over the
    /// weights (multi-column GEMVs); out[i] receives the top-k / logits after token i. Results are bit-identical
    /// to n successive decode() calls. 7 = the token to verify + up to 6 drafts (code-like text accepts long drafts).
    static constexpr int MAX_BATCH = 7;
    /// Overlapped drafting (speculative passes with the MTP block): once the GPU's share of the LM head (the resident
    /// and streamed vocabulary rows) has its top-k, `guess(partial, n)` is called with results restricted to those
    /// rows while the CPU computes the rest. It returns the expected outcome of the cycle (accepted drafts, next
    /// token, drafts wanted for the next cycle; k = 0: none) and the engine drafts the next cycle on the GPU as if
    /// that outcome had been committed. take_drafts() hands those drafts out once the real outcome is known.
    struct SpecGuess { int accepted = -1; int32_t next = -1; int k = 0; };
    using SpecGuessFn = std::function<SpecGuess(const DecodeResult* partial, int n)>;
    void decode_batch(const int32_t* tokens, int n, int topk, bool want_logits, DecodeResult* out, bool speculative = false,
                      const SpecGuessFn* guess = nullptr);
    /// After commit(): the drafts made during the last verify pass if its guess matched the real outcome (same
    /// position, same next token, at least k drafts); otherwise the MTP state is rolled back and false is returned
    /// (draft with mtp_draft()).
    bool take_drafts(int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts);

    /// After a speculative decode_batch (tokens at position() .. position()+n-1): keep the first m (1 <= m <= n) of
    /// them. position() advances by m; the DeltaNet state is committed lazily (replayed at the start of the next
    /// pass), the KV rows of rejected tokens are simply overwritten later.
    void commit(int m);
    /// Apply pending (committed, not yet replayed) DeltaNet tokens to the state now.
    void flush_pending();

    /// MTP drafting (requires the MTP block, has_mtp()). The MTP layer keeps its own KV cache of pairs
    /// (target h_{q-1}, token x_q) at position q. mtp_catchup() adds the pairs whose token is already known
    /// (q < position()); mtp_draft(next, k) adds the pair for q = position() with the token `next` (sampled from
    /// the last committed logits, not yet decoded) and chains k-1 more steps on the MTP's own hidden state;
    /// writes k draft tokens (argmax of the draft head = resident LM-head rows [0, draft_rows)).
    bool has_mtp() const { return has_mtp_; }
    void mtp_catchup();
    void mtp_draft(int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts);

    /// An image in a prefill: tokens [offset, offset + nx * ny) of the prompt are its merged patches in raster order
    /// (their ids are ignored; the chat template uses <|image_pad|>) and take the embeddings `emb` (encode_image,
    /// nx * ny rows of 5120) instead of token embeddings. Positions follow Qwen3-VL's M-RoPE: with p the position the
    /// first image token would have as text, the token in row r, column c gets (t, h, w) = (p, p + r, p + c), and the
    /// text after the image continues at p + max(nx, ny) although the image fills nx * ny cache rows (rope_delta()).
    struct PrefillImage { int offset = 0, nx = 0, ny = 0; const float* emb = nullptr; };
    /// Batched prefill on the GPU (host-resident weight shares streamed over PCIe). Logits are produced for
    /// the prompt positions listed in `logit_rows` (indices into tokens) into `rows_out` (full logits) and the
    /// top-k of the last token into `last_result` (if non-null). chunk = tokens per batch (0 = auto). progress(n) is
    /// called once per chunk when the GPU has finished it, with n = tokens of `tokens` processed so far. `images`:
    /// image token spans, in order, inside `tokens`; the prompt must not end inside one.
    void prefill_batched(const std::vector<int32_t>& tokens, DecodeResult* last_result, int topk,
                         const std::vector<int>& logit_rows = {}, std::vector<std::vector<float>>* rows_out = nullptr,
                         int chunk = 0, const std::function<void(int)>& progress = {},
                         const std::vector<PrefillImage>& images = {});
    /// Rotary position of the next token minus position(): the sum of max(nx, ny) - nx * ny over the images in the
    /// cache (0 for text only).
    int rope_delta() const { return rope_delta_; }
    struct PrefillStats { double t_total = 0, t_copy_wait = 0; double bytes_streamed = 0; int tokens = 0; };
    PrefillStats& prefill_stats() { return pstats_; }

    void reset();

    /// Run the vision encoder on a preprocessed image (runtime/vision.h): FP16 GEMMs on the GPU with the weights
    /// streamed from pinned host memory through the decode streaming ring, activations in temporary VRAM. Writes
    /// n_tokens rows of 5120 floats (the image's embeddings for the language model). Between decode passes only.
    bool encode_image(const VisionModel& vm, const ImagePatches& ip, std::vector<float>& out, std::string& err);
    /// Temporary VRAM outside the decode plan: one buffer per size, in free VRAM or, when that is short, in the
    /// resident LM-head rows (restored by scratch_release). Between decode passes only.
    bool scratch_acquire(const std::vector<size_t>& sizes, std::vector<void*>& out);
    void scratch_release();
    int position() const { return pos_; }
    int max_context() const { return cfg_.max_context; }   // current KV capacity
    /// Largest supported context: the model's training context and the decode attention kernels' limit (262144).
    int max_context_limit() const;
    /// Engine log on stderr (plans, buffer sizes, resizes): EngineConfig::verbose, changeable after init().
    void set_verbose(bool v) { cfg_.verbose = v; }

    /// Grow the KV capacity to new_ctx positions keeping the conversation: the live state (KV rows, DeltaNet
    /// states, MTP rings) is parked in host memory, the engine is re-planned for the smaller weight budget and
    /// reloaded, and the state restored. A few seconds; position() and outputs are unchanged.
    bool resize(int new_ctx);
    /// State snapshot for conversation reuse (agent loops resend the whole history; the re-rendered previous answer
    /// rarely matches the generated tokens exactly): save_snapshot() records the recurrent state at position() in
    /// pinned host memory (DeltaNet states and conv windows, MTP rings; ~160 MB, ~25 ms). KV rows need no copy:
    /// positions below the snapshot are never rewritten unless reset() runs, which invalidates it.
    /// restore_snapshot() returns to that position, after which decoding continues from there.
    bool save_snapshot();
    bool restore_snapshot();
    int snapshot_position() const { return snap_valid_ ? snap_pos_ : -1; }
    /// Benchmark-only: jump to position `pos` over the current (zero-initialized) KV cache and
    /// recurrent state. Decode cost at depth depends only on the KV length, not its contents.
    void skip_to(int pos) { flush_pending(); pos_ = pos; }
    const ModelConfig& config() const { return gguf_.get_config(); }
    const GGUFParser& gguf() const { return gguf_; }
    const Plan& plan() const { return plan_; }
    DecodeStats& stats() { return stats_; }
    size_t vram_used_bytes() const { return vram_used_; }
    /// Wait profile (enabled by env Q38_PROFILE_WAITS=1): per wait site, host time spent while the GPU was
    /// still computing the previous group's GEMVs ("behind") vs the serial chain after them ("chain").
    void print_wait_profile() const;
    size_t host_weight_bytes() const { return host_bytes_; }

private:
    EngineConfig cfg_;
    GGUFParser gguf_;
    Plan plan_;
    std::unique_ptr<cpu::ThreadPool> pool_;
    cudaStream_t st_ = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t ev_ = nullptr;
    int pos_ = 0;
    int n_trunk_ = 64;           // trunk layers; L_[n_trunk_] is the MTP block when has_mtp_
    bool has_mtp_ = false;
    // lazy DeltaNet commit: conv window buffer holding the current state, tokens committed but not yet applied
    // to the state (inputs in save buffer dn_sb_), an open speculative pass awaiting commit()
    int conv_cur_ = 0, dn_pending_ = 0, dn_sb_ = 0, spec_n_ = 0;
    bool spec_pass_ = false, spec_open_ = false;
    // images in the cache (M-RoPE): cache rows [start, start + nx * ny) per image, in order; text at cache row i >= the
    // last image's end turns at rotary position i + rope_delta_
    struct ImageSpan { int start, nx, ny; };
    std::vector<ImageSpan> images_;
    int rope_delta_ = 0;
    int rope_delta_at(int pos) const;                          // rotary offset of text at cache row pos
    bool rope_positions(int p0, int n, std::vector<int>& pos3) const;   // (t, h, w) of rows p0.. if an image is among them
    float* d_dnsave_ = nullptr;   // [2 buffers][num_layers][MAX_BATCH] x (qkv 10240 | beta 64 | alpha 64)
    // MTP inputs: target final hidden states (output_norm(x)) and tokens per position (ring), MTP chain state
    static constexpr int HRING = 16;
    float* d_hring_ = nullptr;    // [HRING][5120], row = position % HRING
    float* d_hmtp_ = nullptr;     // [5120] MTP output state of the last draft row (chained drafting)
    int32_t tok_ring_[HRING] = {};
    int mtp_pos_ = 0;             // MTP KV holds the true pairs for positions < mtp_pos_
    // snapshot (save_snapshot / restore_snapshot); host buffer kept across resize()
    uint8_t* h_snap_ = nullptr;
    size_t h_snap_size_ = 0;
    bool snap_valid_ = false;
    int snap_pos_ = 0, snap_conv_cur_ = 0, snap_mtp_pos_ = 0, snap_rope_delta_ = 0;
    std::vector<ImageSpan> snap_images_;
    int32_t snap_tok_ring_[HRING] = {};
    void mtp_rows(int q0, int m, const int32_t* toks, const float* h_first, bool h_from_ring, int32_t* draft);
    static constexpr int DNS_TOK = 10240 + 64 + 64;
    // lazy-commit save buffers of the DeltaNet layers only (layer il is attention when (il + 1) % 4 == 0)
    static constexpr int DN_LAYERS = 48;
    float* dn_save(int buf, int il) const {
        return d_dnsave_ + ((size_t)buf * DN_LAYERS + (il - (il + 1) / 4)) * MAX_BATCH * DNS_TOK;
    }
    DecodeStats stats_;
    size_t vram_used_ = 0, host_bytes_ = 0;

    std::vector<LayerWeights> L_;
    float* output_norm_ = nullptr;
    SplitMat lm_;
    const TensorInfo* tok_embd_ = nullptr;

    // device memory
    uint8_t* d_arena_ = nullptr;      // all GPU weights
    uint8_t* d_misc_ = nullptr;       // states, KV, activations
    uint8_t* h_weights_ = nullptr;    // all CPU weights (huge-page backed)
    size_t h_weights_size_ = 0;

    // activations (device)
    float *d_x = nullptr, *d_xn = nullptr, *d_qkv = nullptr, *d_z = nullptr, *d_beta = nullptr, *d_alpha = nullptr,
          *d_o = nullptr, *d_y = nullptr, *d_y2 = nullptr, *d_g = nullptr, *d_u = nullptr, *d_qg = nullptr,
          *d_k = nullptr, *d_v = nullptr, *d_q = nullptr, *d_gate = nullptr, *d_logits = nullptr, *d_tkv = nullptr;
    int* d_tki = nullptr;
    void* d_attn_work = nullptr;
    void* d_topk_work = nullptr;
    cuda::ActQ8 act_x_, act_o_, act_h_;

    // pinned host buffers
    float *h_x = nullptr, *h_o = nullptr, *h_rows = nullptr, *h_rows2 = nullptr, *h_g = nullptr,
          *h_u = nullptr, *h_y2 = nullptr, *h_logits = nullptr, *h_tkv = nullptr;
    int* h_tki = nullptr;
    block_q8_K *h_xq = nullptr, *h_hq = nullptr;

    bool load_weights();
    void teardown();   // free everything init() allocated except the parsed GGUF and the thread pool
    // weight tensors with substituted data (the requantized MTP block); kept across resize()
    struct WOverride { TensorInfo info; std::vector<uint8_t> data; };
    std::map<std::string, WOverride> woverride_;
    const TensorInfo& wt(const std::string& name) const;
    const uint8_t* wdata(const TensorInfo& t) const;
    void requantize_mtp();
    bool parsed_ = false;
    uint8_t* h_base_ = nullptr;   // pinned activation buffers (one allocation)
    bool build_plan(double budget_bytes);
    bool alloc_runtime();
    void upload_split(SplitMat& m, const TensorInfo& t, uint8_t*& dcur, uint8_t*& hcur);

    enum WaitSite { W_DN_IN, W_DN_OUT, W_AT_IN, W_AT_OUT, W_FFN, W_LM, W_MTP, W_NSITES };
    void wait_event(int site);
    void mark_gpu_group();
    void begin_gpu_group();
    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> gprof_ev_;   // per group of a token: GPU begin/end timing events
    std::vector<int> gprof_site_;
    int gprof_n_ = 0;
    double gprof_gpu_[W_NSITES] = {}, gprof_cpu_[W_NSITES] = {};
    int cur_site_ = 0;
    std::vector<double> gcpu_last_, ggpu_last_;   // per group of the last token (profile level 2 dump)
    bool prof_waits_ = false;
    double job_idle_ = 0;   // profiling: CPU job wall time minus mean thread busy time
    double site_idle_[8] = {}, site_maxgap_[8] = {};   // per wait site: imbalance, wall minus the longest thread
    long site_jobs_[8] = {}, idle_hist_[8] = {};       // jobs per site; histogram of per-job imbalance
    double site_skew_[8] = {}, site_endspread_[8] = {}, site_chunkspread_[8] = {};
    cudaEvent_t ev_g_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
    int gi_ = 0;
    struct WaitProf { double behind = 0, chain = 0; long n = 0; } wprof_[W_NSITES];
    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> sprof_ev_;   // per slice: GPU stream time before/after waiting for its copy
    std::vector<double> sprof_stall_;     // accumulated GPU stall per slice (s)
    long sprof_tokens_ = 0;
    // nb = tokens in the pass; activations of token t at act + t*act_ld, outputs at y + t*y_ld
    void gemv_gpu(const SplitMat& m, const cuda::ActQ8& act, float* y, int nb = 1, int act_ld = 0, int y_ld = 0);
    void layer_deltanet(int il, int nb);
    void layer_attention(int il, int nb, int pos0);
    void layer_ffn(int il, int nb);
    // CPU-share tails read directly from mapped host memory by the next GPU kernel (no copy kernels):
    // the attention/ssm_out output rows [ytail_split_, D) live in h_rows (stride ytail_ld_, 0 = none),
    // the FFN down CPU partial sums in h_y2 (stride D) when ffn_cpu_ is set.
    int ytail_split_ = 0, ytail_ld_ = 0;
    bool ffn_cpu_ = false;
    cuda::RowSrc y_src() const;      // attention/ssm_out output (device head + host tail)
    cuda::RowSrc y2_src() const;     // FFN CPU partial sums (host) or absent
    void lm_head(int topk, bool want_logits, DecodeResult* out, int nb = 1, bool streamed = true,
                 const SpecGuessFn* guess = nullptr);
    // overlapped drafting (decode_batch guess): drafts of the next cycle made during the LM head of a verify pass
    struct PreDraft { bool valid = false; int pos = 0; int32_t next = -1; int k = 0; int32_t d[MAX_BATCH] = {}; int old_mtp_pos = 0; };
    PreDraft pre_;
    bool pool_async_ = false;            // the pool runs the LM-head job asynchronously: CPU jobs run on the caller
    std::vector<block_q8_K> h_xq_lm_;    // private copy of the LM-head activations for that job
    cudaEvent_t ev_lmtopk_ = nullptr;    // GPU top-k of the LM head copied to the host
    void mtp_draft_at(int pos, int32_t next, int k, int32_t* drafts);
    void discard_predraft();             // roll the MTP state back past unused drafts
    void run_pool(cpu::ThreadPool::Fn fn, void* ctx);   // pool run, or inline on the caller while pool_async_

    // ---- batched prefill ----
    void* cublas_ = nullptr;
    PrefillStats pstats_;
    struct PB {
        int B = 0;
        uint8_t* base = nullptr;
        // live through the whole chunk: residual stream, layer output, normed FP16 input
        float *X, *Y;
        __half* Xn16;
        // one region shared by the phases of a layer and by the MTP block (see prefill_alloc)
        float *qkv, *k, *v, *q, *gate;                 // attention (O16 inside qkv's slot)
        float *z, *beta, *alpha, *dn_out;              // DeltaNet (dn_out and O16 inside qkv's slot)
        void* dn_work;
        __half* O16;
        float *G, *U;                                  // FFN; H16 = G's rows in place (row stride 2*FF halves)
        __half* H16;
        float *mHn, *mHs, *mE, *mk, *mv, *mq, *mgate;  // MTP block
        __half* mH16;
        __half* Wf16;
        uint8_t* stage[2];
        size_t wf16_elems = 0, stage_bytes = 0;   // stage_bytes per slot
        float* h_emb[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};     // pinned [B][5120], per chunk parity (read by async copies)
        void* cublas_ws = nullptr;
        float* rope = nullptr;                    // [B][64]: cos / sin of the chunk's positions (cuda::rope_table)
        int* rope_pos = nullptr;                  // [B][3]: (t, h, w) of chunks with image tokens (cuda::rope_table_pos)
        bool borrowed = false;                    // part of the buffers sits in the resident LM-head rows
    } pb_;
    uint8_t* scratch_base_ = nullptr;   // scratch_acquire's cudaMalloc
    bool scratch_borrowed_ = false;     // ... and whether it borrowed the LM-head rows
    uint8_t* h_lm_gpu_copy_ = nullptr;   // pinned host copy of the resident LM-head rows (restored after prefill)
    uint8_t* h_re_ = nullptr;            // re-encoded copies of the IQ2 / IQ3 host shares (CPU kernels only)
    size_t h_re_size_ = 0;
    void build_re();
    cudaEvent_t ev_emb_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // last async read of h_emb[i] done
    bool emb_pending_[2] = {false, false};
    // streamed host-weight pieces, in the exact order gemm_split consumes them (2-slot staging ring)
    struct Piece { const SplitMat* m; int r0, n; };
    std::vector<Piece> pieces_;
    size_t piece_issue_limit_ = 0, piece_next_ = 0;
    cudaStream_t cst_ = nullptr;                  // copy stream
    cudaEvent_t ev_copied_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr}, ev_used_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
    bool slot_used_[2] = {false, false};
    bool host_registered_ = false;
    // decode-time streaming ring
    static constexpr int NSLOT = 8;
    // streaming planner model constants (measured on the target machine)
    static constexpr double kStreamGpuEff = 0.75;        // streamed pieces run at ~75% of the full-matrix GPU rate
    static constexpr double kSliceOverhead = 10e-6;      // per streamed piece: launch + copy-event wait (s)
    static constexpr double kGroupHostOverhead = 15e-6;  // per sync group: quantize, launches, event latency (s)
    static constexpr double kPcieBytesPerS = 6.0e9;      // pinned H2D under decode load
    static constexpr size_t SLOT_BYTES = 8u << 20;
    uint8_t* d_slots_ = nullptr;
    uint8_t* h_strdup_ = nullptr;   // pinned contiguous duplicates of streamed FFN-down column slices
    cudaEvent_t ev_scopied_[NSLOT] = {}, ev_sused_[NSLOT] = {};
    bool sslot_used_[NSLOT] = {};
    // one streamed piece (fits a ring slot): rows [off, off+n) of a row-split stream part, or columns
    // [off, off+n) of a column-split one (host source then is a contiguous pinned duplicate)
    struct SPiece { const SplitMat* m; int off, n; const uint8_t* src; size_t bytes, rb; };
    std::vector<SPiece> spieces_;            // in decode consumption order
    // Streaming sequence numbers run across tokens (slice = seq % count, slot = seq % NSLOT), so the ring keeps
    // prefetching the next token's first slices while the LM head runs (weights are constant).
    uint64_t sseq_ = 0, sissued_ = 0;
    double streamed_bytes_per_token_ = 0;
    void plan_streaming(double budget_bytes);
    void issue_spiece(uint64_t seq);
    void stream_gemv(const SplitMat& m, const cuda::ActQ8& act, float* y, bool accumulate, int nb = 1, int act_ld = 0,
                     int y_ld = 0);
    void skip_stream(const SplitMat& m);
    void build_pieces();
    void issue_piece(size_t i);
    bool prefill_alloc(int B, size_t stage_bytes = 24u << 20, bool borrow = false);
    void prefill_free();
    void prefill_mtp(const std::vector<int32_t>& tokens, int c0, int nb, int p0, const float* h_emb);
    // prefill phase profiling (env Q38_PROFILE_PREFILL=1): GPU time between consecutive marks, per phase
    enum PPhase { PP_DEQ, PP_GEMM, PP_WAIT, PP_ATTN, PP_DN, PP_OTHER, PP_N };
    bool pprof_ = false;
    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> pp_ev_;
    std::vector<int> pp_phase_;
    size_t pp_n_ = 0;
    double pp_ms_[PP_N] = {};
    void pmark(int phase);
    void pflush();
    void gemm_split(const SplitMat& m, const __half* X, int ldx, int B, float* Y, int ldy);
    void gemm_bf16(const uint16_t* W, int N, const __half* X, int B, float* Y);
};

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/gguf_parser.cpp =====

#include "gguf_parser.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

namespace q38rt {

namespace {
// Sanity limits for values read from the file before they drive allocations.
constexpr uint64_t MAX_TENSORS       = 1000000;
constexpr uint64_t MAX_KV            = 100000;
constexpr uint64_t MAX_STRING_BYTES  = 1ull << 30;   // 1 GiB
constexpr uint64_t MAX_ARRAY_ELEMS   = 1ull << 32;
constexpr uint32_t MAX_DIMS          = 4;            // GGML_MAX_DIMS
constexpr int      MAX_ARRAY_NESTING = 4;

size_t scalar_size(GGUFType t) {
    switch (t) {
        case GGUFType::UINT8: case GGUFType::INT8: case GGUFType::BOOL: return 1;
        case GGUFType::UINT16: case GGUFType::INT16: return 2;
        case GGUFType::UINT32: case GGUFType::INT32: case GGUFType::FLOAT32: return 4;
        case GGUFType::UINT64: case GGUFType::INT64: case GGUFType::FLOAT64: return 8;
        default: return 0;  // STRING / ARRAY / invalid are not fixed-size
    }
}
} // namespace

const char* gguf_type_name(GGUFType t) {
    switch (t) {
        case GGUFType::UINT8: return "UINT8";
        case GGUFType::INT8: return "INT8";
        case GGUFType::UINT16: return "UINT16";
        case GGUFType::INT16: return "INT16";
        case GGUFType::UINT32: return "UINT32";
        case GGUFType::INT32: return "INT32";
        case GGUFType::FLOAT32: return "FLOAT32";
        case GGUFType::BOOL: return "BOOL";
        case GGUFType::STRING: return "STRING";
        case GGUFType::ARRAY: return "ARRAY";
        case GGUFType::UINT64: return "UINT64";
        case GGUFType::INT64: return "INT64";
        case GGUFType::FLOAT64: return "FLOAT64";
        default: return "INVALID";
    }
}

GGUFParser::GGUFParser() {}

GGUFParser::~GGUFParser() {
    if (base_) munmap(const_cast<uint8_t*>(base_), file_size_);
    if (fd_ >= 0) close(fd_);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bounds-checked readers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

void GGUFParser::need(uint64_t n, const char* what) const {
    if (pos_ > file_size_ || n > file_size_ - pos_) {
        LOG_ERROR("Unexpected EOF reading %s (%llu bytes) at offset 0x%llx (file size 0x%llx)",
                  what, (unsigned long long)n, (unsigned long long)pos_, (unsigned long long)file_size_);
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("Unexpected EOF reading ") + what);
    }
}

template <typename T>
T GGUFParser::read_pod(const char* what) {
    need(sizeof(T), what);
    T v;
    memcpy(&v, base_ + pos_, sizeof(T));
    pos_ += sizeof(T);
    return v;
}

std::string GGUFParser::read_string() {
    const uint64_t at = pos_;
    const uint64_t length = read_uint64();
    if (length > MAX_STRING_BYTES) {
        LOG_ERROR("Invalid GGUF string length=%llu at offset=0x%llx",
                  (unsigned long long)length, (unsigned long long)at);
        throw std::runtime_error("Invalid GGUF string length");
    }
    need(length, "string bytes");
    std::string s(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(base_ + pos_), length);
    pos_ += length;
    return s;
}

bool GGUFParser::read_array(GGUFValue& out, int depth) {
    const uint64_t at = pos_;
    // GGUF ARRAY encoding: uint32 element type, then uint64 count, then elements.
    const uint32_t raw_type = read_uint32();
    const uint64_t count = read_uint64();
    out.arr_type = static_cast<GGUFType>(raw_type);
    out.arr_count = count;
    if (count > MAX_ARRAY_ELEMS) {
        LOG_ERROR("Array count %llu too large at offset 0x%llx", (unsigned long long)count, (unsigned long long)at);
        return false;
    }
    if (out.arr_type == GGUFType::STRING) {
        out.arr_str.reserve(count);
        for (uint64_t i = 0; i < count; i++) out.arr_str.push_back(read_string());
        return true;
    }
    if (out.arr_type == GGUFType::ARRAY) {
        // Nested arrays are legal in the spec; validate and skip them (unused here).
        if (depth >= MAX_ARRAY_NESTING) {
            LOG_ERROR("Array nesting too deep at offset 0x%llx", (unsigned long long)at);
            return false;
        }
        for (uint64_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            GGUFValue inner;
            if (!read_array(inner, depth + 1)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    const size_t es = scalar_size(out.arr_type);
    if (es == 0) {
        LOG_ERROR("Unsupported GGUF array element type %u at offset 0x%llx", raw_type, (unsigned long long)at);
        return false;
    }
    const uint64_t bytes = count * es;  // count <= 2^32, es <= 8: no overflow
    need(bytes, "array elements");
    out.arr_raw.assign(base_ + pos_, base_ + pos_ + bytes);
    pos_ += bytes;
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::read_value(GGUFType type, GGUFValue& out, int depth) {
    out.type = type;
    switch (type) {
        case GGUFType::UINT8:   out.u64 = read_uint8(); return true;
        case GGUFType::INT8:    out.i64 = read_int8(); return true;
        case GGUFType::UINT16:  out.u64 = read_uint16(); return true;
        case GGUFType::INT16:   out.i64 = read_int16(); return true;
        case GGUFType::UINT32:  out.u64 = read_uint32(); return true;
        case GGUFType::INT32:   out.i64 = read_int32(); return true;   // exactly 4 bytes
        case GGUFType::FLOAT32: out.f64 = read_float(); return true;
        case GGUFType::BOOL:    out.u64 = read_uint8(); return true;   // 1 byte
        case GGUFType::STRING:  out.str = read_string(); return true;
        case GGUFType::ARRAY:   return read_array(out, depth);
        case GGUFType::UINT64:  out.u64 = read_uint64(); return true;
        case GGUFType::INT64:   out.i64 = read_int64(); return true;
        case GGUFType::FLOAT64: out.f64 = read_double(); return true;
        default:
            LOG_ERROR("Unsupported GGUF metadata type %u at offset 0x%llx",
                      static_cast<unsigned>(type), (unsigned long long)pos_);
            return false;
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

bool GGUFParser::parse(const std::string& path, bool verbose, const std::string& arch) {
    verbose_ = verbose;
    fd_ = open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY);
    if (fd_ < 0) {
        LOG_ERROR("Cannot open GGUF file: %s", path.c_str());
        return false;
    }
    struct stat st;
    if (fstat(fd_, &st) != 0) {
        LOG_ERROR("fstat failed: %s", path.c_str());
        return false;
    }
    file_size_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(st.st_size);
    void* m = mmap(nullptr, file_size_, PROT_READ, MAP_SHARED, fd_, 0);
    if (m == MAP_FAILED) {
        LOG_ERROR("mmap failed: %s", path.c_str());
        return false;
    }
    base_ = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(m);
    pos_ = 0;

    try {
        if (!parse_header()) return false;
        if (!parse_kv()) return false;
        if (!parse_tensors()) return false;
        if (!finalize_tensors()) return false;
        if (arch == "qwen35") {
            if (!build_config()) return false;
        } else if (get_kv_string("general.architecture") != arch) {
            LOG_ERROR("%s: architecture '%s', expected '%s'", path.c_str(), get_kv_string("general.architecture").c_str(),
                      arch.c_str());
            return false;
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        LOG_ERROR("GGUF parse failed at offset 0x%llx: %s", (unsigned long long)pos_, e.what());
        return false;
    }
    LOG_INFO("GGUF v%u: %llu KV, %llu tensors, data at 0x%llx (align %u), file %llu bytes",
             header_.version, (unsigned long long)header_.tensor_kv_count,
             (unsigned long long)header_.tensor_count, (unsigned long long)data_offset_,
             alignment_, (unsigned long long)file_size_);
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::parse_header() {
    header_.magic = read_uint32();
    if (header_.magic != Q38_GGUF_MAGIC) {
        LOG_ERROR("Invalid GGUF magic 0x%08x (expected 0x%08x)", header_.magic, Q38_GGUF_MAGIC);
        return false;
    }
    header_.version = read_uint32();          // uint32, not uint64
    if (header_.version < 2 || header_.version > 3) {
        LOG_ERROR("Unsupported GGUF version %u", header_.version);
        return false;
    }
    header_.tensor_count = read_uint64();
    header_.tensor_kv_count = read_uint64();
    if (header_.tensor_count > MAX_TENSORS) {
        LOG_ERROR("Invalid tensor count: %llu", (unsigned long long)header_.tensor_count);
        return false;
    }
    if (header_.tensor_kv_count > MAX_KV) {
        LOG_ERROR("Invalid metadata count: %llu", (unsigned long long)header_.tensor_kv_count);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::parse_kv() {
    kv_list_.reserve(header_.tensor_kv_count);
    for (uint64_t i = 0; i < header_.tensor_kv_count; i++) {
        GGUFKVPair kv;
        kv.file_offset = pos_;
        kv.key = read_string();
        const uint32_t raw_type = read_uint32();
        if (!read_value(static_cast<GGUFType>(raw_type), kv.value, 0)) {
            LOG_ERROR("Failed to read value of KV %llu '%s' (type %u)",
                      (unsigned long long)i, kv.key.c_str(), raw_type);
            return false;
        }
        if (verbose_) {
            LOG_DEBUG("KV[%llu] @0x%llx key='%s' type=%s", (unsigned long long)i,
                      (unsigned long long)kv.file_offset, kv.key.c_str(), gguf_type_name(kv.value.type));
        }
        if (kv_index_.count(kv.key)) {
            LOG_ERROR("Duplicate GGUF key '%s'", kv.key.c_str());
            return false;
        }
        kv_index_[kv.key] = kv_list_.size();
        kv_list_.push_back(std::move(kv));
    }
    if (const GGUFValue* a = find_kv("general.alignment")) {
        if (a->type != GGUFType::UINT32 || a->u64 == 0 || (a->u64 & (a->u64 - 1)) != 0) {
            LOG_ERROR("Invalid general.alignment");
            return false;
        }
        alignment_ = static_cast<uint32_t>(a->u64);
    }
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::parse_tensors() {
    tensors_.reserve(header_.tensor_count);
    for (uint64_t i = 0; i < header_.tensor_count; i++) {
        TensorInfo t;
        const uint64_t at = pos_;
        t.name = read_string();
        const uint32_t ndim = read_uint32();      // uint32, not uint64
        if (ndim == 0 || ndim > MAX_DIMS) {
            LOG_ERROR("Invalid tensor ndim %u for '%s' at offset 0x%llx", ndim, t.name.c_str(), (unsigned long long)at);
            return false;
        }
        t.shape.ndim = ndim;
        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < ndim; j++) {
            t.shape.dims[j] = read_uint64();
            if (t.shape.dims[j] == 0 || t.shape.dims[j] > (1ull << 40)) {
                LOG_ERROR("Invalid dim %u=%llu for '%s'", j, (unsigned long long)t.shape.dims[j], t.name.c_str());
                return false;
            }
        }
        const uint32_t raw_type = read_uint32();
        t.quant = static_cast<QuantFormat>(raw_type);   // exact ggml_type id, never remapped
        t.rel_offset = read_uint64();
        const QuantBlockInfo bi = quant_block_info(t.quant);
        if (bi.block_elems == 0) {
            LOG_ERROR("Unknown ggml type %u for tensor '%s'", raw_type, t.name.c_str());
            return false;
        }
        if (t.shape.dims[0] % bi.block_elems != 0) {
            LOG_ERROR("Tensor '%s' row length %llu not a multiple of %s block size %u", t.name.c_str(),
                      (unsigned long long)t.shape.dims[0], bi.name, bi.block_elems);
            return false;
        }
        t.size_bytes = t.shape.size() / bi.block_elems * bi.block_bytes;

        // block index of "blk.N.<role>" tensors (model-level tensors keep -1)
        if (t.name.compare(0, 4, "blk.") == 0) {
            const size_t dot = t.name.find('.', 4);
            if (dot == std::string::npos) {
                LOG_ERROR("Malformed tensor name '%s'", t.name.c_str());
                return false;
            }
            t.layer_index = std::stoi(t.name.substr(4, dot - 4));
        }

        if (verbose_) {
            LOG_DEBUG("tensor[%llu] @0x%llx '%s' ndim=%u %s %s rel_off=0x%llx", (unsigned long long)i,
                      (unsigned long long)at, t.name.c_str(), ndim, t.shape.name().c_str(), bi.name,
                      (unsigned long long)t.rel_offset);
        }
        if (tensor_index_.count(t.name)) {
            LOG_ERROR("Duplicate tensor name '%s'", t.name.c_str());
            return false;
        }
        tensor_index_[t.name] = tensors_.size();
        tensors_.push_back(std::move(t));
    }
    meta_end_ = pos_;
    data_offset_ = (meta_end_ + alignment_ - 1) / alignment_ * alignment_;
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::finalize_tensors() {
    if (data_offset_ > file_size_) {
        LOG_ERROR("Data section offset 0x%llx beyond file size", (unsigned long long)data_offset_);
        return false;
    }
    const uint64_t data_bytes = file_size_ - data_offset_;
    // GGUF does not require the info table to be in offset order: validate on a sorted view.
    std::vector<size_t> order(tensors_.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); i++) order[i] = i;
    std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(),
              [&](size_t a, size_t b) { return tensors_[a].rel_offset < tensors_[b].rel_offset; });
    for (size_t k = 0; k < order.size(); k++) {
        TensorInfo& t = tensors_[order[k]];
        if (t.rel_offset % alignment_ != 0) {
            LOG_ERROR("Tensor '%s' offset 0x%llx not %u-aligned", t.name.c_str(),
