                      (unsigned long long)t.rel_offset, alignment_);
            return false;
        }
        const uint64_t end = t.rel_offset + t.size_bytes;
        const uint64_t next = (k + 1 < order.size()) ? tensors_[order[k + 1]].rel_offset : data_bytes;
        if (end > next) {
            LOG_ERROR("Tensor '%s' [0x%llx, 0x%llx) overlaps next tensor/EOF at 0x%llx", t.name.c_str(),
                      (unsigned long long)t.rel_offset, (unsigned long long)end, (unsigned long long)next);
            return false;
        }
        t.padding = next - end;
        t.offset = data_offset_ + t.rel_offset;
    }
    return true;
}

bool GGUFParser::build_config() {
    const std::string arch = get_kv_string("general.architecture");
    if (arch != "qwen35") {
        LOG_ERROR("Unsupported architecture '%s' (this runtime is specialized for qwen35)", arch.c_str());
        return false;
    }
    ModelConfig& c = config_;
    auto u = [&](const char* k, int32_t& dst) {
        const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(k);
        if (!v) { LOG_ERROR("Missing required key %s", k); throw std::runtime_error(k); }
        dst = static_cast<int32_t>(get_kv_uint(k));
    };
    u("qwen35.block_count", c.num_hidden_layers);
    // MTP variants append next-token-prediction blocks after the trunk (blk.64 here)
    c.num_nextn_layers = find_kv("qwen35.nextn_predict_layers") ? (int32_t)get_kv_uint("qwen35.nextn_predict_layers") : 0;
    c.num_layers = c.num_hidden_layers - c.num_nextn_layers;
    u("qwen35.context_length", c.train_context);
    u("qwen35.embedding_length", c.hidden_size);
    u("qwen35.feed_forward_length", c.ffn_hidden);
    u("qwen35.attention.head_count", c.num_attention_heads);
    u("qwen35.attention.head_count_kv", c.num_key_value_heads);
    u("qwen35.attention.key_length", c.head_dim);
    u("qwen35.ssm.conv_kernel", c.conv_window);
    u("qwen35.ssm.state_size", c.delta_net_state_dim);
    u("qwen35.ssm.group_count", c.ssm_group_count);
    u("qwen35.ssm.time_step_rank", c.value_heads);
    u("qwen35.ssm.inner_size", c.ssm_inner_size);
    u("qwen35.full_attention_interval", c.full_attn_interval);
    u("qwen35.rope.dimension_count", c.rope_dims);
    c.rms_eps = get_kv_float("qwen35.attention.layer_norm_rms_epsilon", 1e-6f);
    c.rope_theta = get_kv_float("qwen35.rope.freq_base", 1e7f);
    c.rope_partial_ratio = (float)c.rope_dims / (float)c.head_dim;
    if (const GGUFValue* s = find_kv("qwen35.rope.dimension_sections")) {
        if (s->type == GGUFType::ARRAY && s->arr_type == GGUFType::INT32 && s->arr_count == 4) {
            c.rope_mrope_sections.resize(4);
            memcpy(c.rope_mrope_sections.data(), s->arr_raw.data(), 16);
        }
    }
    int32_t value_len = 0;
    u("qwen35.attention.value_length", value_len);
    c.num_full_attn = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < c.num_layers; i++) c.num_full_attn += c.is_full_attention(i) ? 1 : 0;
    c.num_linear_layers = c.num_layers - c.num_full_attn;

    if (const TensorInfo* emb = find_tensor("token_embd.weight")) c.vocab_size = (int32_t)emb->shape.dims[1];
    c.bos_token_id = (int32_t)get_kv_uint("tokenizer.ggml.bos_token_id", c.bos_token_id);
    c.eos_token_id = (int32_t)get_kv_uint("tokenizer.ggml.eos_token_id", c.eos_token_id);
    c.pad_token_id = (int32_t)get_kv_uint("tokenizer.ggml.padding_token_id", c.pad_token_id);
    c.add_bos_token = get_kv_bool("tokenizer.ggml.add_bos_token", false);

    // The kernels are specialized: refuse files that do not match the compiled shapes.
    const bool ok = c.num_layers == NUM_LAYERS && c.hidden_size == NUM_HIDDEN && c.ffn_hidden == FFN_HIDDEN &&
                    c.num_attention_heads == NUM_ATTENTION_HEADS && c.num_key_value_heads == NUM_KEY_VALUE_HEADS &&
                    c.head_dim == HEAD_DIM && value_len == HEAD_DIM && c.delta_net_state_dim == DELTA_NET_STATE_DIM &&
                    c.value_heads == VALUE_HEADS && c.ssm_group_count == 16 && c.ssm_inner_size == 6144 &&
                    c.conv_window == CONV_WINDOW && c.full_attn_interval == FULL_ATTN_INTERVAL &&
                    c.rope_dims == 64 && c.vocab_size == VOCAB_SIZE && c.num_full_attn == NUM_FULL_ATTN &&
                    c.rope_mrope_sections == std::vector<int32_t>{11, 11, 10, 0} &&   // cuda::rope_table_pos
                    (c.num_nextn_layers == 0 || c.num_nextn_layers == 1);
    if (!ok) {
        LOG_ERROR("GGUF hyperparameters differ from the specialized Qwen3.8-27B shapes");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Accessors
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const TensorInfo* GGUFParser::find_tensor(const std::string& name) const {
    auto it = tensor_index_.find(name);
    return it == tensor_index_.end() ? nullptr : &tensors_[it->second];
}

const GGUFValue* GGUFParser::find_kv(const std::string& key) const {
    auto it = kv_index_.find(key);
    return it == kv_index_.end() ? nullptr : &kv_list_[it->second].value;
}

std::string GGUFParser::get_kv_string(const std::string& key, const std::string& def) const {
    const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(key);
    return (v && v->type == GGUFType::STRING) ? v->str : def;
}

uint64_t GGUFParser::get_kv_uint(const std::string& key, uint64_t def) const {
    const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(key);
    if (!v) return def;
    switch (v->type) {
        case GGUFType::UINT8: case GGUFType::UINT16: case GGUFType::UINT32:
        case GGUFType::UINT64: case GGUFType::BOOL: return v->u64;
        case GGUFType::INT8: case GGUFType::INT16: case GGUFType::INT32:
        case GGUFType::INT64: return v->i64 < 0 ? def : (uint64_t)v->i64;
        default: return def;
    }
}

int64_t GGUFParser::get_kv_int(const std::string& key, int64_t def) const {
    const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(key);
    if (!v) return def;
    switch (v->type) {
        case GGUFType::INT8: case GGUFType::INT16: case GGUFType::INT32: case GGUFType::INT64: return v->i64;
        case GGUFType::UINT8: case GGUFType::UINT16: case GGUFType::UINT32:
        case GGUFType::UINT64: case GGUFType::BOOL: return (int64_t)v->u64;
        default: return def;
    }
}

float GGUFParser::get_kv_float(const std::string& key, float def) const {
    const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(key);
    if (!v) return def;
    if (v->type == GGUFType::FLOAT32 || v->type == GGUFType::FLOAT64) return (float)v->f64;
    return def;
}

bool GGUFParser::get_kv_bool(const std::string& key, bool def) const {
    const GGUFValue* v = find_kv(key);
    return (v && v->type == GGUFType::BOOL) ? v->u64 != 0 : def;
}

std::vector<uint8_t> GGUFParser::get_tensor_data(const std::string& name, size_t offset, size_t length) const {
    const TensorInfo* t = find_tensor(name);
    if (!t) {
        LOG_ERROR("Tensor not found: %s", name.c_str());
        return {};
    }
    if (offset > t->size_bytes) return {};
    const size_t n = length ? std::min<size_t>(length, t->size_bytes - offset) : t->size_bytes - offset;
    const uint8_t* p = tensor_data(*t) + offset;
    return std::vector<uint8_t>(p, p + n);
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/gguf_parser.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file gguf_parser.h
/// GGUF v3 parser for Qwen3.8-27B (spec: ggml/docs/gguf.md).
///
/// The file is memory-mapped; metadata is parsed with a bounds-checked cursor so
/// every field consumes exactly the number of bytes the format defines and any
/// out-of-range read is an error with a file offset (never an allocation from
/// an unchecked length). Tensor offsets in GGUF are relative to the start of the
/// tensor-data section, which begins at the first `general.alignment` (default
/// 32) boundary after the tensor-info table.

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include "../common/utils.h"
#include "../../include/q38rt/q38rt_types.h"

namespace q38rt {

static constexpr uint32_t Q38_GGUF_MAGIC = 0x46554747; // "GGUF" little-endian

/// GGUF metadata value types — exact values from the GGUF spec.
enum class GGUFType : uint32_t {
    UINT8   = 0,
    INT8    = 1,
    UINT16  = 2,
    INT16   = 3,
    UINT32  = 4,
    INT32   = 5,
    FLOAT32 = 6,
    BOOL    = 7,
    STRING  = 8,
    ARRAY   = 9,
    UINT64  = 10,
    INT64   = 11,
    FLOAT64 = 12,
};

const char* gguf_type_name(GGUFType t);

struct GGUFHeader {
    uint32_t magic = 0;
    uint32_t version = 0;          // uint32 in GGUF v2/v3
    uint64_t tensor_count = 0;
    uint64_t tensor_kv_count = 0;
};

/// One metadata value. Scalars are widened into the matching 64-bit field;
/// arrays keep their raw little-endian element bytes (fixed-size element types)
/// or a vector of strings (STRING arrays).
struct GGUFValue {
    GGUFType type = GGUFType::UINT8;
    uint64_t u64 = 0;              // unsigned integer types and BOOL
    int64_t  i64 = 0;              // signed integer types
    double   f64 = 0.0;            // FLOAT32 / FLOAT64
    std::string str;               // STRING
    GGUFType arr_type = GGUFType::UINT8;
    uint64_t arr_count = 0;
    std::vector<uint8_t> arr_raw;  // fixed-size element arrays
    std::vector<std::string> arr_str; // STRING arrays
};

struct GGUFKVPair {
    std::string key;
    uint64_t file_offset = 0;      // where the key's length field starts
    GGUFValue value;
};

class GGUFParser {
public:
    GGUFParser();
    ~GGUFParser();
    GGUFParser(const GGUFParser&) = delete;
    GGUFParser& operator=(const GGUFParser&) = delete;

    /// Map and parse the file. Tensor data is not touched (the mapping is lazy). `arch`: the expected
    /// general.architecture; the model configuration (get_config) is built for "qwen35" only (the vision projector
    /// file is "clip").
    bool parse(const std::string& path, bool verbose = false, const std::string& arch = "qwen35");

    const GGUFHeader& get_header() const { return header_; }
    const ModelConfig& get_config() const { return config_; }
    const std::vector<TensorInfo>& get_tensors() const { return tensors_; }
    const std::vector<GGUFKVPair>& get_kv_list() const { return kv_list_; }

    const TensorInfo* find_tensor(const std::string& name) const;
    const GGUFValue* find_kv(const std::string& key) const;

    std::string get_kv_string(const std::string& key, const std::string& def = "") const;
    uint64_t get_kv_uint(const std::string& key, uint64_t def = 0) const;
    int64_t get_kv_int(const std::string& key, int64_t def = 0) const;
    float get_kv_float(const std::string& key, float def = 0.0f) const;
    bool get_kv_bool(const std::string& key, bool def = false) const;

    /// Pointer to the tensor's bytes inside the read-only mapping.
    const uint8_t* tensor_data(const TensorInfo& t) const { return base_ + t.offset; }
    /// Copy of the tensor's bytes (or a sub-range).
    std::vector<uint8_t> get_tensor_data(const std::string& name, size_t offset = 0, size_t length = 0) const;

    uint64_t file_size() const { return file_size_; }
    uint64_t data_offset() const { return data_offset_; }
    uint64_t metadata_end() const { return meta_end_; }
    uint32_t alignment() const { return alignment_; }
    const uint8_t* mapping() const { return base_; }

private:
    GGUFHeader header_;
    ModelConfig config_;
    std::vector<TensorInfo> tensors_;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, size_t> tensor_index_;
    std::vector<GGUFKVPair> kv_list_;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, size_t> kv_index_;

    int fd_ = -1;
    const uint8_t* base_ = nullptr;
    uint64_t file_size_ = 0;
    uint64_t pos_ = 0;              // parse cursor
    uint64_t meta_end_ = 0;         // end of tensor-info table
    uint64_t data_offset_ = 0;      // aligned start of tensor data
    uint32_t alignment_ = 32;
    bool verbose_ = false;

    bool parse_header();
    bool parse_kv();
    bool parse_tensors();
    bool finalize_tensors();
    bool build_config();

    // Bounds-checked readers: throw std::runtime_error (with offset) on EOF.
    void need(uint64_t n, const char* what) const;
    template <typename T> T read_pod(const char* what);
    uint8_t  read_uint8()  { return read_pod<uint8_t>("uint8"); }
    int8_t   read_int8()   { return read_pod<int8_t>("int8"); }
    uint16_t read_uint16() { return read_pod<uint16_t>("uint16"); }
    int16_t  read_int16()  { return read_pod<int16_t>("int16"); }
    uint32_t read_uint32() { return read_pod<uint32_t>("uint32"); }
    int32_t  read_int32()  { return read_pod<int32_t>("int32"); }
    uint64_t read_uint64() { return read_pod<uint64_t>("uint64"); }
    int64_t  read_int64()  { return read_pod<int64_t>("int64"); }
    float    read_float()  { return read_pod<float>("float32"); }
    double   read_double() { return read_pod<double>("float64"); }
    std::string read_string();
    bool read_value(GGUFType type, GGUFValue& out, int depth);
    bool read_array(GGUFValue& out, int depth);
};

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/planner.cpp =====

/// @file planner.cpp
/// Greedy residency planning (see planner.h).

#include "runtime/planner.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <map>
#include <queue>
#include <sstream>

namespace q38rt {

namespace {
bool g_cpu_r4 = true, g_cpu_r2 = true;
bool is_iq3(QuantFormat t) { return t == QuantFormat::IQ3_S || t == QuantFormat::IQ3_XXS; }
bool is_iq2(QuantFormat t) { return t == QuantFormat::IQ2_XXS || t == QuantFormat::IQ2_XS || t == QuantFormat::IQ2_S; }
bool use_re(QuantFormat t, int re) { return re < 0 ? DeviceRates::reencoded(t) : re != 0 && (is_iq3(t) || is_iq2(t)); }
} // namespace
void DeviceRates::set_cpu_reencode(bool r4, bool r2) { g_cpu_r4 = r4; g_cpu_r2 = r2; }
bool DeviceRates::reencoded(QuantFormat t) { return (g_cpu_r4 && is_iq3(t)) || (g_cpu_r2 && is_iq2(t)); }

DeviceRates DeviceRates::measured_defaults(int re) {
    // Measured on the target machine: CPU 2026-10-15 (tests/kernels/test_cpu_gemv --bench, 8 threads, 512 MB
    // working set); GPU 2026-10-16 (tests/kernels/test_gemv --bench, conservative average over K = 5120 / 6144 /
    // 17408). GB/s of weight bytes.
    DeviceRates r;
    auto set = [&](QuantFormat t, double c, double g) { r.cpu_gbs[(int)t] = c; r.gpu_gbs[(int)t] = g; };
    // (Q2_K, Q4_K, IQ2_*: scaled to the current kernels by speed ratios from interleaved runs, 2026-10-17)
    set(QuantFormat::Q2_K, 31.4, 175);
    set(QuantFormat::Q4_K, 32.1, 220);
    // (R2: scaled by the re-encoded / gather single-column ratios, GB/s of source bytes)
    set(QuantFormat::IQ2_XXS, use_re(QuantFormat::IQ2_XXS, re) ? 23.8 : 19.0, 140);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ2_XS, use_re(QuantFormat::IQ2_XS, re) ? 21.2 : 19.5, 155);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ2_S, use_re(QuantFormat::IQ2_S, re) ? 25.1 : 23.4, 165);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ3_XXS, use_re(QuantFormat::IQ3_XXS, re) ? 21.3 : 21.7, 180);   // R4: GB/s of source bytes (R4/gather 0.98)
    set(QuantFormat::IQ3_S, 25.9, 182);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ1_S, 14.9, 85);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ1_M, 8.7, 122);
    set(QuantFormat::IQ4_XS, 31.6, 320);
    set(QuantFormat::Q6_K, 33.0, 210);    // MTP block only (measured on the -mtp.gguf tensors)
    // sustained decode runs the CPU thermally throttled (~2.6 GHz at 100 C vs the cool micro-benchmarks); planning
    // with rates scaled by 0.8 measured best (plain decode A/B: 1.0 / 0.8 / 0.65 -> 7.49 / 7.66 / 7.51 tok/s)
    double scale = 0.8;
    if (const char* e = getenv("Q38_CPU_RATE_SCALE")) scale = atof(e);
    for (double& c : r.cpu_gbs) c *= scale;
    return r;
}

double DeviceRates::cpu_cols_factor(QuantFormat t, int n, int re) {
    // tests/kernels/test_cpu_gemv --bench, 8 threads: pass time for n = 2..7 columns vs 1. n = 2-4: average of 2
    // interleaved runs (2026-10-17); n = 5-7: one run (2026-10-22)
    if (n <= 1) return 1.0;
    n = std::min(n, 7);
    struct F { QuantFormat t; double f[6]; };
    static const F tab[] = {
        {QuantFormat::IQ4_XS, {1.02, 1.05, 1.18, 1.23, 1.44, 1.60}}, {QuantFormat::IQ3_S, {1.22, 1.53, 1.64, 1.66, 1.76, 2.07}},
        {QuantFormat::IQ3_XXS, {1.12, 1.25, 1.46, 1.71, 1.92, 2.04}}, {QuantFormat::IQ2_S, {1.25, 1.53, 1.86, 1.86, 2.13, 2.41}},
        {QuantFormat::IQ2_XS, {1.16, 1.50, 1.70, 2.06, 2.06, 2.26}}, {QuantFormat::IQ2_XXS, {1.18, 1.39, 1.61, 1.78, 2.04, 2.32}},
        {QuantFormat::Q2_K, {1.11, 1.43, 1.65, 1.74, 1.95, 2.34}}, {QuantFormat::Q4_K, {1.04, 1.12, 1.26, 1.26, 1.30, 1.61}},
        {QuantFormat::IQ1_S, {1.21, 1.60, 2.00, 2.23, 2.60, 2.94}}, {QuantFormat::IQ1_M, {1.08, 1.22, 1.65, 1.65, 1.65, 1.66}},
        {QuantFormat::Q6_K, {1.03, 1.03, 1.08, 1.15, 1.25, 1.35}}};
    // re-encoded rows: R4 and R2, averages of 3 runs; n = 5-7 one run
    static const F tab_re[] = {{QuantFormat::IQ3_S, {1.05, 1.08, 1.24, 1.24, 1.41, 1.60}},
                               {QuantFormat::IQ3_XXS, {1.03, 1.08, 1.20, 1.27, 1.47, 1.61}},
                               {QuantFormat::IQ2_XXS, {1.01, 1.21, 1.52, 2.12, 2.20, 2.25}},
                               {QuantFormat::IQ2_XS, {1.00, 1.34, 1.42, 1.51, 1.88, 1.97}},
                               {QuantFormat::IQ2_S, {1.06, 1.22, 1.44, 1.49, 1.79, 1.94}}};
    if (use_re(t, re)) for (const F& e : tab_re) if (e.t == t) return e.f[n - 2];
    for (const F& e : tab) if (e.t == t) return e.f[n - 2];
    return 1.0 + 0.25 * (n - 1);
}

double DeviceRates::gpu_cols_factor(int n) {
    // tests/kernels/test_gemv --bench (2026-10-16), average over formats
    static const double f[5] = {1.0, 1.0, 1.30, 1.65, 2.00};
    return f[std::max(1, std::min(n, 4))];
}

namespace {

struct GroupState {
    double cpu = 0, gpu = 0;
};

double group_time(const GroupState& g) { return std::max(g.cpu, g.gpu); }

} // namespace

void plan_residency(Plan& plan) {
    const int ng = (int)plan.groups.size();
    std::vector<GroupState> gs(ng);
    for (int g = 0; g < ng; g++) gs[g].gpu = plan.groups[g].gpu_fixed_s;
    double used = 0;
    for (PlanItem& it : plan.items) {
        it.gpu_units = std::min(it.min_gpu_units, it.units);
        gs[it.group].cpu += (it.units - it.gpu_units) * it.cpu_s_per_unit;
        gs[it.group].gpu += it.gpu_units * it.gpu_s_per_unit;
        used += it.gpu_units * it.bytes_per_unit;
    }
    // Move units in chunks; benefit per byte = reduction of the group's max(cpu, gpu).
    // Priority: largest benefit per byte. Recompute lazily (group state changes).
    struct Cand { double score; int item; };
    auto chunk_of = [](const PlanItem& it) { return std::max(1, it.units / 64); };
    auto benefit = [&](int i, int* n_out) -> double {
        const PlanItem& it = plan.items[i];
        const int left = it.units - it.gpu_units;
        if (left <= 0) return -1;
        const int n = std::min(left, chunk_of(it));
        *n_out = n;
        const GroupState& g = gs[it.group];
        GroupState g2 = g;
        g2.cpu -= n * it.cpu_s_per_unit;
        g2.gpu += n * it.gpu_s_per_unit;
        const double gain = plan.groups[it.group].weight * (group_time(g) - group_time(g2));
        return gain / (n * it.bytes_per_unit);
    };
    std::priority_queue<std::pair<double, int>> pq;
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)plan.items.size(); i++) {
        int n;
        const double b = benefit(i, &n);
        if (b > 0) pq.push({b, i});
    }
    while (!pq.empty()) {
        auto [score, i] = pq.top();
        pq.pop();
        int n;
        const double b = benefit(i, &n);
        if (b <= 0) continue;
        if (b < score * 0.999) { pq.push({b, i}); continue; }   // stale: re-queue with fresh score
        PlanItem& it = plan.items[i];
        const double bytes = n * it.bytes_per_unit;
        if (used + bytes > plan.budget_bytes) {
            // fit as many units as remain in the budget
            const int fit = (int)std::floor((plan.budget_bytes - used) / it.bytes_per_unit);
            if (fit <= 0) continue;
            n = std::min(n, fit);
        }
        it.gpu_units += n;
        used += n * it.bytes_per_unit;
        gs[it.group].cpu -= n * it.cpu_s_per_unit;
        gs[it.group].gpu += n * it.gpu_s_per_unit;
        int n2;
        const double b2 = benefit(i, &n2);
        if (b2 > 0) pq.push({b2, i});
    }
    plan.gpu_bytes = used;
    plan.cpu_bytes = 0;
    plan.est_cpu_s = plan.est_gpu_s = plan.est_total_s = 0;
    for (PlanItem& it : plan.items) {
        plan.cpu_bytes += (it.units - it.gpu_units) * it.bytes_per_unit;
        if (it.gpu_units == it.units) it.reason = "all on GPU: budget allowed and GPU time stays hidden";
        else if (it.gpu_units == 0) it.reason = "all on CPU: VRAM spent where it saves more CPU time per byte";
        else it.reason = "split: group balanced or VRAM budget reached";
    }
    for (int g = 0; g < ng; g++) {
        const double w = plan.groups[g].weight;
        plan.est_cpu_s += w * gs[g].cpu;
        plan.est_gpu_s += w * gs[g].gpu;
        plan.est_total_s += w * group_time(gs[g]);
    }
}

std::string plan_summary(const Plan& p) {
    std::ostringstream o;
    char buf[256];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "budget %.0f MiB | GPU weights %.0f MiB | CPU weights %.0f MiB\n", p.budget_bytes / 1048576.0,
             p.gpu_bytes / 1048576.0, p.cpu_bytes / 1048576.0);
    o << buf;
    snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "estimated per token: CPU %.1f ms, GPU %.1f ms, sum of group maxima %.1f ms (%.2f tok/s)\n",
             p.est_cpu_s * 1e3, p.est_gpu_s * 1e3, p.est_total_s * 1e3, 1.0 / p.est_total_s);
    o << buf;
    std::map<std::string, std::pair<double, double>> by_kind;   // gpu bytes, cpu bytes
    for (const PlanItem& it : p.items) {
        std::string kind = it.name.substr(it.name.find('.', 4) + 1);
        auto& e = by_kind[kind];
        e.first += it.gpu_units * it.bytes_per_unit;
        e.second += (it.units - it.gpu_units) * it.bytes_per_unit;
    }
    for (auto& kv : by_kind) {
        snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "  %-22s GPU %8.1f MiB  CPU %8.1f MiB\n", kv.first.c_str(), kv.second.first / 1048576.0,
                 kv.second.second / 1048576.0);
        o << buf;
    }
    return o.str();
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/planner.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file planner.h
/// Residency planner: decides, for every large weight matrix, how many rows (or FFN columns)
/// live in VRAM (computed by the GPU) versus host DRAM (computed by the CPU), given the VRAM
/// budget and measured per-format CPU/GPU GEMV throughput.
///
/// Model: decode executes a sequence of sync groups; within a group the GPU part and the CPU part
/// run concurrently, so group time ~= max(T_gpu, T_cpu). Every byte must be read once per token,
/// so the planner moves to the GPU, under the VRAM budget, the bytes that save the most CPU time,
/// never pushing a group past its CPU/GPU balance point.

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "q38rt/q38rt_types.h"

namespace q38rt {

/// Measured decode GEMV throughput (GB/s) of a weight format on each device.
struct DeviceRates {
    double cpu_gbs[32] = {};
    double gpu_gbs[32] = {};
    /// Values measured on the target machine (see docs). re: host rows of re-encodable formats run the re-encoded
    /// kernels (cpu::re_*: R4 for IQ3, R2 for IQ2) -- 1 all, 0 none, -1 per the process setting (set_cpu_reencode).
    static DeviceRates measured_defaults(int re = -1);
    /// Time of one weight pass computing n (1..4) token columns relative to n = 1 (measured, see docs).
    static double cpu_cols_factor(QuantFormat t, int n, int re = -1);
    static void set_cpu_reencode(bool r4, bool r2);
    static bool reencoded(QuantFormat t);   // per the process setting
    static double gpu_cols_factor(int n);
};

/// One splittable unit: a matrix (or the FFN triple sharing one split) inside a sync group.
struct PlanItem {
    std::string name;
    int group = -1;            // sync group index
    int units = 0;             // split granularity count (rows, or 256-column blocks for the FFN)
    int unit_rows = 1;         // rows (or columns) per unit
    double bytes_per_unit = 0; // weight bytes per unit
    double cpu_s_per_unit = 0; // CPU seconds per unit
    double gpu_s_per_unit = 0; // GPU seconds per unit
    int gpu_units = 0;         // result: units placed on the GPU (prefix)
    int min_gpu_units = 0;     // forced GPU prefix (placed before the greedy, e.g. the MTP draft-head rows)
    std::string reason;        // why it ended where it is
};

struct PlanGroup {
    double gpu_fixed_s = 0;    // GPU-only work in this group (recurrence, attention, ...)
    double weight = 1;         // executions per planned step (e.g. MTP groups run once per draft token)
};

struct Plan {
    std::vector<PlanItem> items;
    std::vector<PlanGroup> groups;
    double budget_bytes = 0;
    double gpu_bytes = 0, cpu_bytes = 0;
    double est_cpu_s = 0, est_gpu_s = 0, est_total_s = 0;   // per token (sum over groups of max)
};

/// Greedy planner: fills `gpu_units` of every item. Returns estimated per-token time.
void plan_residency(Plan& plan);

/// Human-readable summary (per type, per group kind).
std::string plan_summary(const Plan& plan);

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/tokenizer.cpp =====

/// @file tokenizer.cpp
/// Byte-level BPE with GGUF vocabulary/merges and the Frokenizer (re2c, Qwen3.5 regex) pre-tokenizer.

#include "runtime/tokenizer.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
#include <queue>
#include "common/utils.h"
#include "frokenizer_generated/lexer.hpp"

namespace q38rt {

namespace {

// GPT-2 bytes_to_unicode: printable bytes map to themselves, the other 68 to U+0100.. in order.
struct ByteMap {
    uint32_t to_cp[256];
    std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint8_t> to_byte;
    ByteMap() {
        int n = 0;
        for (int b = 0; b < 256; b++) {
            const bool keep = (b >= 33 && b <= 126) || (b >= 161 && b <= 172) || (b >= 174 && b <= 255);
            to_cp[b] = keep ? (uint32_t)b : (uint32_t)(256 + n++);
            to_byte[to_cp[b]] = (uint8_t)b;
        }
    }
};
const ByteMap& bytemap() {
    static ByteMap m;
    return m;
}

std::string utf8(uint32_t cp) {
    std::string s;
    if (cp < 0x80) s += (char)cp;
    else if (cp < 0x800) { s += (char)(0xC0 | (cp >> 6)); s += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F)); }
    else if (cp < 0x10000) { s += (char)(0xE0 | (cp >> 12)); s += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3F)); s += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F)); }
    else { s += (char)(0xF0 | (cp >> 18)); s += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 12) & 0x3F)); s += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3F)); s += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F)); }
    return s;
}

inline uint64_t pair_key(int32_t a, int32_t b) { return ((uint64_t)(uint32_t)a << 32) | (uint32_t)b; }

} // namespace

bool Tokenizer::load(const GGUFParser& g) {
    const GGUFValue* toks = g.find_kv("tokenizer.ggml.tokens");
    const GGUFValue* merges = g.find_kv("tokenizer.ggml.merges");
    const GGUFValue* types = g.find_kv("tokenizer.ggml.token_type");
    if (!toks || !merges || toks->arr_type != GGUFType::STRING || merges->arr_type != GGUFType::STRING) {
        LOG_ERROR("GGUF has no BPE vocabulary/merges");
        return false;
    }
    if (g.get_kv_string("tokenizer.ggml.model") != "gpt2" || g.get_kv_string("tokenizer.ggml.pre") != "qwen35") {
        LOG_ERROR("tokenizer is specialized for gpt2/qwen35");
        return false;
    }
    tokens_ = toks->arr_str;
    type_.assign(tokens_.size(), 1);
    if (types && types->arr_type == GGUFType::INT32 && types->arr_count == tokens_.size())
        memcpy(type_.data(), types->arr_raw.data(), tokens_.size() * 4);
    vocab_.reserve(tokens_.size() * 2);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < tokens_.size(); i++) vocab_.emplace(tokens_[i], (int32_t)i);
    const ByteMap& bm = bytemap();
    for (int b = 0; b < 256; b++) {
        auto it = vocab_.find(utf8(bm.to_cp[b]));
        if (it == vocab_.end()) { LOG_ERROR("byte %d has no token", b); return false; }
        byte_token_[b] = it->second;
    }
    merges_.reserve(merges->arr_str.size() * 2);
    for (size_t r = 0; r < merges->arr_str.size(); r++) {
        const std::string& m = merges->arr_str[r];
        const size_t sp = m.find(' ', 1);
        if (sp == std::string::npos) continue;
        const std::string a = m.substr(0, sp), b = m.substr(sp + 1);
        auto ia = vocab_.find(a), ib = vocab_.find(b), ic = vocab_.find(a + b);
        if (ia == vocab_.end() || ib == vocab_.end() || ic == vocab_.end()) continue;
        merges_.emplace(pair_key(ia->second, ib->second), std::make_pair((int32_t)r, ic->second));
    }
    specials_.clear();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < tokens_.size(); i++)
        if (type_[i] == 3 || type_[i] == 4) specials_.push_back({tokens_[i], (int32_t)i});
    std::sort(specials_.begin(), specials_.end(),
              [](const auto& x, const auto& y) { return x.first.size() > y.first.size(); });
    for (auto& v : special_by_byte_) v.clear();
    for (size_t k = 0; k < specials_.size(); k++)
        if (!specials_[k].first.empty()) special_by_byte_[(uint8_t)specials_[k].first[0]].push_back((int)k);
    eos_ = (int32_t)g.get_kv_uint("tokenizer.ggml.eos_token_id", 248046);
    bos_ = (int32_t)g.get_kv_uint("tokenizer.ggml.bos_token_id", 248044);
    return true;
}

// Standard byte-level BPE on one pre-token: lowest merge rank first, leftmost on ties.
void Tokenizer::bpe_chunk(const char* s, size_t n, std::vector<int32_t>& out) const {
    if (n == 0) return;
    if (n == 1) { out.push_back(byte_token_[(uint8_t)s[0]]); return; }
    std::vector<int32_t> sym(n);
    std::vector<int> prev(n), next(n);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        sym[i] = byte_token_[(uint8_t)s[i]];
        prev[i] = (int)i - 1;
        next[i] = (int)i + 1;
    }
    struct Item { int32_t rank; int pos; int32_t l, r; };
    auto cmp = [](const Item& a, const Item& b) { return a.rank > b.rank || (a.rank == b.rank && a.pos > b.pos); };
    std::priority_queue<Item, std::vector<Item>, decltype(cmp)> pq(cmp);
    auto push = [&](int i) {
        const int j = next[i];
        if (i < 0 || j >= (int)n) return;
        auto it = merges_.find(pair_key(sym[i], sym[j]));
        if (it != merges_.end()) pq.push({it->second.first, i, sym[i], sym[j]});
    };
    for (int i = 0; i + 1 < (int)n; i++) push(i);
    std::vector<char> dead(n, 0);
    while (!pq.empty()) {
        const Item it = pq.top();
        pq.pop();
        const int i = it.pos;
        if (dead[i] || sym[i] != it.l) continue;
        const int j = next[i];
        if (j >= (int)n || dead[j] || sym[j] != it.r) continue;
        sym[i] = merges_.at(pair_key(it.l, it.r)).second;
        dead[j] = 1;
        next[i] = next[j];
        if (next[j] < (int)n) prev[next[j]] = i;
        if (prev[i] >= 0) push(prev[i]);
        push(i);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)n; i = next[i]) out.push_back(sym[i]);
}

void Tokenizer::encode_plain(const char* s, size_t n, std::vector<int32_t>& out) const {
    frokenizer::LexerState st;
    frokenizer::lexer_init(&st, s, n);
    frokenizer::Token tok;
    while (frokenizer::lexer_next(&st, &tok) != frokenizer::TOKEN_EOF) bpe_chunk(tok.start, tok.length, out);
}

std::vector<int32_t> Tokenizer::encode(const std::string& text, bool parse_special) const {
    std::vector<int32_t> out;
    out.reserve(text.size() / 3 + 8);
    if (!parse_special) {
        encode_plain(text.data(), text.size(), out);
        return out;
    }
    size_t seg = 0, i = 0;
    while (i < text.size()) {
        int32_t id = -1;
        size_t len = 0;
        for (int k : special_by_byte_[(uint8_t)text[i]]) {   // longest match first
            const auto& sp = specials_[k];
            if (sp.first.size() <= text.size() - i && memcmp(text.data() + i, sp.first.data(), sp.first.size()) == 0) {
                id = sp.second;
                len = sp.first.size();
                break;
            }
        }
        if (id < 0) { i++; continue; }
        encode_plain(text.data() + seg, i - seg, out);
        out.push_back(id);
        i += len;
        seg = i;
    }
    encode_plain(text.data() + seg, text.size() - seg, out);
    return out;
}

std::string Tokenizer::token_to_piece(int32_t id) const {
    if (id < 0 || id >= (int32_t)tokens_.size()) return "";
    const std::string& t = tokens_[id];
    if (is_special(id)) return t;
    const ByteMap& bm = bytemap();
    std::string out;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < t.size();) {
        uint32_t cp;
        const unsigned char c = (unsigned char)t[i];
        int len = c < 0x80 ? 1 : (c >> 5) == 6 ? 2 : (c >> 4) == 14 ? 3 : 4;
        if (i + len > t.size()) break;
        cp = len == 1 ? c : len == 2 ? (c & 0x1F) : len == 3 ? (c & 0x0F) : (c & 0x07);
        for (int k = 1; k < len; k++) cp = (cp << 6) | ((unsigned char)t[i + k] & 0x3F);
        auto it = bm.to_byte.find(cp);
        if (it != bm.to_byte.end()) out += (char)it->second;
        else out.append(t, i, len);
        i += len;
    }
    return out;
}

std::string Tokenizer::decode(const std::vector<int32_t>& ids) const {
    std::string s;
    for (int32_t id : ids) s += token_to_piece(id);
    return s;
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/tokenizer.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file tokenizer.h
/// Byte-level BPE tokenizer for the target GGUF (tokenizer.ggml.model = gpt2, pre = qwen35).
///
/// Pre-tokenization uses the vendored Frokenizer lexer (include/frokenizer_generated/lexer.hpp), generated by
/// re2c from the Qwen3.5 pre-tokenizer regex. Frokenizer's own baked vocabulary belongs to an older 151,643-token
/// Qwen vocabulary (wrong ids for this model; not vendored), so vocabulary, merges and special tokens are loaded
/// from the GGUF at runtime. Validated id-for-id against llama.cpp's tokenizer (tests/unit/test_tokenizer).

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include "runtime/gguf_parser.h"

namespace q38rt {

class Tokenizer {
public:
    bool load(const GGUFParser& gguf);

    /// Encode UTF-8 text. With parse_special, control/user-defined token strings (e.g. <|im_start|>)
    /// map to their ids; otherwise they are tokenized as plain text.
    std::vector<int32_t> encode(const std::string& text, bool parse_special = true) const;

    /// Raw bytes of one token (byte-level decoded; special tokens yield their literal text).
    std::string token_to_piece(int32_t id) const;
    std::string decode(const std::vector<int32_t>& ids) const;

    int32_t n_vocab() const { return (int32_t)tokens_.size(); }
    int32_t eos() const { return eos_; }
    int32_t bos() const { return bos_; }
    bool is_special(int32_t id) const { return id >= 0 && id < (int32_t)type_.size() && (type_[id] == 3 || type_[id] == 4); }

private:
    std::vector<std::string> tokens_;          // byte-level unicode strings as stored in the GGUF
    std::vector<int32_t> type_;                // 1 normal, 3 control, 4 user defined, ...
    std::unordered_map<std::string, int32_t> vocab_;
    std::unordered_map<uint64_t, std::pair<int32_t, int32_t>> merges_;   // (left,right) -> (rank, merged id)
    int32_t byte_token_[256] = {};             // id of the single-byte symbol for each byte
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, int32_t>> specials_;   // sorted by length, longest first
    std::vector<int> special_by_byte_[256];                   // indices into specials_ by first byte
    int32_t eos_ = -1, bos_ = -1;

    void bpe_chunk(const char* s, size_t n, std::vector<int32_t>& out) const;
    void encode_plain(const char* s, size_t n, std::vector<int32_t>& out) const;
};

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/vision.cpp =====

/// @file vision.cpp
/// See vision.h. Host side: image decoding, preprocessing, mmproj loading. The GPU forward is Engine::encode_image
/// (vision_encode.cpp).

#include "runtime/vision.h"
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <immintrin.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include "common/utils.h"

#define STB_IMAGE_IMPLEMENTATION
#define STBI_NO_STDIO
#define STBI_FAILURE_USERMSG
#pragma GCC diagnostic push
#pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wunused-but-set-variable"
#include "third_party/stb_image.h"
#pragma GCC diagnostic pop

namespace q38rt {

bool decode_image(const void* data, size_t bytes, Image& out, std::string& err) {
    int w = 0, h = 0, n = 0;
    uint8_t* px = stbi_load_from_memory((const stbi_uc*)data, (int)bytes, &w, &h, &n, 3);
    if (!px) {
        err = std::string("cannot decode the image: ") + stbi_failure_reason();
        return false;
    }
    out.width = w;
    out.height = h;
    out.rgb.assign(px, px + (size_t)w * h * 3);
    stbi_image_free(px);
    return true;
}

namespace {

// BF16 / F16 / F32 tensor -> FP16 (round to nearest even). False if a value does not fit FP16.
bool to_fp16(const GGUFParser& g, const TensorInfo& t, uint16_t* dst) {
    const uint8_t* src = g.tensor_data(t);
    const size_t n = t.shape.size();
    float maxabs = 0;
    size_t i = 0;
    auto put8 = [&](__m256 v, size_t at) {
        maxabs = std::max(maxabs, [&] {
            alignas(32) float f[8];
            _mm256_store_ps(f, _mm256_andnot_ps(_mm256_set1_ps(-0.0f), v));
            return *std::max_element(f, f + 8);
        }());
        _mm_storeu_si128((__m128i*)(dst + at), _mm256_cvtps_ph(v, _MM_FROUND_TO_NEAREST_INT));
    };
    switch (t.quant) {
        case QuantFormat::BF16:
            for (; i + 8 <= n; i += 8) {
                const __m128i b = _mm_loadu_si128((const __m128i*)(src + 2 * i));
                put8(_mm256_castsi256_ps(_mm256_slli_epi32(_mm256_cvtepu16_epi32(b), 16)), i);
            }
            break;
        case QuantFormat::FP32:
            for (; i + 8 <= n; i += 8) put8(_mm256_loadu_ps((const float*)src + i), i);
            break;
        case QuantFormat::FP16:
            memcpy(dst, src, n * 2);
            return true;
        default:
            LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s: unsupported type %s", t.name.c_str(), quant_name(t.quant));
            return false;
    }
    if (i != n) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s: %zu elements, not a multiple of 8", t.name.c_str(), n); return false; }
    if (maxabs > 65504.0f) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s: value %g does not fit FP16", t.name.c_str(), maxabs); return false; }
    return true;
}

bool to_f32(const GGUFParser& g, const TensorInfo& t, float* dst) {
    if (t.quant != QuantFormat::FP32) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s: expected F32, got %s", t.name.c_str(), quant_name(t.quant)); return false; }
    memcpy(dst, g.tensor_data(t), t.shape.size() * 4);
    return true;
}

} // namespace

VisionModel::~VisionModel() {
    for (uint16_t* p : {h_layers_, h_patch_, h_mm0_, h_mm2_}) if (p) cudaFreeHost(p);
}

bool VisionModel::load(const std::string& path, int min_tokens, int max_tokens) {
    if (!gguf_.parse(path, false, "clip")) return false;
    min_tokens_ = std::max(1, min_tokens);
    max_tokens_ = std::max(min_tokens_, max_tokens);
    const GGUFParser& g = gguf_;
    auto need_u = [&](const char* key, uint64_t want) {
        if (g.get_kv_uint(key, 0) == want) return true;
        LOG_ERROR("mmproj %s = %llu, this runtime expects %llu (Qwen3.8-27B's vision projector)", key,
                  (unsigned long long)g.get_kv_uint(key, 0), (unsigned long long)want);
        return false;
    };
    if (g.get_kv_string("clip.projector_type") != "qwen3vl_merger") {
        LOG_ERROR("mmproj projector '%s', expected qwen3vl_merger", g.get_kv_string("clip.projector_type").c_str());
        return false;
    }
    if (!need_u("clip.vision.projection_dim", OUT) || !need_u("clip.vision.embedding_length", D) ||
        !need_u("clip.vision.block_count", LAYERS) || !need_u("clip.vision.attention.head_count", HEADS) ||
        !need_u("clip.vision.patch_size", PATCH) || !need_u("clip.vision.feed_forward_length", FF) ||
        !need_u("clip.vision.spatial_merge_size", MERGE))
        return false;
    if (const GGUFValue* ds = g.find_kv("clip.vision.is_deepstack_layers"))
        for (uint8_t b : ds->arr_raw) if (b) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj with deepstack layers is not supported"); return false; }
    for (const char* key : {"clip.vision.image_mean", "clip.vision.image_std"}) {
        const GGUFValue* v = g.find_kv(key);
        if (!v || v->arr_raw.size() != 12) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj: %s missing", key); return false; }
        float f[3];
        memcpy(f, v->arr_raw.data(), 12);
        if (f[0] != 0.5f || f[1] != 0.5f || f[2] != 0.5f) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj: %s is not 0.5 (preprocessing assumes it)", key); return false; }
    }
    eps_ = g.get_kv_float("clip.vision.attention.layer_norm_epsilon", 1e-6f);

    auto tensor = [&](const std::string& name, std::initializer_list<uint64_t> dims) -> const TensorInfo* {
        const TensorInfo* t = g.find_tensor(name);
        if (!t) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s missing", name.c_str()); return nullptr; }
        size_t i = 0;
        for (uint64_t d : dims) {
            if (i >= t->shape.ndim || t->shape.dims[i] != d) { LOG_ERROR("mmproj tensor %s: unexpected shape", name.c_str()); return nullptr; }
            i++;
        }
        return t;
    };
    auto alloc = [&](uint16_t*& p, size_t elems) {
        if (cudaHostAlloc((void**)&p, elems * 2, cudaHostAllocDefault) != cudaSuccess) {
            (void)cudaGetLastError();
            p = nullptr;
            LOG_ERROR("mmproj: cannot allocate %.0f MiB of pinned memory", elems * 2 / 1048576.0);
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    if (!alloc(h_layers_, LAYER_ELEMS * LAYERS) || !alloc(h_patch_, (size_t)D * 3 * PATCH * PATCH) ||
        !alloc(h_mm0_, (size_t)MERGED * MERGED) || !alloc(h_mm2_, (size_t)OUT * MERGED))
        return false;
    small_.assign(LAYER_SMALL * LAYERS, 0.0f);
    for (int il = 0; il < LAYERS; il++) {
        const std::string p = "v.blk." + std::to_string(il) + ".";
        uint16_t* w = h_layers_ + (size_t)il * LAYER_ELEMS;
        float* s = small_.data() + (size_t)il * LAYER_SMALL;
        const TensorInfo *qkv = tensor(p + "attn_qkv.weight", {D, 3 * D}), *out = tensor(p + "attn_out.weight", {D, D}),
                         *up = tensor(p + "ffn_up.weight", {D, FF}), *down = tensor(p + "ffn_down.weight", {FF, D});
        const TensorInfo *ln1w = tensor(p + "ln1.weight", {D}), *ln1b = tensor(p + "ln1.bias", {D}),
                         *qkvb = tensor(p + "attn_qkv.bias", {3 * D}), *outb = tensor(p + "attn_out.bias", {D}),
                         *ln2w = tensor(p + "ln2.weight", {D}), *ln2b = tensor(p + "ln2.bias", {D}),
                         *upb = tensor(p + "ffn_up.bias", {FF}), *downb = tensor(p + "ffn_down.bias", {D});
        if (!qkv || !out || !up || !down || !ln1w || !ln1b || !qkvb || !outb || !ln2w || !ln2b || !upb || !downb) return false;
        if (!to_fp16(g, *qkv, w) || !to_fp16(g, *out, w + QKV_ELEMS) || !to_fp16(g, *up, w + QKV_ELEMS + OUT_ELEMS) ||
            !to_fp16(g, *down, w + QKV_ELEMS + OUT_ELEMS + UP_ELEMS))
            return false;
        float* o = s;
        for (const TensorInfo* t : {ln1w, ln1b, qkvb, outb, ln2w, ln2b, upb, downb}) {
            if (!to_f32(g, *t, o)) return false;
            o += t->shape.size();
        }
    }
    // patch embedding: the two temporal kernels [16][16][3][1152] (ggml order: x fastest) see the same still image
    {
        const TensorInfo *k0 = tensor("v.patch_embd.weight", {PATCH, PATCH, 3, D}),
                         *k1 = tensor("v.patch_embd.weight.1", {PATCH, PATCH, 3, D}),
                         *b = tensor("v.patch_embd.bias", {D});
        if (!k0 || !k1 || !b) return false;
        std::vector<float> a(k0->shape.size()), c(k1->shape.size());
        if (!to_f32(g, *k0, a.data()) || !to_f32(g, *k1, c.data())) return false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); i++) a[i] += c[i];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); i += 8)
            _mm_storeu_si128((__m128i*)(h_patch_ + i), _mm256_cvtps_ph(_mm256_loadu_ps(a.data() + i), _MM_FROUND_TO_NEAREST_INT));
        patch_bias_.resize(D);
        if (!to_f32(g, *b, patch_bias_.data())) return false;
    }
    {
        const TensorInfo* t = tensor("v.position_embd.weight", {D, POS_SIDE * POS_SIDE});
        if (!t) return false;
        pos_.resize(t->shape.size());
        if (!to_f32(g, *t, pos_.data())) return false;
    }
    {
        const TensorInfo *w = tensor("v.post_ln.weight", {D}), *b = tensor("v.post_ln.bias", {D});
        if (!w || !b) return false;
        post_ln_.resize(2 * D);
        if (!to_f32(g, *w, post_ln_.data()) || !to_f32(g, *b, post_ln_.data() + D)) return false;
    }
    {
        const TensorInfo *w0 = tensor("mm.0.weight", {MERGED, MERGED}), *b0 = tensor("mm.0.bias", {MERGED}),
                         *w2 = tensor("mm.2.weight", {MERGED, OUT}), *b2 = tensor("mm.2.bias", {OUT});
        if (!w0 || !b0 || !w2 || !b2) return false;
        if (!to_fp16(g, *w0, h_mm0_) || !to_fp16(g, *w2, h_mm2_)) return false;
        mm_bias_.resize(MERGED + OUT);
        if (!to_f32(g, *b0, mm_bias_.data()) || !to_f32(g, *b2, mm_bias_.data() + MERGED)) return false;
    }
    LOG_INFO("vision encoder: %s (%.0f MiB of FP16 weights in pinned memory), images of %d to %d tokens", path.c_str(),
             (LAYER_ELEMS * LAYERS + (size_t)MERGED * (MERGED + OUT)) * 2 / 1048576.0, min_tokens_, max_tokens_);
    return true;
}

// llama.cpp img_tool::calc_size_preserved_ratio (transformers' smart_resize): sides multiples of 32 near the input's,
// area within [min_tokens, max_tokens] * 32 * 32 pixels.
void VisionModel::target_size(int width, int height, int& out_w, int& out_h) const {
    const int f = PATCH * MERGE;
    const int min_pixels = min_tokens_ * f * f, max_pixels = max_tokens_ * f * f;
    auto round_f = [f](float x) { return (int)std::round(x / (float)f) * f; };
    auto ceil_f = [f](float x) { return (int)std::ceil(x / (float)f) * f; };
    auto floor_f = [f](float x) { return (int)std::floor(x / (float)f) * f; };
    int h = std::max(f, round_f((float)height)), w = std::max(f, round_f((float)width));
    if (h * w > max_pixels) {
        const float beta = std::sqrt((float)(height * width) / max_pixels);
        h = std::max(f, floor_f(height / beta));
        w = std::max(f, floor_f(width / beta));
    } else if (h * w < min_pixels) {
        const float beta = std::sqrt((float)min_pixels / (height * width));
        h = ceil_f(height * beta);
        w = ceil_f(width * beta);
    }
    out_w = w;
    out_h = h;
}

namespace {

// Pillow's Image.resize(BICUBIC) (ImagingResample): separable, horizontal then vertical; bicubic kernel a = -0.5,
// widened by the scale factor when downsampling; 22-bit fixed-point weights rounded half away from zero.
// The Hugging Face Qwen2-VL image processor, used for the model's training, resizes this way.
void resize_bicubic_pillow(const std::vector<uint8_t>& in, int iw, int ih, std::vector<uint8_t>& out, int ow, int oh) {
    constexpr int PREC = 22;
    auto filter = [](double x) {
        constexpr double a = -0.5;
        x = std::fabs(x);
        if (x < 1.0) return ((a + 2.0) * x - (a + 3.0)) * x * x + 1;
        if (x < 2.0) return (((x - 5) * x + 8) * x - 4) * a;
        return 0.0;
    };
    struct Coeffs { int ksize; std::vector<int> bounds; std::vector<int32_t> k; };
    auto coeffs = [&](int in_size, int out_size) {
        Coeffs c;
        const double scale = (double)in_size / out_size, fscale = std::max(1.0, scale), support = 2.0 * fscale;
        c.ksize = (int)std::ceil(support) * 2 + 1;
        c.bounds.resize(2 * out_size);
        c.k.assign((size_t)out_size * c.ksize, 0);
        std::vector<double> w(c.ksize);
        for (int xx = 0; xx < out_size; xx++) {
            const double center = (xx + 0.5) * scale;
            int xmin = (int)(center - support + 0.5), xmax = (int)(center + support + 0.5);
            xmin = std::max(xmin, 0);
            xmax = std::min(xmax, in_size) - xmin;
            double ww = 0;
            for (int x = 0; x < xmax; x++) { w[x] = filter((x + xmin - center + 0.5) / fscale); ww += w[x]; }
            for (int x = 0; x < xmax; x++) {
                const double v = ww != 0.0 ? w[x] / ww : 0.0;
                c.k[(size_t)xx * c.ksize + x] = (int32_t)(v * (1 << PREC) + (v < 0 ? -0.5 : 0.5));
            }
            c.bounds[2 * xx] = xmin;
            c.bounds[2 * xx + 1] = xmax;
        }
        return c;
    };
    auto clip8 = [](int32_t v) { return (uint8_t)(v < 0 ? 0 : v > 255 ? 255 : v); };
    std::vector<uint8_t> tmp;
    const std::vector<uint8_t>* src = &in;
    int w = iw;
    if (ow != iw) {   // horizontal
        const Coeffs c = coeffs(iw, ow);
        tmp.resize((size_t)ow * ih * 3);
        for (int y = 0; y < ih; y++)
            for (int xx = 0; xx < ow; xx++) {
                const int xmin = c.bounds[2 * xx], xcnt = c.bounds[2 * xx + 1];
                const int32_t* k = c.k.data() + (size_t)xx * c.ksize;
                for (int ch = 0; ch < 3; ch++) {
                    int32_t ss = 1 << (PREC - 1);
                    for (int x = 0; x < xcnt; x++) ss += (*src)[((size_t)y * iw + xmin + x) * 3 + ch] * k[x];
                    tmp[((size_t)y * ow + xx) * 3 + ch] = clip8(ss >> PREC);
                }
            }
        src = &tmp;
        w = ow;
    }
    if (oh != ih) {   // vertical
        const Coeffs c = coeffs(ih, oh);
        out.resize((size_t)w * oh * 3);
        for (int yy = 0; yy < oh; yy++) {
            const int ymin = c.bounds[2 * yy], ycnt = c.bounds[2 * yy + 1];
            const int32_t* k = c.k.data() + (size_t)yy * c.ksize;
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++)
                for (int ch = 0; ch < 3; ch++) {
                    int32_t ss = 1 << (PREC - 1);
                    for (int y = 0; y < ycnt; y++) ss += (*src)[((size_t)(ymin + y) * w + x) * 3 + ch] * k[y];
                    out[((size_t)yy * w + x) * 3 + ch] = clip8(ss >> PREC);
                }
        }
    } else {
        out = *src;
    }
}

} // namespace

void resize_bicubic(const Image& in, int width, int height, Image& out) {
    out.width = width;
    out.height = height;
    if (width == in.width && height == in.height) { out.rgb = in.rgb; return; }
    resize_bicubic_pillow(in.rgb, in.width, in.height, out.rgb, width, height);
}

ImagePatches VisionModel::preprocess(const Image& img) const {
    ImagePatches ip;
    target_size(img.width, img.height, ip.width, ip.height);
    ip.nx = ip.width / (PATCH * MERGE);
    ip.ny = ip.height / (PATCH * MERGE);
    // stretched to the target size (as the Qwen2-VL processor; llama.cpp pads to keep the aspect ratio instead)
    std::vector<uint8_t> rs;
    const std::vector<uint8_t>* px = &img.rgb;
    if (ip.width != img.width || ip.height != img.height) {
        resize_bicubic_pillow(img.rgb, img.width, img.height, rs, ip.width, ip.height);
        px = &rs;
    }
    // normalize ((v / 255 - 0.5) / 0.5) and cut into patches in merge order
    ip.patch.resize((size_t)ip.n_patches() * 3 * PATCH * PATCH);
    for (int my = 0; my < ip.ny; my++)
        for (int mx = 0; mx < ip.nx; mx++)
            for (int dy = 0; dy < MERGE; dy++)
                for (int dx = 0; dx < MERGE; dx++) {
                    const size_t t = (((size_t)my * ip.nx + mx) * MERGE + dy) * MERGE + dx;
                    float* o = ip.patch.data() + t * 3 * PATCH * PATCH;
                    const int x0 = (mx * MERGE + dx) * PATCH, y0 = (my * MERGE + dy) * PATCH;
                    for (int c = 0; c < 3; c++)
                        for (int ky = 0; ky < PATCH; ky++)
                            for (int kx = 0; kx < PATCH; kx++) {
                                const float v = (float)(*px)[((size_t)(y0 + ky) * ip.width + x0 + kx) * 3 + c] / 255.0f;
                                o[c * PATCH * PATCH + ky * PATCH + kx] = (v - 0.5f) / 0.5f;
                            }
                }
    return ip;
}

// ggml_interpolate(BILINEAR | ALIGN_CORNERS) of the 48x48 grid to the image's patch grid, then merge order
std::vector<float> VisionModel::position_embeddings(int nx, int ny) const {
    const int W = nx * MERGE, H = ny * MERGE;
    std::vector<float> grid((size_t)W * H * D);   // raster order
    if (W == POS_SIDE && H == POS_SIDE) {
        grid = pos_;
    } else {
        const float sf0 = W > 1 ? (float)(W - 1) / (POS_SIDE - 1) : (float)W / POS_SIDE;
        const float sf1 = H > 1 ? (float)(H - 1) / (POS_SIDE - 1) : (float)H / POS_SIDE;
        for (int y = 0; y < H; y++) {
            const float fy = (float)y / sf1;
            int y0 = (int)std::floor(fy), y1 = y0 + 1;
            y0 = std::max(0, std::min(y0, POS_SIDE - 1));
            y1 = std::max(0, std::min(y1, POS_SIDE - 1));
            const float dy = std::max(0.0f, std::min(fy - (float)y0, 1.0f));
            for (int x = 0; x < W; x++) {
                const float fx = (float)x / sf0;
                int x0 = (int)std::floor(fx), x1 = x0 + 1;
                x0 = std::max(0, std::min(x0, POS_SIDE - 1));
                x1 = std::max(0, std::min(x1, POS_SIDE - 1));
                const float dx = std::max(0.0f, std::min(fx - (float)x0, 1.0f));
                const float* a = pos_.data() + ((size_t)y0 * POS_SIDE + x0) * D;
                const float* b = pos_.data() + ((size_t)y0 * POS_SIDE + x1) * D;
                const float* c = pos_.data() + ((size_t)y1 * POS_SIDE + x0) * D;
                const float* d = pos_.data() + ((size_t)y1 * POS_SIDE + x1) * D;
                float* o = grid.data() + ((size_t)y * W + x) * D;
                for (int e = 0; e < D; e++)
                    o[e] = a[e] * (1 - dx) * (1 - dy) + b[e] * dx * (1 - dy) + c[e] * (1 - dx) * dy + d[e] * dx * dy;
            }
        }
    }
    std::vector<float> out(grid.size());
    for (int my = 0; my < ny; my++)
        for (int mx = 0; mx < nx; mx++)
            for (int dy = 0; dy < MERGE; dy++)
                for (int dx = 0; dx < MERGE; dx++) {
                    const size_t t = (((size_t)my * nx + mx) * MERGE + dy) * MERGE + dx;
                    const size_t src = (size_t)(my * MERGE + dy) * W + mx * MERGE + dx;
                    memcpy(out.data() + t * D, grid.data() + src * D, D * 4);
                }
    return out;
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/vision.h =====

#pragma once
/// @file vision.h
/// Image input: the vision encoder of Qwen3.8-27B's mmproj GGUF and the preprocessing in front of it.
///
/// The encoder is a Qwen3-VL vision transformer: 16x16-pixel patches embedded to width 1152 (two temporal patch kernels,
/// summed for a still image), plus learned 48x48 position embeddings resized bilinearly to the image's patch grid;
/// 27 pre-norm blocks (LayerNorm, 16-head attention with 2D rotary positions, GELU MLP of width 4304); a final
/// LayerNorm; then each 2x2 group of patches is concatenated (4608) and projected to the language model's width 5120 by
/// a 2-layer GELU MLP. One merged token per 32x32 pixels. The network follows llama.cpp's mtmd (clip_graph_qwen3vl),
/// which the tests compare against; the preprocessing follows the Hugging Face Qwen2-VL image processor used in
/// training (smart resize to multiples of 32, stretched with Pillow's bicubic filter), where llama.cpp pads the image to
/// keep its aspect ratio and resamples bilinearly.
///
/// Weights stay in pinned host memory (converted once from BF16 to FP16) and are streamed to the GPU layer by layer
/// for each image (Engine::encode_image): the VRAM belongs to the language model's weights.

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "runtime/gguf_parser.h"

namespace q38rt {

/// An image decoded to 8-bit RGB.
struct Image {
    int width = 0, height = 0;
    std::vector<uint8_t> rgb;   // [height][width][3]
};

/// Decode PNG, JPEG, BMP, GIF (first frame), TGA, PSD, HDR or PNM bytes (stb_image). False with a message on failure.
bool decode_image(const void* data, size_t bytes, Image& out, std::string& err);
/// Resize like Pillow's Image.resize(..., BICUBIC), byte for byte (tests/unit/test_image_resize.py).
void resize_bicubic(const Image& in, int width, int height, Image& out);

/// An image prepared for the encoder: resized to multiples of 32 pixels (smart resize: sides rounded to multiples of 32,
/// area scaled into the token limits, aspect ratio approximately kept), normalized, cut into 16x16 patches in merge
/// order (the 4 patches of each 2x2 group consecutive, groups in raster order).
struct ImagePatches {
    int width = 0, height = 0;   // resized size in pixels
    int nx = 0, ny = 0;          // merged-token grid: width / 32, height / 32
    std::vector<float> patch;    // [4 * nx * ny][768]: value of channel c at (y, x) of the patch at c * 256 + y * 16 + x
    int n_tokens() const { return nx * ny; }
    int n_patches() const { return 4 * nx * ny; }
};

class VisionModel {
public:
    static constexpr int PATCH = 16, MERGE = 2, D = 1152, HEADS = 16, HEAD_DIM = 72, FF = 4304, LAYERS = 27,
                         POS_SIDE = 48, OUT = 5120;
    static constexpr int MERGED = D * MERGE * MERGE;   // 4608

    VisionModel() = default;
    ~VisionModel();
    VisionModel(const VisionModel&) = delete;
    VisionModel& operator=(const VisionModel&) = delete;

    /// Load the mmproj GGUF (architecture clip, projector qwen3vl_merger, projection 5120). Image size limits in merged
    /// tokens (32x32-pixel areas): images are scaled to hold between min_tokens and max_tokens.
    bool load(const std::string& path, int min_tokens = 8, int max_tokens = 1024);
    bool loaded() const { return h_layers_ != nullptr; }
    int max_tokens() const { return max_tokens_; }

    /// Merged-token grid (nx, ny) and resized pixel size of a width x height image (smart resize).
    void target_size(int width, int height, int& out_w, int& out_h) const;
    ImagePatches preprocess(const Image& img) const;

    // ---- weights, for the GPU forward (Engine::encode_image) ----
    /// Per layer, FP16 in pinned host memory, contiguous: qkv [3456][1152] | out [1152][1152] | up [4304][1152] |
    /// down [1152][4304] (row = output feature).
    static constexpr size_t QKV_ELEMS = (size_t)3 * D * D, OUT_ELEMS = (size_t)D * D, UP_ELEMS = (size_t)FF * D,
                            DOWN_ELEMS = (size_t)D * FF;
    static constexpr size_t LAYER_ELEMS = QKV_ELEMS + OUT_ELEMS + UP_ELEMS + DOWN_ELEMS;
    const uint16_t* layer_weights(int il) const { return h_layers_ + (size_t)il * LAYER_ELEMS; }
    /// Per layer, FP32: ln1 w, b | qkv bias (3456) | out bias | ln2 w, b | up bias (4304) | down bias.
    static constexpr size_t LAYER_SMALL = (size_t)D * 2 + 3 * D + D + D * 2 + FF + D;
    const std::vector<float>& small() const { return small_; }   // [LAYERS][LAYER_SMALL]
    const uint16_t* patch_weight() const { return h_patch_; }     // [1152][768] FP16 (both temporal kernels summed)
    const std::vector<float>& patch_bias() const { return patch_bias_; }
    const std::vector<float>& post_ln() const { return post_ln_; }   // w [1152] | b [1152]
    const uint16_t* mm0() const { return h_mm0_; }   // [4608][4608] FP16
    const uint16_t* mm2() const { return h_mm2_; }   // [5120][4608] FP16
    const std::vector<float>& mm_bias() const { return mm_bias_; }   // mm0 bias [4608] | mm2 bias [5120]
    /// Learned position embeddings resized to an nx x ny merged grid, in patch merge order: [4 * nx * ny][1152].
    std::vector<float> position_embeddings(int nx, int ny) const;
    float eps() const { return eps_; }

private:
    GGUFParser gguf_;
    int min_tokens_ = 8, max_tokens_ = 1024;
    float eps_ = 1e-6f;
    uint16_t* h_layers_ = nullptr;   // pinned
    uint16_t* h_patch_ = nullptr;
    uint16_t* h_mm0_ = nullptr;
    uint16_t* h_mm2_ = nullptr;
    std::vector<float> small_, patch_bias_, post_ln_, mm_bias_, pos_;   // pos_: [48 * 48][1152]
};

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/runtime/vision_encode.cpp =====

/// @file vision_encode.cpp
/// Engine::encode_image: the GPU forward of the vision encoder (runtime/vision.h).
///
/// Weights: each layer's four matrices (30.5 MB FP16) are copied from pinned host memory into one half of the decode
/// streaming ring (2 x 32 MB, idle between decode passes) while the GPU computes the previous layer from the other
/// half; the projector matrices go through in row pieces. Activations (FP32 residual stream, FP16 GEMM inputs) live in
/// scratch VRAM; the attention runs per head and per chunk of query rows (scores, softmax, P V as cuBLAS GEMMs), so
/// memory grows linearly with the image (about 110 MB for a 1024-token image).

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include "cuda/prefill.cuh"
#include "cuda/vision.cuh"
#include "runtime/engine.h"
#include "runtime/vision.h"

namespace q38rt {

bool Engine::encode_image(const VisionModel& vm, const ImagePatches& ip, std::vector<float>& out, std::string& err) {
    using V = VisionModel;
    const int N = ip.n_patches(), T = ip.n_tokens(), D = V::D, FF = V::FF, KP = 3 * V::PATCH * V::PATCH;
    if (N <= 0 || (size_t)ip.patch.size() != (size_t)N * KP) { err = "empty image"; return false; }
    const size_t slot_bytes = (size_t)NSLOT * SLOT_BYTES / 2;
    if (!d_slots_ || V::LAYER_ELEMS * 2 > slot_bytes) { err = "vision encoder needs the decode streaming ring"; return false; }
    if (spec_open_) { err = "encode_image during a speculative pass"; return false; }
    const int C = std::min(N, 1024);   // token chunk: QKV projection, attention queries, FFN

    // ---- scratch: buffers live in different phases share space ----
    const size_t small_n = V::LAYER_SMALL * V::LAYERS + D + 2 * D + V::MERGED + V::OUT;
    const size_t r_attn = (size_t)C * 3 * D * 4 + (size_t)C * N * 4 + (size_t)C * N * 2;   // QKV32 | S32 | P16
    const size_t r_ffn = (size_t)C * FF * 4 + (size_t)C * FF * 2;                          // U32 | U16
    const size_t r_misc = std::max({r_attn, r_ffn, (size_t)T * V::OUT * 4, (size_t)N * KP * 2});   // + OUT32, IN16
    enum { B_SMALL, B_PW, B_X, B_XN, B_QK, B_VT, B_R, B_O32, B_O16, B_WS, NB };
    std::vector<size_t> sizes(NB);
    sizes[B_SMALL] = small_n * 4;
    sizes[B_PW] = (size_t)D * KP * 2;
    sizes[B_X] = (size_t)N * D * 4;          // residual stream; merger: H32 [T][4608] lives in B_QK
    sizes[B_XN] = (size_t)N * D * 2;         // LayerNorm output; merger input M16 [T][4608]
    sizes[B_QK] = (size_t)2 * N * D * 2;     // Q16, K16 [16][N][72]
    sizes[B_VT] = (size_t)N * D * 2;         // Vt16 [16][72][N]; merger: H16
    sizes[B_R] = r_misc;
    sizes[B_O32] = (size_t)C * D * 4;
    sizes[B_O16] = (size_t)C * D * 2;
    sizes[B_WS] = 4u << 20;                  // cuBLAS workspace
    std::vector<void*> b;
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(cst_));   // decode-ring prefetches land in d_slots_
    if (!scratch_acquire(sizes, b)) {
        err = "not enough free VRAM for an image of " + std::to_string(T) + " tokens (lower --image-max-tokens)";
        return false;
    }
    float* small = (float*)b[B_SMALL];
    __half* pw = (__half*)b[B_PW];
    float* X = (float*)b[B_X];
    __half* XN = (__half*)b[B_XN];
    __half* Q = (__half*)b[B_QK];
    __half* K = Q + (size_t)N * D;
    __half* Vt = (__half*)b[B_VT];
    float* QKV = (float*)b[B_R];
    float* S = QKV + (size_t)C * 3 * D;
    __half* P = (__half*)(S + (size_t)C * N);
    float* U32 = (float*)b[B_R];
    __half* U16 = (__half*)(U32 + (size_t)C * FF);
    float* O32 = (float*)b[B_O32];
    __half* O16 = (__half*)b[B_O16];
    if (!cuda::cublas_bind(cublas_, st_, b[B_WS], sizes[B_WS])) { scratch_release(); err = "cuBLAS setup failed"; return false; }

    // small parameters: per-layer norms and biases | patch bias | post norm | projector biases
    float* sm_patch_b = small + V::LAYER_SMALL * V::LAYERS;
    float* sm_post = sm_patch_b + D;
    float* sm_mm = sm_post + 2 * D;
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(small, vm.small().data(), V::LAYER_SMALL * V::LAYERS * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(sm_patch_b, vm.patch_bias().data(), D * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(sm_post, vm.post_ln().data(), 2 * D * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(sm_mm, vm.mm_bias().data(), (V::MERGED + V::OUT) * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(pw, vm.patch_weight(), sizes[B_PW], cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));

    // ---- weight stream: jobs in consumption order through the two halves of the ring ----
    struct Job { const uint16_t* src; size_t elems; };
    std::vector<Job> jobs;
    for (int il = 0; il < V::LAYERS; il++) jobs.push_back({vm.layer_weights(il), V::LAYER_ELEMS});
    const int piece_rows = (int)(slot_bytes / ((size_t)V::MERGED * 2));
    struct Piece { int r0, n; };
    std::vector<Piece> mm0_pieces, mm2_pieces;
    for (int r0 = 0; r0 < V::MERGED; r0 += piece_rows) {
        mm0_pieces.push_back({r0, std::min(piece_rows, V::MERGED - r0)});
        jobs.push_back({vm.mm0() + (size_t)r0 * V::MERGED, (size_t)mm0_pieces.back().n * V::MERGED});
    }
    for (int r0 = 0; r0 < V::OUT; r0 += piece_rows) {
        mm2_pieces.push_back({r0, std::min(piece_rows, V::OUT - r0)});
        jobs.push_back({vm.mm2() + (size_t)r0 * V::MERGED, (size_t)mm2_pieces.back().n * V::MERGED});
    }
    cudaEvent_t ready[2], freed[2];
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ready[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&freed[i], cudaEventDisableTiming));
    }
    bool used[2] = {false, false};
    auto issue = [&](size_t j) {
        if (j >= jobs.size()) return;
        const int s = (int)(j & 1);
        if (used[s]) CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamWaitEvent(cst_, freed[s], 0));
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_slots_ + s * slot_bytes, jobs[j].src, jobs[j].elems * 2, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, cst_));
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(ready[s], cst_));
    };
    size_t job = 0;
    issue(0);
    issue(1);
    auto take = [&]() -> const __half* {   // weights of the next job (stream-ordered after its copy)
        const int s = (int)(job & 1);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamWaitEvent(st_, ready[s], 0));
        return (const __half*)(d_slots_ + s * slot_bytes);
    };
    auto done = [&]() {   // the GPU is done with the current job's slot: refill it with the job two ahead
        const int s = (int)(job & 1);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaEventRecord(freed[s], st_));
        used[s] = true;
        issue(job + 2);
        job++;
    };

    // ---- patch embedding + bias + resized position embeddings ----
    {
        std::vector<__half> in16((size_t)N * KP);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < in16.size(); i++) in16[i] = __float2half_rn(ip.patch[i]);
        const std::vector<float> pos = vm.position_embeddings(ip.nx, ip.ny);
        __half* IN16 = (__half*)b[B_R];
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(IN16, in16.data(), in16.size() * 2, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(X, pos.data(), pos.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st_));
        cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, pw, D, KP, IN16, KP, N, X, D, true, st_);   // X = pos + patches W^T
        cuda::vis_bias(X, sm_patch_b, N, D, st_);
        CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));   // host buffers in16 / pos go out of scope
    }

    // ---- 27 blocks ----
    const float kq_scale = 1.0f / std::sqrt((float)V::HEAD_DIM), eps = vm.eps();
    for (int il = 0; il < V::LAYERS; il++) {
        const __half* W = take();
        const __half *Wqkv = W, *Wout = W + V::QKV_ELEMS, *Wup = Wout + V::OUT_ELEMS, *Wdown = Wup + V::UP_ELEMS;
        const float* sp = small + (size_t)il * V::LAYER_SMALL;
        const float *ln1w = sp, *ln1b = sp + D, *qkvb = sp + 2 * D, *outb = qkvb + 3 * D, *ln2w = outb + D,
                    *ln2b = ln2w + D, *upb = ln2b + D, *downb = upb + FF;
        // attention: Q, K (rotated), V for all patches, then per chunk of query rows and head
        cuda::vis_layernorm(X, ln1w, ln1b, N, D, eps, XN, st_);
        for (int t0 = 0; t0 < N; t0 += C) {
            const int c = std::min(C, N - t0);
            cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Wqkv, 3 * D, D, XN + (size_t)t0 * D, D, c, QKV, 3 * D, false, st_);
            cuda::vis_qkv_rope(QKV, qkvb, t0, c, N, ip.nx, Q, K, Vt, st_);
        }
        for (int t0 = 0; t0 < N; t0 += C) {
            const int c = std::min(C, N - t0);
            for (int h = 0; h < V::HEADS; h++) {
                const __half *Qh = Q + (size_t)h * N * V::HEAD_DIM, *Kh = K + (size_t)h * N * V::HEAD_DIM,
                             *Vh = Vt + (size_t)h * V::HEAD_DIM * N;
                cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Kh, N, V::HEAD_DIM, Qh + (size_t)t0 * V::HEAD_DIM, V::HEAD_DIM, c, S, N, false, st_);
                cuda::vis_softmax(S, c, N, kq_scale, P, st_);
                cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Vh, V::HEAD_DIM, N, P, N, c, O32 + h * V::HEAD_DIM, D, false, st_);
            }
            cuda::vis_to_f16(O32, (size_t)c * D, O16, st_);
            cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Wout, D, D, O16, D, c, O32, D, false, st_);
            cuda::vis_residual(X + (size_t)t0 * D, O32, outb, c, D, st_);
        }
        // MLP
        cuda::vis_layernorm(X, ln2w, ln2b, N, D, eps, XN, st_);
        for (int t0 = 0; t0 < N; t0 += C) {
            const int c = std::min(C, N - t0);
            cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Wup, FF, D, XN + (size_t)t0 * D, D, c, U32, FF, false, st_);
            cuda::vis_bias_gelu(U32, upb, c, FF, U16, st_);
            cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, Wdown, D, FF, U16, FF, c, O32, D, false, st_);
            cuda::vis_residual(X + (size_t)t0 * D, O32, downb, c, D, st_);
        }
        done();
    }

    // ---- post norm, 2x2 merge (4 consecutive patches = one row of 4608), projector ----
    cuda::vis_layernorm(X, sm_post, sm_post + D, N, D, eps, XN, st_);
    float* H32 = (float*)b[B_QK];
    __half* H16 = (__half*)b[B_VT];
    float* OUT = (float*)b[B_R];
    for (const Piece& pc : mm0_pieces) {
        const __half* W = take();
        cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, W, pc.n, V::MERGED, XN, V::MERGED, T, H32 + pc.r0, V::MERGED, false, st_);
        done();
    }
    cuda::vis_bias_gelu(H32, sm_mm, T, V::MERGED, H16, st_);
    for (const Piece& pc : mm2_pieces) {
        const __half* W = take();
        cuda::gemm_f16(cublas_, W, pc.n, V::MERGED, H16, V::MERGED, T, OUT + pc.r0, V::OUT, false, st_);
        done();
    }
    cuda::vis_bias(OUT, sm_mm + V::MERGED, T, V::OUT, st_);
    out.resize((size_t)T * V::OUT);
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaMemcpyAsync(out.data(), OUT, out.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, st_));
    CHECK_CUDA(cudaStreamSynchronize(st_));
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) { cudaEventDestroy(ready[i]); cudaEventDestroy(freed[i]); }
    sseq_ = sissued_ = 0;   // the decode ring held encoder weights: the next pass re-issues its first pieces
    scratch_release();
    return true;
}

} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/cuda/common.cuh =====

#pragma once
/// @file common.cuh
/// Device helpers shared by the decode (ops.cu) and prefill (prefill.cu) kernels.

#include <cfloat>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include "cuda/gemv.cuh"
#include "cuda/ops.cuh"

namespace q38rt {
namespace cuda {
namespace dev {

__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_sum(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o);
    return v;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_max(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v = fmaxf(v, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o));
    return v;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float sigmoidf_(float x) { return 1.0f / (1.0f + __expf(-x)); }
__device__ __forceinline__ float siluf_(float x) { return x / (1.0f + __expf(-x)); }

// Block-wide sum (blockDim.x multiple of 32, <= 1024). `red` = 32 floats of shared memory.
__device__ __forceinline__ float block_sum(float v, float* red) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    v = warp_sum(v);
    __syncthreads();
    if (lane == 0) red[warp] = v;
    __syncthreads();
    const int nw = blockDim.x >> 5;
    float t = lane < nw ? red[lane] : 0.0f;
    t = warp_sum(t);
    return t;
}

// Quantize 32 values held one per lane into ActQ8 block `blk` (warp-collective).
__device__ __forceinline__ void quant_block_q8(float v, int blk, const ActQ8& q) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const float amax = warp_max(fabsf(v));
    const float d = amax / 127.0f;
    const int qi = d > 0.0f ? __float2int_rn(v / d) : 0;
    q.q[blk * 32 + lane] = (int8_t)qi;
    const float s = warp_sum((float)qi);
    if (lane == 0) { q.d[blk] = d; q.s[blk] = d * s; }
}

// Store one 256-element row (256 threads, element t per thread) in the split KV layout.
// NB: no stores through packed-struct members in device code (nvcc 12.4 miscompiled them).
__device__ __forceinline__ void kv_store_row(uint8_t* vals, uint16_t* scales, KVType kvt, float v, int t) {
    const int lane = t & 31, blk = t >> 5;
    if (kvt == KVType::F16) {
        ((__half*)vals)[t] = __float2half_rn(v);
    } else if (kvt == KVType::Q8_0) {
        const float amax = warp_max(fabsf(v));
        const float d = amax / 127.0f;
        const float id = d ? 1.0f / d : 0.0f;
        ((int8_t*)vals)[t] = (int8_t)roundf(v * id);
        if (lane == 0) scales[blk] = __half_as_ushort(__float2half_rn(d));
    } else {   // Q4_0, ggml semantics: d = max / -8 with max the signed value of the first largest |x|
        float amax = fabsf(v);
        float mx = v;
        int bi = lane;
#pragma unroll
        for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) {
            const float oa = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, amax, o), om = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, mx, o);
            const int ob = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, bi, o);
            if (oa > amax || (oa == amax && ob < bi)) { amax = oa; mx = om; bi = ob; }
        }
        const float d = mx / -8.0f;
        const float id = d ? 1.0f / d : 0.0f;
        const int qv = min(15, (int)(int8_t)(v * id + 8.5f));
        const int nxt = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, qv, 1);
        if ((t & 1) == 0) vals[t >> 1] = (uint8_t)(qv | (nxt << 4));
        if (lane == 0) scales[blk] = __half_as_ushort(__float2half_rn(d));
    }
}

// Load 8 consecutive key/value elements [8j, 8j+8) of a row as floats (before scale for Q formats).
__device__ __forceinline__ void kv_vals8(const uint8_t* row, KVType kvt, int j, float* out) {
    if (kvt == KVType::F16) {
        const uint4 u = ((const uint4*)row)[j];
        const __half2* hp = (const __half2*)&u;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const float2 f = __half22float2(hp[i]); out[2 * i] = f.x; out[2 * i + 1] = f.y; }
    } else if (kvt == KVType::Q8_0) {
        const uint2 u = ((const uint2*)row)[j];
        const int8_t* q = (const int8_t*)&u;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) out[i] = (float)q[i];
    } else {
        const uint32_t u = ((const uint32_t*)row)[j];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) out[i] = (float)((int)((u >> (4 * i)) & 0xF) - 8);
    }
}


} // namespace dev
} // namespace cuda
} // namespace q38rt

// ===== src/cuda/gemv.cu =====

/// @file gemv.cu
/// Decode-path quantized GEMV for 1-4 token columns: y_c = W x_c with W in a ggml format and each x_c quantized
/// to int8 per 32 elements (dp4a dot products, per-block scales applied in float). The activations are staged once
/// per CTA in shared memory; each warp owns a row at a time and its lanes split K into 32-weight sub-blocks; each
/// weight sub-block is decoded once and dotted with all columns (per-column results bit-identical to 1 column).

#include "cuda/gemv.cuh"
#include "cuda/quant_dot.cuh"
#include "common/utils.h"

namespace q38rt {
namespace cuda {

namespace {

constexpr int GEMV_WARPS = 8;

__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_sum(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o);
    return v;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_max(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v = fmaxf(v, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o));
    return v;
}

// One warp per 32-element block: d = amax/127, q = round(x/d), s = d*sum(q).
__global__ void k_quantize_act_q8(const float* __restrict__ x, int n_blocks, int8_t* __restrict__ q,
                                  float* __restrict__ d, float* __restrict__ s) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const int blk = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
    if (blk >= n_blocks) return;
    const float v = x[(size_t)blk * 32 + lane];
    const float amax = warp_max(fabsf(v));
    const float dd = amax / 127.0f;
    const int qi = dd > 0.0f ? __float2int_rn(v / dd) : 0;
    q[(size_t)blk * 32 + lane] = (int8_t)qi;
    float sum = warp_sum((float)qi);
    if (lane == 0) {
        d[blk] = dd;
        s[blk] = dd * sum;
    }
}

// y[c*y_ld + row] (+)= W[row,:] . x_c for NB activation columns (x_c = act + c*act_ld). With STAGE the
// activations are copied to shared memory once per CTA, else read through L1 (identical arithmetic).
template <QuantFormat T, int NB, bool STAGE>
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GEMV_WARPS * 32)
k_gemv_q8(const uint8_t* __restrict__ W, size_t row_bytes, int K, int N, const int8_t* __restrict__ aq,
          const float* __restrict__ ad, const float* __restrict__ as, int act_ld, float* __restrict__ y, int y_ld,
          int accumulate) {
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) uint8_t smem[];
    const int nsb = K / 32;                       // 32-weight sub-blocks per row
    constexpr int TB = QTable<T>::BYTES;          // lookup table (16-byte multiple)
    uint8_t* stab = smem;                         // [TB]
    int8_t* sq = (int8_t*)(smem + TB);            // [NB][K]
    float* sd = (float*)(smem + TB + NB * K);     // [NB][nsb]
    float* ss = sd + NB * nsb;                    // [NB][nsb]
    if constexpr (TB > 0) {
        const int4* src = (const int4*)QTable<T>::src();
        for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < TB / 16; i += blockDim.x) ((int4*)stab)[i] = src[i];
    }
    if constexpr (STAGE) {
        for (int c = 0; c < NB; c++) {
            const int4* src = (const int4*)(aq + (size_t)c * act_ld);
            int4* dst = (int4*)(sq + c * K);
            for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < K / 16; i += blockDim.x) dst[i] = src[i];
            for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < nsb; i += blockDim.x) {
                sd[c * nsb + i] = ad[(size_t)c * (act_ld / 32) + i];
                ss[c * nsb + i] = as[(size_t)c * (act_ld / 32) + i];
            }
        }
    }
    __syncthreads();
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    for (int row = blockIdx.x * GEMV_WARPS + warp; row < N; row += gridDim.x * GEMV_WARPS) {
        const uint8_t* wr = W + (size_t)row * row_bytes;
        float acc[NB];
#pragma unroll
        for (int c = 0; c < NB; c++) acc[c] = 0.0f;
#pragma unroll 4
        for (int sb = lane; sb < nsb; sb += 32) {
            int q8[NB][8];   // explicit 16-byte loads (c*K / c*act_ld offsets are 16-byte multiples)
            float d8[NB], s8[NB];
#pragma unroll
            for (int c = 0; c < NB; c++) {
                int4 lo, hi;
                if constexpr (STAGE) {
                    const int4* src = (const int4*)(sq + c * K + sb * 32);
                    lo = src[0];
                    hi = src[1];
                    d8[c] = sd[c * nsb + sb];
                    s8[c] = ss[c * nsb + sb];
                } else {
                    const int4* src = (const int4*)(aq + (size_t)c * act_ld + sb * 32);
                    lo = __ldg(src);
                    hi = __ldg(src + 1);
                    d8[c] = __ldg(ad + (size_t)c * (act_ld / 32) + sb);
                    s8[c] = __ldg(as + (size_t)c * (act_ld / 32) + sb);
                }
                q8[c][0] = lo.x; q8[c][1] = lo.y; q8[c][2] = lo.z; q8[c][3] = lo.w;
                q8[c][4] = hi.x; q8[c][5] = hi.y; q8[c][6] = hi.z; q8[c][7] = hi.w;
            }
